Chapter 1051: 1050: Past Buddha, Present Buddha, and Future Buddha

"Has your immortal body been fully shaped?" Lu Yang asked, aware that the immortal body the Time Immortal had been condensing for three hundred thousand years felt much stronger than the one he temporarily condensed.

"It was completed over a decade ago," replied the Time Immortal with a smile, indicating his current peak state.

"Do you know Tang Chuanwu?"

"Tang Chuanwu? Oh, he’s the kid to whom I granted the ability of Time Retrospection after foreseeing the possibility of him encountering a deadly calamity in the future."

"To test the reaction of the hidden enemy behind the scenes?" Lu Yang asked again.

The Time Immortal looked at Lu Yang with surprise and revealed a smile, "Exactly."

"Although I wasn’t able to clearly see who ambushed me back then, the hidden enemy also isn’t sure whether I saw them or not. So, I gave Tang Chuanwu the ability of Time Retrospection to inform the hidden enemy that I am still alive. They will surely want to find me and might reveal their flaws in their haste."

Lu Yang thought to himself that it indeed was as he suspected. When he had previously speculated about the user of the Time Dao Fruit, he wondered if the user was using Tang Chuanwu to send a message to the hidden enemy.

What message to shout? It was the Time Immortal informing the hidden enemy, "I am still alive."

Later on, Lu Yang also learned the origin of the Time Seeking Immortal Order, which used the oppression of a Dao Fruit over an immature Dao Fruit to locate the Time Immortal.

The suppression of a Dao Fruit over its immature form is not something you can simply escape by hiding in a Small World; otherwise, the Qilin Immortal would have long ago hidden in a Small World, allowing the outsiders Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi to become immortals.

The fact that the Great Qian Dynasty was able to create the Time Seeking Immortal Order indicates that the user of the Time Dao Fruit existed during the Great Qian Period.

Both of these facts imply the possibility that the Time Immortal is still alive.

Thus, when Lu Yang saw the Time Immortal in the Mahavira Hall, he wasn’t too surprised.

But this gives rise to another question—just who is the reincarnation of Li Haoran?

If Li Haoran is not the reincarnation of the Time Immortal, then could he be the reincarnation of the immortal who was possessed by the hidden enemy?!

As long as Li Haoran continues to improve his cultivation level, he will be able to revive his memories and thus learn who he was in his first life.

If that’s the case, then the fact that Junior Brother Li is a reincarnation must not become known to others.

If the hidden enemy finds out about Junior Brother Li’s existence, they will certainly stop at nothing to kill him!

"Young friend Lu Yang, since you’ve brought up Tang Chuanwu, does that mean you’ve met him?"

"I have. The hidden enemy has made a move, but they didn’t act directly, leaving few clues," Lu Yang replied.

"I see," sighed the Time Immortal with a hint of regret. The hidden enemy indeed is very cautious.

Suddenly, a knocking sound came from outside the door of the Mahavira Hall.

"Junior Brother, are you in there?"

"Who is it?!" The Time Immortal was startled.

"Hey, just a little girl," the Immortal Fairy said casually, waving her hand, "Little Yang, go open the door."

While Lu Yang went to open the door, Immortal Fairy quietly sent a message to the Time Immortal.

"Hey Time, did you say before that the future is not set in stone?"

The Time Immortal paused for a moment, then nodded, "I did say that, what about it?"

"The Nian Beast carried us here to Buddha Country, and on the way, we entered the Time Torrent. We saw things from the past, as well as a glimpse of the future."

"So what did you see?" The Time Immortal’s expression turned curious; he had never seen the Immortal Fairy acting so hesitantly before.

"What I saw, it was me reluctantly saying goodbye to Little Yang, saying ’I really can’t bear to leave you.’"

The Time Immortal was immediately taken aback.

"What?"

"That’s why I’m asking you, can the future I saw be changed?"

Time Immortal’s expression became serious, as he realized this was an extremely important matter, "What time in the future did you see?"

...

Lu Yang pushed open the door of the Mahavira Hall, where outside stood a swordswoman in a long white robe, wearing a bamboo hat covered with white cloth, concealing her face.

Upon seeing Lu Yang, the swordswoman took off her hat, revealing her delicate face, it was the Eldest Senior Sister.

"Eldest Senior Sister, why did you come?" Lu Yang was pleasantly surprised as he clearly hadn’t called for her.

"Your Second Senior Brother contacted me with a relay paper, saying something happened at the Western Paradise Temple."

Lu Yang felt he had everything under control, and indeed it was so, but clearly Ye Zijin didn’t think the same.

"Relay paper?" Lu Yang remembered that the Third Senior Sister used such paper to contact the Eldest Senior Sister while she was in the Demon Territory, it seems the Second Senior Brother also had one.

"Then why don’t I have it?"

"I was worried about the safety of the Second Disciple and the Third Junior Sister, so I gave them relay papers. Of course, you don’t need such a thing."

Yun Zhi studied her junior brother, whom she hadn’t seen for months, and rarely showed a hint of a smile.

"After the Nian Beast took you away, Mengmeng was quite worried about you. It seems you did well in Buddha Country, even condensing an Immortal Body," she said.

Touching the back of his head, Lu Yang smiled sheepishly, "I just took a shortcut. Please come in, Senior Sister. Fairy and Time Immortal are inside."

"Oh, you found Time Immortal too?" Yun Zhi knew that her junior brother could take care of himself. He could handle situations on his own while traveling and even encounter such fortunate adventures afterward.

"Come here, Miss Yun, let me introduce you. This glowing Buddha statue is Time Immortal," Fairy Eternity said, patting Time Immortal’s shoulder.

"Junior Yun Zhi pays respect to Time Immortal," she greeted.

"Immortal, who exactly is this person?" asked Time Immortal upon meeting Yun Zhi for the first time, feeling there was something extraordinary about her but couldn’t pinpoint what it was.

He seemed to have heard the name Yun Zhi from Ming Yu, describing her as the true Sect Leader of the Seek Dao Sect.

Fairy Eternity stood proudly between Lu Yang and Yun Zhi, pointing at herself: "I am the strongest from Ancient Times."

Then she pointed to Yun Zhi on her right: "Miss Yun is the strongest of the present day."

"Little Yang is the strongest of the future."

Embracing both to her left and right, Fairy Eternity chuckled happily, "We three are the strongest cultivators of the past, present, and future!"

"Isn’t there something in Buddhism about a Past Buddha, Present Buddha, and Future Buddha? Time, when you look back, you should include the three of us," she suggested.

"The strongest of the present?" Time Immortal was startled. It wasn’t easy to earn such high praise from someone as proud as Eternity.

But given that Lu Yang was dubbed "the strongest of the future," he couldn’t help but feel that the "strongest of the present" title was somewhat inflated.

"What, you don’t believe it? If you don’t believe it, why don’t you spar with her?" Fairy Eternity egged on.

Time Immortal truly didn’t believe it, as if not for an accident along the way, he would have been the first to become an immortal. And afterwards, he came into possession of the mysterious Time Dao Fruit; besides Fairy Eternity, he really feared no one.

"Then, let’s try!" Having not had the chance to flex his muscles since condensing the Incense Immortal Body, this was the perfect opportunity!

Yun Zhi and Time Immortal faced each other, the atmosphere tense. Time Immortal used the Time Dao Fruit to examine all future possibilities!

"Please enlighten me, senior," Yun Zhi said calmly.

"Wait a moment." Just before the battle started, Time Immortal suddenly spoke, "Immortal, what did you say just now?"

"Spar with her?"

"The sentence before that."

"What, you don’t believe it?"

"I believe it."

Chapter 1052: 1051: Elder Brother Dai, I Have Become an Immortal

In ancient times, the Time Immortal seldom faced defeat, primarily because he could foresee the future, knowing whether his chances of winning or losing a battle were higher.

This ability was praised by Fairy Eternity as the "Great Invincible Pill"!

After viewing all possible futures, the Time Immortal burst into a cold sweat and cursed Ming Yu for being unreliable under his breath.

"The true Sect Leader of Seek Dao Sect? With such an astonishing cultivation level, saying she is the real ruler of Great Xia would be more like it!"

Thinking this, a smile piled upon the Time Immortal’s face, which already bore a Buddha-like serenity, becoming even more benevolent.

"I have known Fairy Eternity for a very long time, how could I not believe her words?"

"She said you are the strongest in the world, you definitely are the strongest in the world!"

The Time Immortal altered the future merely through his words without anyone noticing, a testament to his formidable power!

"So you’re saying I am truly the strongest in the future?" Lu Yang pointed at himself, rather excited, though the future was elusive, if it were evaluated by the Time Immortal who controlled time, then that future seemed quite credible.

"Of course, Lu Yang, my young friend, you are indeed the strongest in the future," the Time Immortal affirmed, now ready to dispute with anyone who denied Lu Yang’s supremacy.

"You possess the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, and possess an Immortal’s Body; thus, you are no longer merely at the Divinity Transformation Stage, but have created a completely new realm—Immortal Transformation Stage!"

"Creating something out of nothing, crafting realms, these are acts only the ancient sages could achieve!"

Lu Yang gasped, was he really that strong?

Eldest Senior Sister was very curious about the experiences of the Time Immortal over the past three hundred thousand years, so the Time Immortal had no choice but to recount once again how he had jumped through time over the years.

"The techniques of Elder Time Immortal are truly wonderful."

"Then would Elder Time Immortal like to visit our Seek Dao Sect?" Lu Yang invited.

"No, Western Paradise Temple has just undergone a great battle, unforeseeable incidents might occur, I will stay in the Mahavira Hall as a precaution," replied the Time Immortal.

"Please also keep my existence a secret for now, to prevent any accidents."

"What about Yingtian Immortal then..."

"There’s no need to meet him or to let him know of my presence. Yingtian’s nine-layered Kirin, I trust none of them now."

Upon saying this, the Time Immortal paused, "However, you can give this Sky Opening Axe back to Yingtian Immortal; I’ve borrowed it for three hundred thousand years, it’s time to return it."

Lu Yang awkwardly said, "Ah, Yingtian Immortal forged himself a new weapon, he seems not to need the Sky Opening Axe anymore."

"Is that so, then I shall continue to keep it safe for him," the Time Immortal took the Sky Opening Axe handed over by Lu Yang.

In case of another unforeseen event at Western Paradise Temple, this Sky Opening Axe could still mask his presence.

"There’s one more thing, how is Tang Chuanwu, that boy?"

"We were worried about him facing another accident, so he now lives in our Seek Dao Sect."

"I remember he has no Spirit Root," the Time Immortal noted as he picked up several different Spirit Fruits from the altar.

These were offerings in Mahavira Hall, each Spirit Fruit’s value was immeasurable.

"Give him these fruits in the order I prepared for you, they should help him develop a Postnatal Golden Spirit Root."

"I thank Elder on behalf of Chuanwu," Lu Yang said.

"It’s a trivial matter."

Lu Yang and his companions bid farewell to the Time Immortal and left the Mahavira Hall, while the Time Immortal waved them goodbye.

After sending off the three, the Time Immortal looked at the severely damaged Western Paradise Temple and muttered to himself quietly.

"Lu Yang sure did a thorough job blowing it up; I wonder when Western Paradise Temple will be repaired."

...

"Won’t the Heavenly Venerate return again?" During the quiet of deep night, Xin Ding tossed and turned on his bed, his mind full of the day’s battle.

During the chaos of the battle, no one noticed that the Green Peak Sword had flown out from behind him.

After that battle, Heavenly Venerate disappeared and never showed up again.

Suddenly, his tent lit up with specks of golden light, which converged to form a golden silhouette.

"Little monk, still not asleep at this hour?"

"Heavenly Venerate!"

Tofu Heavenly Venerable stood with his hands behind his back, gazing at the sky, his eyes filled with the vicissitudes of life, "Ultimately, Western Paradise Temple turned into ruins because of my emergence, but through this battle, my causality is dissolved, and I no longer have a reason to stay."

"Heavenly Venerate, may I go with you?" Xin Ding looked at Tofu Heavenly Venerate reluctantly. Although they had been together for only a few months, Xin Ding had already come to regard Tofu Heavenly Venerable as his master.

Tofu Heavenly Venerable shook his head: "Little monk, you are naturally attuned to Buddhism. If you were to follow my Taoist sect, it would only hold you back. Staying at Western Paradise Temple is the best choice for you."

"If you diligently cultivate, there may yet be a time when you see me again."

With that, Tofu Heavenly Venerable turned again into specks of golden light and disappeared.

Lu Yang arrived at the gates of Western Heaven City and met up with Eldest Senior Sister and Second Senior Brother.

"All set?"

"All set, let’s head back."

Ye Zijin curiously circled around Lu Yang, "Junior Brother, have you really become an immortal?"

He still hadn’t figured out how his junior brother had changed like this.

"Only in the Buddha Country, and even then, it’s not truly becoming an immortal," Lu Yang said modestly.

When Lu Yang left Buddha Country, he left both the Immortal’s Body and the Eternity Dao Fruit Outline behind. If he returned to Buddha Country, he could become an immortal again at any time.

Although somewhat reluctant, he could not stay in Buddha Country forever.

"Back to the sect!"

Lu Yang spoke with his chest puffed out and his head held high, displaying great vigor. This trip to Buddha Country had been fertile; he had found the Sky Opening Axe and Time Immortal, gained the Immortal’s Body, and captured Si Chen God Lord. It was not an exaggeration to call him a great hero who had saved Buddha Country.

Then Lu Yang stealthily crossed the border and returned to Great Xia.

He had been taken out of Great Xia by the Nian Beast, with no exit records.

Lu Yang returned to the familiar Seek Dao Sect and relaxed.

Though Buddha Country was delightful, Seek Dao Sect felt more comfortable.

"Eldest Senior Sister, see you in a bit!" Lu Yang briefly turned around for a farewell and headed straight for the Task Hall.

With such significant events happening in Buddha Country, including capturing Si Chen God Lord, how many contribution points could he gain?

In the room, Dai Bufan held some intelligence and rubbed his chin thoughtfully, hearing the familiar knock at the door.

"Elder Brother Dai, are you in?"

"It’s Junior Brother Lu, come in."

Lu Yang entered, beaming with a spring-like demeanor, in stark contrast to the thoughtful Dai Bufan.

"Junior Brother Lu, your timing is perfect," Dai Bufan waved Lu Yang over.

"I just received intelligence that Si Chen God Lord attacked Western Paradise Temple, but was defeated by a suddenly appearing Mysterious Immortal. Eldest Senior Sister mentioned that you have quite a connection with the immortal. Do you know who this Mysterious Immortal is?"

"I do."

"You really know?" Dai Bufan was surprised; he had asked without much hope, but Junior Brother actually had an answer?

"Who is it?"

Proudly pointing at himself, Lu Yang said, "It’s me."

"Huh?" Dai Bufan was full of question marks, failing to understand the meaning of Lu Yang’s statement, or rather, with his comprehension, he could not grasp what Lu Yang meant.

Seeing Elder Brother Dai’s disbelief, Lu Yang produced a Recording Orb, showing the first-person perspective of his battle with Si Chen God Lord.

With a broad smile, Lu Yang shared the joyful news with his respected Elder Brother Dai:

"Elder Brother Dai, I’ve become an immortal."

Chapter 1053: 1052: Advanced Studies at Captive Peak

Dai Bufan had a lengthy dream, where he envisioned his birth accompanied by unusual celestial phenomena, marking him as a prodigy. After joining the Seek Dao Sect, which was filled with talents, he, except for Yun Zhi, was still able to dominate his peers and sweep through all challenges.

He was measured in his actions and deeply cherished by his junior brothers and sisters. After entering the Body Integration Realm, he took charge of various affairs in the Task Hall, earning the heartfelt admiration from all sides...

"Elder Brother Dai, Elder Brother Dai, wake up!"

Lu Yang shook Elder Brother Dai, who was lying on the ground, and pinched him forcefully on the philtrum. Elder Brother Dai immediately sat up stiffly, his eyes clouded and memory jumbled.

Had he just experienced a life flashing before his eyes?

"Elder Brother Dai, you finally woke up; you scared me to death just now," Lu Yang wiped his sweat, affirming the old saying that both great joy and great sorrow are harmful to the body; the ancients truly did not deceive me.

"What happened to me just now?"

Lu Yang looked at Elder Brother Dai, his eyes bright and sincere, "Just now I said I had become an Immortal, and you, Elder Brother Dai, stood up happily, but you staggered and fainted."

"You became an Immortal?" Elder Brother Dai thought for a moment, then immediately pulled out a crimson Auspicious Cloud Banner from his storage ring, making a gesture to smash it towards Lu Yang.

"You’re only at the Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit now, you dare to fool even me?"

Lu Yang hurriedly covered his head, "Ay, ay, ay, don’t hit, don’t hit, I’m telling the truth."

"I even have a witness."

"A witness?"

Lu Yang touched his forehead lightly, and a comatose Soul Body floated out.

"This person is the Si Chen God Lord of Great Qian."

After experiencing several self-detonations, Si Chen God Lord had luckily survived but had not woken up ever since.

Seeing the half-dead witness, Elder Brother Dai withdrew the Auspicious Cloud Banner, smilingly brought Lu Yang to the main seat, and began to massage his shoulders.

Lu Yang tried to get up several times but was firmly pressed down by Elder Brother Dai.

Elder Brother Dai leaned in close and whispered in Lu Yang’s ear, making Lu Yang shiver involuntarily, "Immortal Lu, what exactly is going on? What have you been doing in the Buddha Country?"

"Oh, nothing much, just that I’ve done many good deeds as usual, which are considered great merits to the Buddha Country. The Power of Incense and Fire from the Buddha Country just kept rushing towards me non-stop. I couldn’t even refuse it or block it, so I used the Power of Incense and Fire to condense an Immortal Body."

"And I also captured Si Chen God Lord with this Immortal Body."

"However, this Immortal Body was temporarily condensed and can’t leave the Buddha Country. Once I returned to our Great Xia, my realm reverted back to normal."

"What good deeds have you done, so much so that the Power of Incense was enough to condense an Immortal Body and dig out the Worldly Venerable of Buddhism?"

Lu Yang grinned and gave Elder Brother Dai a thumbs-up, "They don’t say Elder Brother Dai is the backbone of Seek Dao Sect for nothing; accurate to a tee."

Elder Brother Dai took Lu Yang’s banter as mere jest, and waved him off impatiently, his mind relieved from not seeing and worrying, "Go, go, go, if you refuse to explain, just leave it be. Quickly send Si Chen God Lord to Captive Peak."

"Alright."

Lu Yang briskly left the Task Hall, making his way familiarly into Captive Peak.

The inmates in Captive Peak who had been chatting, quieted down immediately upon seeing Lu Yang. They hastily bowed their heads, not daring to meet Lu Yang’s gaze, afraid of making eye contact.

The Child Grandmaster floated over with a cheerful laugh, "Little Lu is here, eh? What sort of person did you catch this time?"

"Hmm, I caught four, and this one is Si Chen God Lord of Great Qian, a Semi-Immortal."

Lu Yang moved Si Chen God Lord out from the Spiritual Space, not worried about Si Chen God Lord waking up and causing trouble with the initial form of Time Dao Fruit, as the Time Immortal had already used the Time Dao Fruit to lock down the capabilities of Si Chen God Lord’s Dao Fruit Outline.

Now, Si Chen God Lord seemed no different from one who was at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage.

"A Semi-Immortal, not bad, not bad." With even Immortals detained here, Grandmaster, upon seeing a Semi-Immortal, found it unsurprising.

He even thought Si Chen God Lord was apprehended by Yun Zhi.

Then, Lu Yang moved Qing Yan Venerable, at Early Tribulation Stage, Duan Qingshui, at Early Integration Stage, and Mo Xiaochou, at Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit, from the Green Peak Sword.

The three were frightened as Lu Yang gripped them and they sensed the realm of Captive Peak’s prisoners, shivering.

"Eldest Senior Sister will come over to interrogate Si Chen God Lord."

Lu Yang transmitted to Grandmaster, "Grandmaster, once it’s your turn, please interrogate Duan Qingshui and Mo Xiaochou. These two attempted theft at Western Paradise Temple; if there are no other cases against them, it shouldn’t extend to a death penalty. At worst, they’d be imprisoned for several decades or centuries."

Lu Yang was not worried that these two would reveal his affairs upon their release.

By the time these two were released, it would be many years later, and by then, it was likely already widely known.

"Alright, no need to bother with the rest." Grandmaster detained these four in the prison cells, and as he observed the increasingly insufficient cells, he felt a sense of satisfaction.

Seeing new faces, the curious prisoners of Captive Peak gathered. Qing Yan Venerable was somewhat better, having an acceptable cultivation level. Duan Qingshui and Mo Xiaochou were not; especially Mo Xiaochou, who was at the bottom, causing the prisoners to shake their heads and show disdain.

"What kind of cultivation are you two practicing? Were your techniques picked from a trash heap?"

"This spiritual essence circulation route is also wrong; there are so many errors."

"Come here, I’ll teach you how to cultivate properly."

The prisoners of Captive Peak were often bored, having nothing better to do; they might as well teach the newcomers how to cultivate.

Duan Qingshui and Mo Xiaochou, flattered, listened attentively.

...

In the Mysterious Land, a silhouette sat on a throne built of dry bones, pondering the freshly received intelligence, eyes gradually brightening; the hidden affairs behind this intelligence were no small matter.

"Mysterious Immortal, multiple self-detonations, could it be that the Eternity Dao Fruit has emerged?!"

"Who is this Mysterious Immortal, is it Immortal? Has Immortal been resurrected? Who did it, Ying Tian, Suiyue, them?"

The figure quickly calmed down, realizing something was amiss.

"No, according to the descriptions of Ying Tian, Suiyue, and others, if it were the Eternity Dao Fruit, the self-detonation power would have been greater."

"Plus, Ying Tian, Suiyue, and the others have no reason to resurrect Immortal; if Immortal were resurrected, how could they obtain the Eternity Dao Fruit?"

"Could it be the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit?"

At that thought, the figure stood excitedly and paced, "The Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit, the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit has finally appeared!"

"It’s just unfortunate that when the Eternity Sect first condensed the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit, they planned to wait a while to allow the Immortal to increase the consummation of the Initial Form, and that’s when they lost the initiative, disturbed by Yun Zhi of Seek Dao Sect, and even had their clone killed."

"If I had acted earlier, the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit would have been in my hands already!"

"If I had acquired the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, even if I faced the Silent Extermination Immortal, I would have a chance to escape!"

"However, that Mysterious Immortal is a bit strange; the body is condensed from the incense of Buddha’s Country, which means he isn’t an Immortal yet."

"That’s good, not being an Immortal is good."

Chapter 1054: 1053: I Am the Real Her!

The breathtakingly beautiful female immortal carried her basket of vegetables down the bustling street; oddly enough, bystanders looked the other way, as if they couldn’t see the stunning immortal at all.

Recalling the intelligence she had just gathered, the female immortal was distracted and gradually became panicked.

"A mysterious immortal self-destructed in Buddha Country, and at that time, that kid Lu Yang was at the Western Paradise Temple. It can’t be such a coincidence; that mysterious immortal must be Lu Yang!"

"How could it be, how could it be, how could that kid Lu Yang have an Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit? How did he get it?"

"His realm isn’t high enough; he definitely didn’t condense it himself. That means someone gave him the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit!"

"Immortal, it must be Immortal who resurrected. Only she could do such a thing!"

"This is bad, Qinghe..."

With that thought, Ashy Bean no longer mimicked the life of mortals, twisted space, and instantly arrived at the small and unpopular restaurant.

Ashy Bean carried her basket of vegetables into the kitchen and, seeing that Qinghe was still flipping through the recipe book, breathed a sigh of relief.

Seeing Ashy Bean hurry back in a rush, Qinghe closed the recipe book and with a calm and collected voice, said the words Ashy Bean least wanted to hear.

"You should have heard about the mysterious immortal appearing in Buddha Country, I need to find him."

"No, from now on you’re not going anywhere!"

Qinghe looked directly into Ashy Bean’s bright and angry immortal eyes and said, word by word, "I, must, go!"

Ashy Bean flew into a rage, instantly moved in front of Qinghe, grabbed her wrist, but Qinghe was no match and was immediately pushed against the wall; pots and pans fell, creating a noisy clatter in the kitchen.

"Qinghe, don’t push me!"

"Remember your place. The only reason I’ve kept you until now is because I wanted someone to accompany me. I can replace you at any time!"

Looking at the teeth-clenched Ashy Bean, Qinghe instead let out a laugh, which agitated Ashy Bean all the more.

"What are you laughing at!"

"I had only suspected that the mysterious immortal in Buddha Country was related to you, but seeing your extraordinary reaction now convinces me I was right."

Ashy Bean was seething with anger and let out a cold huff as she released Qinghe’s wrist.

"Immortal, is it? I will definitely kill her and prove that I am the true one!"

...

"Old Lu, how come you managed to come back? I heard you got carried away by the Nian Beast during the New Year," said Meng Jingzhou as he pushed open the door of the Abode, only to see a laugh on Lu Yang’s face that seemed insincere.

"The Nian Beast simply took a liking to me and brought me to the Buddha Country for a vacation; why can’t I return?"

"You went to Buddha Country again?" At the thought of Buddha Country’s endless deserts, Meng Jingzhou felt sorry for Old Lu. Having been away for several months, he must have suffered quite a bit in Buddha Country before his return.

The Nian Beast truly lives up to its reputation as an auspicious creature, always doing good deeds.

"Let me tell you, Buddha Country is really a great place. If you ever go there, brother, I’ve got you covered!" Lu Yang patted his chest as he assured, making a grand promise.

"You have that much clout?" Meng Jingzhou looked at Lu Yang skeptically, feeling that he probably shouldn’t have asked that question.

Lu Yang shook his head and sighed, humbly saying, "I am not talented; I attained immortality in Buddha Country."

"Psh, Old Lu, your lies have been getting more and more far-fetched lately."

"It’s okay, I know you don’t believe me, so I kept evidence."

Lu Yang cheerfully pulled out the Recording Orb, playing the first-person perspective of his fight against the Si Chen God Lord as he condescended to explain it personally for Meng Jingzhou.

"This is the Si Chen God Lord of Great Qian, not much of a talent, just that he has the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit and can manipulate time. Even Master Mingyu, the Abbot of the Western Paradise Temple, was defeated by him."

"Eh, it’s quite the coincidence actually, resurrecting the fairy counts as a great merit, just happened to be enough for me to become an immortal."

"I thought this won’t do, so I reluctantly became an immortal and took this kid down. See, this is the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s Avatar appearing, not a match for me either..."

"Later on, I also met the Time Immortal, who outright praised me as the strongest in the future."

Meng Jingzhou instantly became envious. His character wasn’t any worse than Old Lu’s, so why didn’t such good things happen to him?

"Why do I only come across such small fry?"

"What small fry have you come across?"

"That was about two months ago; I went out on a mission, planning to keep a low profile, when a sister thought I looked pretty good. She said her grandfather was gravely ill and about to celebrate his last birthday. She asked if I could pretend to be her Dao companion to give her grandfather a nice final birthday."

"That sister’s family was an inconspicuous little influential family, oh, considered a big clan locally."

"I’m happy to help, so I agreed, and thinking that it’s the least I could do for someone’s birthday, I casually picked out a piece of Top-grade Warm Jade from my Identity Jade."

"That sister’s cousin didn’t know the value, insisted my gift was a fake, saying he knew people from the Imperial City and wouldn’t be mistaken. He also said something about that sister’s poor taste, having chosen me as her Dao companion."

"When her grandfather came out, I looked and figured it wasn’t any serious illness; he just had an innate deficiency of Yang Qi, something that could be fixed with a drop of blood. I said I could cure him, but they still didn’t believe me.

Her grandfather, who seemed to be sensible, or perhaps was open-minded, said I could give it a try, but the family insisted on blocking me, calling me a swindler."

"Right when I was about to reveal my identity, some people from the Imperial City also arrived to celebrate the birthday. I don’t quite remember who it was specifically, I just remember someone’s minion — but that person recognized me.

Upon seeing me, he knelt down and called out ’Young Master Meng,’ immediately distancing himself from the cousin, claiming that in future business, he would only recognize that sister."

Lu Yang: "..."

How is this any worse than what I went through? I don’t even dare to reveal my real name in the Western Paradise Temple, and here you are, blatantly showing off your identity!

...

After giving the Spirit Fruit from the Time Immortal to Tang Chuanwu and instructing him to consume it in order, Lu Yang finally returned to Sky Gate Mountain.

"Second-in-command, you’re back?" Yun Mengmeng saw Lu Yang and smiled happily.

She jogged over and put a freshly woven flower crown on Lu Yang’s head: "I was scared to death when the Nian Beast took you away; I thought something might happen to you. It’s good that you’re back safe and sound."

"Hey, what could happen to me?"

"Oh, and this is the special product from Buddha Country we agreed on." Lu Yang took out a perfectly square sand sculpture from behind him. The sand sculpture was finely crafted, with every detail lovingly attended to.

This was a magic artifact that Lu Yang crafted on the way back while he still possessed the Immortal’s Body.

Though Lu Yang wasn’t exactly skilled at crafting magic artifacts, with the empowerment from the Immortal’s Body, even a casual attempt resulted in a high-quality artifact.

For some reason, Lu Yang had two voices in his mind: one said that Fairy Eternity had already brought a special product from Buddha Country for Yun Mengmeng; the other voice insisted that hadn’t happened.

In his quandary, Lu Yang figured he might as well bring back a special product for Yun Mengmeng this time.

"Thank you, Second-in-command!" Yun Mengmeng smiled sweetly, carefully storing away the sand sculpture as if afraid to damage it.

"Second-in-command, what place is this sand sculpture of?"

Lu Yang explained earnestly: "Due to some special reasons, if you visited Buddha Country recently, it would be difficult to see Western Paradise Temple in its complete form. The sand sculpture in your hands is the most complete depiction of Western Paradise Temple, so consider it as if you’ve been there."

Chapter 1055: 1054: Reunion of the Sect

Yun Mengmeng became extremely attached to the sand sculpture she received from the Western Paradise Temple; the sculpture was exquisite, perfectly recreating the temple’s appearance before the battle.

"Did something happen to the Western Paradise Temple that caused it to become incomplete?"

Lu Yang fiercely smacked his thigh, indignantly saying, "It’s all the fault of Si Chen God Lord of Great Qian. He attacked the Western Paradise Temple for his own selfish gains, turning it into ruins."

"How can someone be like this, it’s so despicable." Yun Mengmeng felt indignant on behalf of the temple, thinking Si Chen God Lord was really a bad apple.

"Right? That’s what I think too." Lu Yang agreed.

At that moment, Gan Tian walked over, hearing the conversation between Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng, she wore a strange expression but didn’t say much.

"Eldest Senior Sister said that it’s the first time the four of us from the sect are together, so tonight we all should eat together. She asked if Junior Brother you were free for a barbecue?"

"Of course, I have time. Why would Eldest Senior Sister think I wouldn’t have time?"

"She said you might be busy showing off about becoming an immortal in Buddha Country and might not be available."

The corner of Lu Yang’s eyes twitched, Eldest Senior Sister really knew him too well.

"Second-in-command, you became an immortal?" Yun Mengmeng looked at Lu Yang admiringly, truly befitting the Second-in-command, so capable!

Lu Yang couldn’t help but smile, "Speaking of which, it was all forced by circumstances. Let’s go down the mountain to buy some ingredients, and I can tell you all about it..."

By the time the Second-in-command and Third Boss returned from shopping, the sky had darkened. Lu Yang was carrying ingredients snatched up from a barbecue restaurant, while Yun Mengmeng was carrying dishes from the Hundred Fragrance Building.

Ye Zijin arranged a long table, with dishes from the Hundred Fragrance Building displayed on top. Lu Yang released the Three Flavored True Fire, and soon the long-lost aroma of grilled skewers filled the air.

Gan Tian fiddled with an ancient zither, channeling her spiritual essence into it. It produced a gentle melody on its own. Although not as perfect as Gan Tian playing it personally, it still captured about seventy to eighty percent of its charm. This tune helped others make breakthroughs and was incredibly rare in the outside world.

"Third Junior Sister, don’t you think it’s not quite right for our master to miss the sect’s gathering?"

Gan Tian continued toying with her zither, not turning back as she responded, "Who knows where he is right now, and even if we called him, it would be too late, wouldn’t it?"

"Senior Brother has a way."

Ye Zijin placed a portrait of Daoist Buyu at the end of the table, arranging food and wine in front of it, fully expressing filial piety.

"This makes it much better."

"The skewers are ready!" Lu Yang professionally called out, bringing over two large plates of skewers, and thoughtfully picked a few to place in front of the portrait as well.

Eldest Senior Sister arrived fashionably late, her arrival signifying that dinner had officially begun.

Eldest Senior Sister, Lu Yang, and Yun Mengmeng sat in a row, with Ye Zijin and Gan Tian facing them. The five raised their glasses to celebrate the sect’s reunion.

"A toast to our master, who is far away."

"Master, can you see this? Your four disciples have all come back."

"Master, all your four disciples have become successful."

Ye Zijin took a bite of skewer, eyes widening, "Junior Brother, these skewers are really something. If I had known you were this good, I wouldn’t have taken you to that lousy hot pot in Western Heaven City back then; you could have just treated your senior brother to skewers, right?"

Ye Zijin’s taste buds, which had been tormented for hundreds of years in Buddha Country, began to gradually recover now that he was tasting the popular skewers of Great Xia.

Damn, what the hell was I eating back in Buddha Country!

Yun Mengmeng started eating voraciously as if she wished she had three heads and six arms. Her cheeks smeared with barbecue sauce, yet she didn’t forget her good sister, "Xiao Zhi, let’s clink glasses."

Yun Zhi gently touched her teacup.

Yun Mengmeng drank her peach juice in one gulp and sighed, "I didn’t expect you, Xiao Zhi, to meet so many people outside. Back home, you were always buried in your books, not talking to anyone. I was quite worried about you when you left with Bad Peach."

"Knowing so many people, that’s really good."

Yun Mengmeng genuinely felt happy for her good sister.

"Eldest Senior Sister, I toast to you too. The achievements I have today are all thanks to your help," Lu Yang took the opportunity to express his gratitude to Eldest Senior Sister as he clinked his cup with hers.

Gan Tian joined in with a toast, "Eldest Senior Sister, when Master was away, it was you who guided my cultivation. Whenever I encountered any difficulties in the Demon Territory, you solved them for me. Thank you."

"We are sect siblings, no need for thanks," said Eldest Senior Sister.

They clinked their cups.

"Eldest Senior Sister, I toast to you. Had you not sent me to Buddha Country to gain experience, my cultivation level wouldn’t have increased this quickly. I only understand your good intentions today," he said.

"Second Disciple, you overstate things. Buddha Country is desolate, and these years, you struggled while tracing the clues to the Time Immortal. It wasn’t easy. Now that you are back, you should just peacefully stay and cultivate in the sect," she replied.

Ye Zijin, with his meticulous mind, knew that Eldest Senior Sister was sensitive about her age; otherwise, he wouldn’t have gone to Buddha Country. This time he definitely needed to flatter her according to her wishes.

"What are you talking about, Eldest Senior Sister? At such a young age, you took on the responsibility of managing the sect for Master. You truly had it the hardest," he complimented.

Clang, Lu Yang’s teacup dropped onto the table, and he stared blankly at Ye Zijin with his mouth agape.

Second Senior Brother, your banishment to Buddha Country wasn’t without reason after all.

Lu Yang subtly scooted to the right, closer to Yun Mengmeng and farther from Eldest Senior Sister.

If Eldest Senior Sister really started something, he would quickly hide behind Yun Mengmeng.

...

Second Senior Brother was not one to sit still. He didn’t like staying in the sect and had proposed going out to cultivate on his own. When Lu Yang left seclusion, Second Senior Brother had already left, leaving behind only a letter, without even a chance to say goodbye.

"By the way, Second-in-command, I forgot to give these to you yesterday. These Storage Rings are yours," Yun Mengmeng pulled out several Storage Rings.

Lu Yang took the Storage Rings, puzzled, only to find them filled with Spirit Stones.

"So many Spirit Stones, where did they come from?!"

"They were sent over from the Qiongqi Clan, saying that the business of making Recording Orbs is doing well. They wanted to give you a ten percent profit," she explained.

"You were always away, so Xiao Zhi mentioned that I should collect them for you until you returned," she added.

"So that’s it. It looks like Senior Jin Caiwei’s business is going well," Lu Yang would have almost forgotten that he had suggested Jin Caiwei start producing three-dimensional Recording Orbs, if Yun Mengmeng had not mentioned it.

"If I had known about the three-dimensional Recording Orbs earlier, I would have used them to record the battle scene with the Si Chen God Lord!" Lu Yang regretted profoundly. If it wasn’t because his identity as the Tofu Heavenly Venerate needed to be kept secret, he would definitely make thousands of copies of the Recording Orbs and hand them out for free in the streets.

"The Qiongqi Clan is finally doing well. Little Yang, by giving the Qiongqi Clan business ideas, you have a share in their good fortune," Immortal Fairy emerged, feeling quite emotional, not expecting Little Tiger to have a day of doing business and making money.

"It must be because Little Yang has a tough fate to have subdued the poor luck of the Qiongqi Clan," she commented.

Curiously, Yun Mengmeng asked, "Second-in-command has a tough fate, so why does trouble always follow him wherever he goes?"

Immortal Fairy waved her hand, "Is it because their fates aren’t tough enough?"

Chapter 1056: 1055: The Immortal, Flexible Yet Resilient

Yun Mengmeng recalled the deeds of the Second-in-command over the past years and concluded that wherever he went, trouble seemed to follow.

When the Second-in-command returned to the Seek Dao Sect, the sect remained safe and undisturbed.

"Wait, I heard that our sect had also encountered problems," Yun Mengmeng recalled what Yun Zhi had mentioned about two incidents, "The second Imperial Teacher of Great Yu and the Master of All Laws both infiltrated our sect. Doesn’t that mean the fate of our Seek Dao Sect is not strong enough?"

"Merely two overconfident Semi-Immortals, are they even worth mentioning?" Fairy Eternity scoffed dismissively at the two, one unable to defeat the Fourth Housekeeper, and the other losing to the Vast Sea Daoist Lord, hardly stirring a ripple.

"It does seem that was the case," Yun Mengmeng loyally trusted everything her Chief Housekeeper said.

"Second-in-command, what are you thinking about?" Yun Mengmeng noticed Lu Yang was lost in thought and had not interjected throughout the conversation.

Lu Yang snapped back to reality, "I was thinking, as the Tofu Heavenly Venerate who thwarted Great Qian’s plot and saved the Western Paradise Temple, relying solely on rumors and storytelling to spread such significant deeds—isn’t the efficiency too low?"

"Furthermore, with frequent major events in the cultivation world, the commonfolk lack an official channel to learn about these occurrences, which is not conducive to broadening their horizons."

Blinking her eyes, Yun Mengmeng eagerly awaited the Second-in-command’s enlightening answer, wanting to say that the world of cultivation didn’t seem to change so much before he appeared. However, she felt it would be rather inappropriate to bring that up now.

"Have you ever heard of ’newspapers’?"

"Mm, it’s a type of paper, right? With news about recent events in the cultivation world written on it," Lu Yang tried to explain the concept of newspapers to Yun Mengmeng in the simplest terms.

"But many people can’t read, can they?"

"Not being able to read is no issue—we can add a reading Array Method to the newspapers."

"But the cultivation world is so vast, wouldn’t distributing newspapers everywhere take a lot of time?"

"We can ask for the help of the Vast Sea Ancestral Master. We could ask that elder to set up a few spatial passages."

The more he spoke, the more feasible Lu Yang found the idea and he immediately set out, dragging Yun Mengmeng along to find the Vast Sea Daoist Lord.

Unfortunately, the Vast Sea Daoist Lord was not at the sect; according to the Grandmaster Daoist Xiantian’s guess, he had gone to the East Sea to rendezvous with his old flame, Ao Ya.

Lu Yang also felt that Ancestor Vast Sea had indeed gone to the East Sea to see an old lover without needing Daoist Xiantian’s guess.

With Yun Mengmeng leading the way, the duo traveled unhindered across the surface of the East Sea, encountering no Sea Clan who dared to block their passage and declare their territory a no-fly zone.

This was Yun Mengmeng’s first visit to the Dragon Palace of the East Sea, and she salivated at the sight of the shrimp soldiers and crab generals, who were unaware of her thoughts and felt a deep, instinctive fear.

Ao Ling was not in the East Sea, but knowing that Lu Yang was Ao Ling’s honored guest, the Old Dragon Emperor warmly received him.

With the Old Dragon Emperor’s assistance, Lu Yang waited a whole two days before he finally saw the Vast Sea Ancestral Master gracelessly staggering out of Ao Ya’s bedroom, leaning on the wall.

When the Vast Sea Daoist Lord saw it was Lu Yang, he made an effort to straighten his back and put on a solemn face.

Lu Yang, acting oblivious to the Ancestor’s recent activities, explained his purpose for coming. Realizing it was a matter beneficial to the people, the Vast Sea Daoist Lord readily agreed.

"Establishing spatial passages is easy, and paper is not a problem," Great Xia was not in need of paper, the Dynasty had its own paper-making institution, which did not require cultivators; mortals could complete the task.

"The issue is how do you plan to mass-produce the newspapers? Will you use the printing technique, and how will you replicate the voice, then?"

"I can do that," Yun Mengmeng excitedly raised her hand, pulling out a row of brushes from her Storage Ring, equally spaced.

"When my grandmother punished me by making me copy books, I used this. It speeds up the process by ten times!" It was a treasure from her cherished childhood.

Lu Yang already had a solution: "Great Yu National Preceptor Guan Shanhai has the Imprinting Dao Fruit, and with his abilities, copying mere newspapers should not be a problem."

"Allowing him to regain mastery over the Imprinting Dao Fruit is somewhat dangerous, but since he was able to comprehend the Imprinting Dao Fruit, it’s clear he must be proficient in such magic skills, just give him a speck of spiritual essence, and let him use magic skills to copy it."

"Guan Shanhai has nothing to do all day at Captive Peak, and our Seek Dao Sect, worried he might be lonely by himself, specifically captured a group of prisoners to keep him company. It’s only right that he contributes to our Sect."

Vast Sea Daoist Lord looked at Lu Yang with a strange expression. Is our Sect really that good to Guan Shanhai? Why have I heard that he still has some matters unresolved and that Yun Zhi whips him periodically?

"Then, Ancestor, you go ahead with your tasks. Oh right, if you’re running short on Yang Qi, I suggest you work more with Old Meng, since his blood contains a very rich amount of Yang Qi.

The only problem is his low cultivation level; once his cultivation level goes up, your problem will be solved." As his close friend, Lu Yang didn’t forget to put in a good word for Meng Jingzhou in front of the Ancestor.

"That’s right." Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s eyes lit up, he worked every day urging Elixir Cauldron Peak to produce elixirs, how could he forget about the Meng Family kid.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord directly opened a spatial channel, taking Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng back to the Seek Dao Sect.

Upon returning to the Seek Dao Sect, they immediately went to find Meng Jingzhou to see how the Meng Family kid’s cultivation was coming along.

Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng, on the other hand, headed to Captive Peak.

Upon entering Captive Peak, they heard a group of prisoners intensely discussing something.

"Hey, hey, hey, can you write the cultivation technique like this? At best, this will only allow one to cultivate to the Early Tribulation Stage."

"The cultivation technique should be written as I say, guaranteeing cultivation up to the Middle Tribulation Stage."

"Does your cultivation technique even match Mo Xiaochou’s constitution to listen to your method?"

"That’s simple, after he goes out, just follow my method for taking elixirs, and it’s guaranteed to raise his physique by a notch."

In his lifetime, Mo Xiaochou never imagined he would be surrounded by a group of Transcendance Tribulation Stage prisoners discussing how to make his cultivation better, no, not just Transcendance Tribulation Stage, there were even Semi-Immortals among them.

At the end of Captive Peak were legendary Immortals, who rarely spoke, but when they did, their words had the effect of finality.

Suddenly, all the prisoners at Captive Peak fell silent. Even though Lu Yang hadn’t entered Captive Peak yet, they all had a premonition that Lu Yang was coming.

Sure enough, Lu Yang really did come to Captive Peak again.

"What brings you here again, kid?" The child Grandmaster was startled. Wasn’t it just a few days ago he came over? Has the speed of capturing increased to a daily basis now?

"I’m here to find Guan Shanhai."

"Oh, just walk straight ahead to the end." The child Grandmaster trusted Lu Yang completely, not even asking what he intended to do.

Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng walked to the end and saw Guan Shanhai reduced to a mere prisoner.

"Chap Lu, what business do you have with me?"

Lu Yang did not hide his intentions and explained his thoughts to Guan Shanhai.

Guan Shanhai snorted coldly, his gaze sharp as he stood up, staring intently into Lu Yang’s eyes.

"Do you know that my Imprinting Dao Fruit, when cultivated to the extreme, can be used to create worlds? Those created under the influence of the Dao Fruit may not even be aware of how they came to be!"

"And now you want me to use the Imprinting Dao Fruit for such trivial matters, where should an Immortal’s dignity lie?"

Lu Yang said earnestly, "Then how about I ask Eldest Senior Sister to speak with you?"

"But then again," Guan Shanhai continued, "helping others for joy and benefiting the people does not diminish an Immortal’s dignity."

Chapter 1057: 1056: Bystander Lu

Publishing a newspaper was indeed a major event, not something that Lu Yang could decide alone.

Although Lu Yang had already prepared every aspect, he still needed the approval of the Eldest Senior Sister to make the final decision.

"A newspaper? Good idea, I support it," Yun Zhi quickly understood the importance of the newspaper after a brief consideration—it was an excellent channel for enlightening the masses and spreading knowledge.

"Do we need Guan Shanhai to use his imprinting ability? I’ll threaten him to do it," Yun Zhi asked, also recognizing Guan Shanhai’s crucial role in the making of the newspaper.

In her memory, Guan Shanhai was a prisoner of considerable resilience, never confessing no matter how he was beaten.

"Guan Shanhai has already agreed to help," Lu Yang said.

Yun Zhi nodded in surprise, "Quite cooperative, that does save trouble."

"Have you thought of a name for the newspaper?"

"Considering this is our first attempt at creating and distributing, and not knowing how the people will react to the content, it’s not appropriate to publish too frequently. We’ve temporarily settled on a monthly publication, how about calling it ’Cultivation Monthly’?"

Lu Yang also planned to adjust the content of the newspaper timely based on the feedback from the people, to cater to their needs.

Yun Zhi had no objections, as her junior brother’s naming skills had always been like this, no need to be overly critical.

"But Second-in-command, can we name the newspaper ’Great Bean Monthly’?" Yun Mengmeng suddenly realized not only was she the Third Boss of the Immortal Lineage but also the Minister of Rites for the Great Bean Dynasty, which came with the responsibility of promotion.

"Ah, that’s a good name." Fairy Eternity clapped her hands in approval, seeing it as a perfect opportunity to promote their Great Bean Dynasty.

Seeing the reaction, Lu Yang quickly placed his hands in his sleeves and bowed, "Your Majesty is thoroughly dedicated to the prosperity of the dynasty, and we are deeply impressed by your efforts."

"Being a newly established kingdom, from ancient times any power with ambitions has gone through a period of laying low before openly rebelling with an uprising. Currently, with many official positions in the Great Bean Dynasty remaining vacant and a shortage of manpower and military force, extensively publicizing the dynasty could be acting hastily.

This matter requires further careful planning, waiting for the right opportunity. I hope Your Majesty will consider this thoroughly!"

Fairy Eternity, feeling confused, did not grasp the meaning of Lu Yang’s words, "Then let’s just follow your advice, Little Yang."

Overjoyed at her response, Lu Yang bowed again, "Your Majesty is wise."

Yun Zhi, who had been listening, wanted to ask her junior brother exactly what position he held in the Great Bean Dynasty, but she worried that after asking, the Immortal Senior might remember her and assign her a position as well—so she decided to temporarily put aside her curiosity and instead asked about the newspaper.

"Junior brother, have you thought about the content of the newspaper?"

Lu Yang had already had a general idea about the newspaper content on his way here: "I’ve heard that many people still don’t understand even the basic levels of cultivation. I’m thinking about providing a detailed introduction to cultivation knowledge in the first issue, along with a few Qi Cultivation Mantras."

The public most commonly encountered Qi Cultivation Stage cultivators; for the higher Foundation Establishment Stage and Golden Core Stage, they were only aware of the concepts but not the might and disparity of each realm. Since we’re telling cultivation stories, it is natural to first let the people understand the basic concepts of cultivation.

"Another thing is about the significant events in the Cultivation World, such as the recent battle at the Western Paradise Temple."

"If there are any good stories, we could also serialize them in the newspaper..."

Lu Yang carefully explained the functions of each section of the newspaper, and Yun Zhi nodded frequently, seeing that her junior brother had given this much thought.

Yun Mengmeng watched Lu Yang with admiration as well, considering how thorough the Second-in-command was—she could not have thought of all these details herself.

Afterward, Lu Yang found the Third Elder’s eldest disciple, Ji Hongwen. Brother Ji specialized in the art of painting, and Lu Yang asked him to design the layout for the newspaper.

Ji Hongwen gladly agreed.

"Oh right, leave a gap in the middle of the newspaper; I’m going to use it to advertise for Junior Sister Tao," Lu Yang added a small request.

After all, it was the first edition of the newspaper, and there were no other advertisers, so it might as well benefit their own people.

The newspaper production process was roughly straightened out. Now, the challenge Lu Yang faced was how to describe the battle at Western Paradise Temple objectively and fairly without revealing his identity.

An idea struck Lu Yang, "That’s it, the battle caused such a commotion, and the people of Western Heaven City witnessed it all. I can write from the perspective of a bystander, as if I were interviewing myself."

Lu Yang was brimming with inspiration and quickly wrote a report on the battle at Western Paradise Temple, filling in the other sections with content as well.

He then handed the original newspaper to Guan Shanhai, who was a master of the Imprinting Dao Fruit. In the blink of an eye, Guan Shanhai had copied millions of copies of the newspaper, and even the voice broadcast array method was replicated, significantly reducing the production cost of the newspaper.

By reducing production costs, the price of the newspaper dropped, making it affordable for the common folk.

Lu Yang’s purpose in publishing the newspaper wasn’t profit but propaganda.

The disciples of the Seek Dao Sect, through the spatial passage, sold the first edition of "Cultivation Weekly" throughout Great Xia.

...

"...According to investigations, at the beginning of the fight at Western Paradise Temple, there were two combatants. One was Si Chen, the God Lord of Great Qian, while the other’s identity remains a mystery. Our report speculates, based on hints and whispers, that the latter might be a Mysterious Immortal from Ancient Times, reluctant to engage in the fight."

"As stated by the onlooker I, Lu, the Mysterious Immortal initially intended to spare the life of Si Chen, the God Lord. However, Si Chen provoked the Mysterious Immortal upon seeing him and even questioned the Immortal’s literacy, asking if he knew how to write."

"The Mysterious Immortal, with no other choice, engaged in combat. The Mysterious Immortal’s profound understanding of Dao and swordmanship overwhelmed Si Chen, decisively defeating him in mere moments, forcing him to kneel and beg for mercy."

"An avatar of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord appeared on the scene and, without questioning the right or wrong, simply stated ’What Si Chen did was wrong,’ hoping the Mysterious Immortal would spare Si Chen’s life."

"Afterward, the Emperor’s avatar, realizing the Mysterious Immortal was entangled in causality and could not utilize full power, was still unsure whether he could match him in combat."

"To fight the Mysterious Immortal, the Emperor’s avatar devoured Si Chen, the God Lord, leaving his fate unknown as he was not seen after the battle ended."

"During the fight between the Mysterious Immortal and the Emperor’s avatar, an explosion occurred for unknown reasons, and the scene became too chaotic to determine who caused it. The onlooker I, Lu stated that he heard the Emperor’s avatar laugh amidst the explosion and shout something about ’self-detonation.’

"After the explosion, Western Paradise Temple lay in ruins, the Emperor’s avatar was nowhere to be seen, and the Mysterious Immortal left gracefully."

"Upon further investigation by our newspaper, we discovered that Si Chen, the God Lord, had been lurking in Western Paradise Temple for over a hundred years, hiding beside a young monk, and it remains unclear if he had any special preferences."

Inside the Fairy Palace, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, sitting on his celestial throne, held the newly delivered newspaper, reading it word by word. He trembled with rage, angrier than he had been in a long time.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord clenched his teeth in fury, tore the newspaper to shreds, and furiously struck his celestial throne. His angry voice echoed throughout the Fairy Palace.

"Who the hell wrote this!"

Chapter 1058: 1057: Missing Person Notice

How could the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord not be furious? Although his avatar had died at the Western Heaven Temple, the intelligence from that time had been transmitted back.

"What’s this about Si Chen saying the Mysterious Immortal can’t write, and when the Mysterious Immortal commanded Si Chen to kneel, he kneeled, and when I saw the Mysterious Immortal, I admitted my mistake, and it was me devouring Si Chen that led to his uncertain fate of life and death?"

"And wasn’t it the damned opposite side who self-detonated? How did it become me who seemed to be the one who self-detonated? I’m a victim too, I’ve lost my clone!"

What made the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord most furious was that the newspaper’s description was all objective facts, leaving him clueless about how to refute them!

To lose a battle was one thing, but how had his image also collapsed?

"Who the hell wrote this!"

Finally, he found the journalist responsible for the article in the corner of the newspaper—Lu Yang from Seek Dao Sect.

When he saw the three words ’Seek Dao Sect,’ the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord remembered the countless avatars he had lost there, and the anger rising to his chest suddenly dropped down, calming him.

"I... never mind, a person aiming to accomplish great things should not get bogged down in trivial matters, it’s not worth taking the risk over such a small issue..."

...

The newspaper issue was soon welcomed by the common folk.

"What is that?"

"I heard it’s a newfangled contraption by the Seek Dao Sect, called the ’newspaper.’ I saw a crowd rushing to buy it, so I bought a copy too. It’s not expensive, just five copper coins."

"Why buy that thing." Old Zhang snorted disdainfully; the sight of the densely packed small characters on the newspaper irritated him, "Do the two of us together know even twenty characters?"

"That’s where you’re clueless, this ’newspaper’ thing doesn’t need us to read it, it can read itself out loud."

"That miraculous?" Old Zhang’s interest was piqued; regardless of what was written on it, the function of reading itself out loud was worth the five copper coins.

"Let me think, the newspaper seller said to press hard here." Every section of the newspaper had a circle that, when pressed, would make that section produce sound.

The circles were the Array Eyes of the chanting array method, which, once activated, could absorb the surrounding spiritual energy and transform it into speech.

The sound array method requires delicate work, and each edition of the newspaper is different. Using array methods for printing the newspaper would necessitate adjusting the printing array pattern monthly, so it would be more convenient to directly have Guan Shanhai take charge.

"Cultivation is a process of absorbing spiritual energy from the heavens and earth, transforming it into one’s own spiritual essence..."

"The levels of cultivation are divided into Qi Cultivation Stage, Foundation Establishment Stage, Golden Core Stage, Nascent Soul Stage... The Qi Cultivation Stage is characterized by..."

Old Zhang showed a look of sudden realization upon hearing this, "So the Nascent Soul Stage comes after the Golden Core Stage? I always thought Gold Core Period Cultivators were stronger."

"Why would you think that?" asked Old Liu, surprised. Although he hadn’t received much formal education, being with Old Zhang made him feel almost like a scholar.

Old Zhang scratched the back of his head, "Didn’t the new storyteller that came to town always talk about ’how Talent Lu from the Nascent Soul Stage would reverse defeat the Golden Core Stage’?"

"...Just keep listening."

"Dream Mirage, offering you an experience of a different world..."

"Seek Dao Sect, a good Sect that eradicates demons and defends the Dao..."

"...According to investigations, at the beginning of the recent battle of the Western Heaven Temple, the two warring parties included a total of two..."

The two old farmers were quickly captivated by the content on the newspaper, as it introduced them to a whole new world.

This was different from the stories told by the storytellers; each of them had their variations, and there were even contradictions among them, leaving the two old farmers confused and uncertain about whom to believe.

But the Cultivation Monthly was different; it was a newspaper published by the Seek Dao Sect, and the content was absolutely true and reliable. Moreover, you could listen to it as many times as you wanted.

Similar situations occurred throughout Great Xia, as the Cultivation Monthly, this brand new source of information, was sought after by the populace upon its introduction, even experiencing a demand that exceeded supply.

"So it seems that we still printed too few?" Lu Yang was startled when he heard from his junior brothers and sisters that the newspapers had sold out quickly. He had thought they had printed too many, but it still wasn’t enough.

The junior brothers and sisters looked up to Lu Yang with admiration. Elder Brother Lu Yang always had brilliant ideas; when would they be able to catch up with his pace?

"Then let Guan Shanhai continue printing."

With Guan Shanhai around, any number of newspapers could be printed.

"It’s just that the story section is not quite satisfactory." The story Lu Yang wrote was based on Meng Jingzhou pretending to be a Dao companion and attending a small influential family’s birthday celebration. It was a story put together in a rush.

However, Lu Yang felt his literary skills were lacking, and the story didn’t quite hit the mark. It needed to be polished.

"If only Master were here, he would definitely be able to write a good story."

Lu Yang missed Daoist Buyu, whom he had not seen for several years.

"Although Eldest Senior Sister could find Master, I am now at the Immortal Transformation Stage, and I should not rely on Eldest Senior Sister for everything. I can find Master in my own way."

"Third Senior Sister, I have an idea that I want you to hear and see if it works." Lu Yang approached Senior Sister Gan Tian, who was playing the qin by the river, with Yun Mengmeng kicking water on a big rock not far away.

After listening to Lu Yang’s plan, Senior Sister Gan Tian took a while to repeat his idea:

"You mean, in the next issue of the newspaper, you want to insert a missing person announcement, offering a reward to find our Master, and the reward is ten billion spirit stones?"

"Good idea, right?" Lu Yang, now wealthy and powerful, could easily offer ten billion spirit stones without breaking a sweat.

"Isn’t that reward a bit too high?" Senior Sister Gan Tian hesitated; it was almost the price of Master in the black market.

"But this is our Master, the Sect Master of the Seek Dao Sect, a Great Power at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage who has been missing for so many years. Isn’t his whereabouts worth ten billion?"

After thinking it over, Senior Sister Gan Tian felt she was being too unfilial: "You’re right, I’ll add fifty million spirit stones myself."

Lu Yang believed that Master would surely understand his hint that he wanted him to return to the sect. Of course, even if Master did not understand, someone would bring him back.

He could predict that with the Cultivation Monthly selling so well, and with the help of Master in the future, it would surely be a best-seller in the Cultivation World.

Lu Yang wanted to share this good news with Old Meng, but unexpectedly, he received bad news from the Third Elder about Meng Jingzhou.

"You mean Old Meng was taken by the Vast Sea Ancestral Master to practice near the Sun?"

"Indeed, I was teaching Little Meng form and technique, and out of nowhere, the Vast Sea Ancestral Master came and whisked Little Meng away."

The Third Elder looked distressed. Meng Jingzhou practicing near the Sun would surely speed up his cultivation, and with the Ancestral Master’s supervision, he would certainly avoid danger.

But wasn’t this approach a bit too radical?

"This is Old Meng’s opportunity."

Putting himself in Meng Jingzhou’s shoes, Lu Yang knew Meng Jingzhou himself wouldn’t be happy, but indeed, this was Meng Jingzhou’s chance.

So Lu Yang had to reluctantly be happy for Meng Jingzhou.

Chapter 1059: 1058 Different People Have Different Cultivation Methods

"Ancestor, could we try a different method of cultivation?"

Meng Jingzhou’s body shivered as Vast Sea Daoist Lord brought him near the sun, feeling its scorching heat. The Yang Qi that had always been suppressed within him began to stir restlessly, threatening to break out of control at any moment.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord, with a stern face, put his hands on Meng Jingzhou’s shoulders and said earnestly, "Little Meng, as the saying goes, ’only through great suffering can one become superior to others.’ If you want to surpass Little Lu, you must endure the pain that others cannot, understand?"

The thought of cultivating near the sun to defeat Lu Yang instantly ignited Meng Jingzhou’s fighting spirit, and he forcibly suppressed the fire of desire within him.

"Alright, I’ll cultivate!"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord smiled, "That’s the spirit."

"Don’t worry, I’ve mastered an ancient skill, you won’t lose control."

Meng Jingzhou’s eyes lit up, "Ancient skill?"

Lu Yang has an Immortal by his side and has mastered ancient magic, and now it’s finally his turn to enjoy the benefits of an ancient method too?

"Of course, do you know how the ancients healed injuries and diseases before alchemy was systematized?"

"How did they do it?"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord looked into Meng Jingzhou’s eyes and slowly uttered two words:

"Bloodletting."

Meng Jingzhou instantly felt the urge to turn and run, but he forced a smile and said, "Ancestor, I think surpassing Lu Yang isn’t something we need to rush. Could we go back and consider some other methods of cultivation?"

"We’re already here, let’s give it a try."

Vast Sea Daoist Lord didn’t give Meng Jingzhou a chance to escape, pulling him closer to the sun. Meng Jingzhou felt the spiritual essence within him roil, and his cultivation speed rose to a new level, his Body Refinement Realm’s effects were also unprecedented!

The only problem was that his Single Body Spiritual Root was becoming difficult to control!

Vast Sea Daoist Lord made a light cut on Meng Jingzhou’s wrist, and Pure Yang Blood surged out, calming the Single Body Spiritual Root temporarily.

"See, I told you this method would work." Vast Sea Daoist Lord said with a grin, urging Meng Jingzhou to seize the opportunity to cultivate.

He secretly tasted a bit of the Pure Yang Blood and found it indeed effective, but Meng Jingzhou’s cultivation level was too low, which limited the help that Pure Yang Blood could offer him.

Seeing that this method was indeed effective, Meng Jingzhou steeled his heart and decided to go all in. I’ll cultivate!

"Hey, hey Ancestor, my Single Body Spiritual Root is acting up again!"

"Don’t panic, I’ll continue the bloodletting."

...

"Vast Sea Ancestral Master is personally teaching him, Old Meng is lucky." Lu Yang clicked his tongue. Although he couldn’t share the good news of his newspaper creation with Old Meng for now, he knew that Old Meng was in a critical moment of cultivation and should not be disturbed.

"Junior Brother, I heard that Vast Sea Ancestral Master took Junior Brother Meng to cultivate on the sun?" Yun Zhi, not knowing where she heard the news, approached Lu Yang.

"I’m thinking that cultivating in the universe is indeed a good method; I could also take you to try it out." Yun Zhi was very concerned about Lu Yang’s cultivation progress.

"Ah?"

Without giving Lu Yang a chance to resist, Yun Zhi pulled him into the cosmos, arriving amidst the stars.

"Eldest Senior Sister, are we going to..."

Yun Zhi pointed at an ordinary-looking star not far away, "That star is one of my Golden Cores, containing quite pure Gold Element Power. Your Sword Spirit Root, which evolved from a Golden Spirit Root, is most suited for cultivation here."

Lu Yang sensed for a moment, but he didn’t feel that the star contained any Gold Element Power.

"Using a Golden Core to replace a star is too ostentatious, so I usually try to keep my Golden Cores looking inconspicuous," Yun Zhi explained.

While she spoke, Yun Zhi snapped her fingers, and the ordinary-looking star suddenly burst forth with a terrifying amount of Gold Element Power. Lu Yang’s Spiritual Sense felt as if it were being pricked by needles, the pain was intense.

"Junior Brother, go ahead. Junior Brother Meng has been diligently cultivating—you can’t afford to fall behind."

The Gold Star exerted a strong gravitational pull, and Lu Yang was uncontrollably drawn towards it.

As the Gold Element Power surged around him, Lu Yang found himself trapped in an Earth Gold ocean, screaming in agony. No matter how he struggled, he couldn’t escape the gravitational pull of the Gold Star. Luckily, his body was fortified by torment and was exceptionally strong. If it had been his body from the past, the pain would have been even worse.

"Ah—"

"Hold on, Little Yang! You can definitely do it," Fairy Eternity encouraged Lu Yang from the sidelines. She had floated out of the Spiritual Space before Lu Yang was drawn to the Gold Star and stood beside Yun Zhi, cheering for Lu Yang from a distance.

"Fairy, could you offer something more than just moral support—ah—"

Lu Yang’s pain was so great that he couldn’t finish his sentence and continued to scream.

...

Over the vast grasslands, a figure raced, followed closely by four figures in hot pursuit.

These four figures were from Great Xia, East Sea, Buddha Country, and Demon Territory, all Transcendance Tribulation Stage Cultivators. When confronted with a common enemy, the Transcendance Tribulation Stage Cultivators set aside their differences and united against the external threat.

"Old Buyu, stop running! Everyone at Seek Dao Sect is very concerned about you. Return to Seek Dao Sect with us at once!"

"Aren’t I currently on my way to Seek Dao Sect? Why are you still chasing me?" Old Buyu retorted angrily.

Daoist Buyu was already highly valuable, with bounties on him in the black market and across the East Sea. Now, the bounties had only increased further.

"No way, what if you change direction midway? We must capture you to be at ease!"

"Once you capture me, it will be my turn to be worried. Also, that monk from Buddha Country, I haven’t provoked you, have I?"

"Your second disciple said that a teacher for a day is a father for life, and that it’s the father’s fault when the child doesn’t learn well. If something happens to him, it’s your responsibility," declared the Cultivator from Buddha Country.

After some hesitation, the Transcendance Tribulation Stage Cultivator from Demon Territory joined the chase. Zhu Tian had seen an advertisement in the newspaper saying, "to help Brother Lu find someone," and dispatched him to look for Daoist Buyu.

Although Daoist Buyu was found, it seemed that the other three Transcendance Tribulation Stage Cultivators were not quite in accord with him.

Seeing that he could not escape from the pursuit, Daoist Buyu deliberately turned to fight: "Cultivating through battle is also a manner of cultivation!"

...

As spring turned to autumn, Lu Yang sat cross-legged on the Gold Star, his face breaking into a smile of enjoyment.

Seeing that Lu Yang was suffering quite severely, Yun Zhi asked Chasing Moon True Person to help, employing the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo which allowed Lu Yang to start enjoying the pain. Only then did Lu Yang settle down and continuously absorb the Gold Element Power.

During this period, Lu Yang cultivated assiduously, with exceedingly high demands on himself, allowing himself to relax outside from time to time in his spare moments, such as by accepting invitations to be a judge, stumbling upon murder cases, or going to the mountains with Old Meng, and running into murder cases, or going to the beach and encountering murder cases, or taking a flying boat and finding murder cases...

To save Lu Yang money, Daoist Buyu managed to escape his pursuers, returning to the Sect and regularly publishing stories in Cultivation Monthly, which were widely acclaimed.

Like Ye Zijin, Daoist Buyu also could not stay idle, not staying long in the Sect and continuing to travel outside, but making sure to mail his manuscripts back to the Sect regularly.

Seeing that Seek Dao Sect was publishing a newspaper, other Sects and the Dynasty followed suit. Although they sold well, they were not as appealing as Cultivation Monthly.

Following the formal induction of the Tang Chuanwu siblings into Seek Dao Sect as Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivators, Lu Yang finally experienced a breakthrough in his cultivation.

"Spirit Transformation Late Stage now," he said.

Chapter 1060: 1059: Initial Integration of the Primordial Spirit

Atop the Gold Star, Elder Brother Lu Yang had achieved success in his cultivation and revealed an inscrutable smile.

Being in the later stage of Spirit Transformation meant he was one step closer to passing the tribulation and entering the Void Refinement Stage... Wait, this didn’t seem like something to be happy about

Yun Mengmeng brought over a small stool to sit next to Lu Yang, gleefully eating pastries, unaffected by the Gold Element Qi because she possessed the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

This was her first time in space, and it was quite cold.

There was nothing in space, no food, no fun, just desolation; had the Second-in-command not been cultivating here, she wouldn’t have bothered flying up.

"You’re in the latter stage of Spirit Transformation? Second-in-command is so impressive—here, have a peach," Mengmeng said.

Mengmeng had just taken a bite of her pastry and, seeing the Second-in-command’s successful breakthrough, she wanted to eat another bite but didn’t want to put her pastry down, so she just carried it in her mouth and handed over an Immortal Peach she had just picked from her family’s tree.

Then she brought out Senior Sister Gan Tian’s ancestral guqin she had borrowed, channeled her spiritual essence into the guqin, and a magnificent melody arose. She quickly started clapping to celebrate the Second-in-command reaching the later stage of Spirit Transformation, clapping frantically.

"Thank you," Lu Yang subconsciously took the Immortal Peach and started eating it, then suddenly realized something was wrong.

"Wait, Sister Mengmeng, why are you here?!"

"Oh, I heard you were about to break through, so I waited here to congratulate you."

Lu Yang was deeply touched; he had been cloistered at Gold Star for so long, and Sister Mengmeng had actually waited here for him all this time.

"How long have you been waiting here, Sister Mengmeng?"

"Just arrived."

"..."

Lu Yang drew his Green Peak Sword, the sword intent soaring into the sky. With one swing, he ripped open the void, leaving a spatial rift that wouldn’t close for a long time.

During his cultivation, Lu Yang had continuously absorbed the Gold Element Qi, and now his Sword Qi had reached an unimaginably powerful level.

Lu Yang tossed the Immortal Peach high into the air and lightly nudged the Sword Guard with his thumb. A flash of the sword’s light passed, and the peach fell back into his hand, its skin neatly peeled off while the flesh remained undamaged!

"So impressive!" Yun Mengmeng clapped enthusiastically; she always ended up leaving some flesh on the skin when she peeled fruits herself, "Help me peel one too."

Lu Yang drew his sword a second time, peeled another Immortal Peach for Yun Mengmeng, and her applause grew even more vigorous.

After eating the Immortal Peach, Yun Mengmeng put a floral garland on Lu Yang, adding a ceremonial feel to the occasion.

"Let’s go back, I need to consolidate my realm," said Lu Yang. He was still under attack by the Gold Element Power and influenced by the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo. He was smiling continuously, too happy to calm down and solidify his realm.

Flying back to the Seek Dao Sect, Lu Yang headed straight to the Great Snow Mountain behind Sky Gate Mountain and sought out Chasing Moon True Person.
