"Senior Chasing Moon, can you withdraw the divine ability?" Lu Yang now couldn’t help but slap himself in joy.

At that moment, Chasing Moon True Person was talking with Senior Sister Gan Tian, listening to her experiences in the Demon Territory. While her experiences weren’t as thrilling as Lu Yang’s, they were still quite colorful compared to an ordinary cultivator’s.

Chasing Moon True Person glanced at Lu Yang and asked, "You’ve reached the later stage of Transforming Spirit?"

While speaking, he activated the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo to eliminate the influences.

"Here’s your guqin," Yun Mengmeng handed the ancient guqin back to Senior Sister Gan Tian.

"Junior Brother, you are cultivating faster than me and the Second Senior Brother, perhaps even faster than Master," Senior Sister Gan Tian remarked while playing the guqin, helping Lu Yang to consolidate his realm.

"How about compared to the Eldest Senior Sister?" Lu Yang excitedly asked, feeling better than ever, convinced that even the Eldest Senior Sister, who was in the mid-stage of Transforming Spirit, was no match for him!

"That, I don’t know. The Eldest Senior Sister never mentioned her cultivation speed."

"It seems I’m either faster or slower than the Eldest Senior Sister," Lu Yang was pleased with this response, meaning he had a fifty percent chance of surpassing the Eldest Senior Sister.

Wait, was his way of thinking somehow different from usual? Lu Yang suddenly realized something was off.

Fairy Eternity appeared and explained to Lu Yang, "This is very normal. You’re already at the late Spirit Transformation Stage and will soon enter the Void Refinement Stage before merging with the Invincible Primordial Spirit to ascend to the Body Integration Realm. As second-in-command, your talent is exceptional, and signs of merging have preliminarily appeared, giving you an invincible mindset."

Lu Yang: "..."

Why on earth did I ever decide to make the Unrivaled Pill?

Bai Ye, who replaced Chasing Moon True Person, took out a paper bag from his storage ring: "Elder Brother Lu Yang, here are some petals I just plucked from myself."

Ever since Lu Yang had commented on how delicious the petals were back at the Fairy Palace, Bai Ye remembered and consistently brought petals to Lu Yang after arriving at Seek Dao Sect.

Lu Yang had tried correcting him a few times, hoping Bai Ye wouldn’t call him "Elder Brother Lu Yang," but Bai Ye, being used to it, refused to change, so Lu Yang just let him be.

"Thank you." He popped a petal into his mouth, as fragrantly sweet as ever.

Having eaten petals from the Laurel Tree Ancestor and enjoyed music played by Senior Sister Gan Tian, Lu Yang had solidified his realm without the need for a second retreat.

"Old Meng, I’ve broken through to the late Spirit Transformation Stage. How about you?" Lu Yang went to Meng Jingzhou’s abode, smelling the pungent scent of spice from afar.

Meng Jingzhou stretched out from behind a hot pot, lips and cheeks deathly pale, pouring duck blood into the pot.

"Of course, I beat you to it, you bastard!" Meng Jingzhou responded irritably. Spending his days around the sun, cultivating while bleeding, he progressed swiftly, reaching the late Spirit Transformation Stage before Lu Yang.

The only downside to this method was severe blood loss, necessitating nutritional replenishment.

If he wasn’t so weak right now, he would definitely drag Lu Yang into a fight.

"Come on, I’ll have some too." Meng Jingzhou ate hot pot all the time, but it had been a while for Lu Yang; the last time he had hot pot was when he first met Yun Mengmeng at the Sword Tower.

Lu Yang completely made himself at home in Meng Jingzhou’s abode, sitting down and starting to eat with the utensils he had brought out.

"Hey, leave some for me!" Meng Jingzhou was furious. It was bad enough you were rude, but you even started by stealing my duck blood. Don’t you know I’m anemic right now?

"You’re being stingy. Put some meat in, put some meat in," Lu Yang urged, especially since there was only duck blood in the pot.

Meng Jingzhou stretched out his hand, tore open a space, and brought out a plate of meat, dumping it into the hot pot.

Lu Yang raised an eyebrow, "Space Spell?"

He didn’t remember Old Meng having this trick.

"Jealous, huh? Taught by Vast Sea Ancestral Master," Meng Jingzhou said with a smirk.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord had taught him the space spell while drawing his blood.

"You just broke through, didn’t you? Don’t you need to solidify your realm?" Meng Jingzhou stood up, picking up a piece of ginger that looked like meat and casually placed it in Lu Yang’s bowl as he asked.

"I got lucky this time. After my breakthrough, Sister Mengmeng gave me an Immortal Peach, Junior Sister Bai Ye gave me petals, and Third Senior Sister played the piano," Lu Yang said as he picked up a slice of meat coated with Sichuan pepper and placed it in Meng Jingzhou’s bowl.

Crack.

Meng Jingzhou unwittingly applied too much force and snapped his chopsticks, his face flushing with anger.

Lu Yang watched, baffled, "What’s wrong with you, can’t control your strength after your breakthrough?"

In Meng Jingzhou’s eyes, Lu Yang was purely showing off, and pinpointing that would only make Lu Yang flaunt even more.

"Nothing, nothing, just thinking how lucky you are, kid."

"If I were really that lucky, I wouldn’t have met you."

"To hell with you."

Chapter 1061: 1060

Putting aside the jealousy over Lu Yang’s luck in love affairs, Meng Jingzhou and Lu Yang were still close friends.

"Speaking of which, we haven’t encountered anything lately when we go out," said Meng Jingzhou, picking up a piece of duck blood as he chatted about their outings during leisure time.

"Like when we went to be judges together, played in the mountains, or by the sea..."

Lu Yang remembered that each time they went out, there was always a murder case, with things like locked room mysteries, alibis for everyone involved, and the culprit leaving no clues whatsoever.

Though the culprits were cunning, with unfathomable and unpredictable methods, Lu Yang was no ordinary man; he was the strategist for Fairy Eternity, so how could the culprit possibly escape from him.

Lu Yang simply asked Tang Chuanwu to rewind time, and the truth naturally surfaced, not to mention they could save lives along the way.

The only downside was that Meng Jingzhou couldn’t remember a thing that had happened.

"I told you, the incidents we ran into when we went out were purely coincidental. Look, nothing happened these past few times," he said.

Lu Yang nodded vigorously, feeling that Old Meng was right.

When eating hotpot, Lu Yang dipped meat, vegetables, and tofu skin, while Meng Jingzhou dipped pig’s blood, duck blood, and cow’s blood; there were no conflicts over food, and the atmosphere was harmonious.

Thanks to Lu Yang’s luck in love affairs, Meng Jingzhou left the meal with a glowing, rosy complexion.

"That casual space slicing to grab a plate by Old Meng was so cool," he mused.

Lu Yang saw it with his own eyes and envied him in his heart.

He too wished to wave his sleeves and have a table full of delicious dishes appear before him.

"Little Yang, your Town in Palm can do that," noted Meng Jingzhou.

The Town in Palm had two modes of use: one for combat, turning into a gigantic palm, and another that formed a temporary storage space in the palm of one’s hand.

Lu Yang shook his head firmly, having strict demands for magic skills: "Fairy, the essence of it lies in the act of waving the sleeve. Although it might not seem different to outsiders, it’s not something I can fool myself with."

"Oh," Fairy Eternity nodded, seemingly half-understanding.

While they talked, Lu Yang returned to Sky Gate Mountain.

"Second-in-command, you’re back?" Yun Mengmeng sat on the Emperor’s Chair, legs crossed on the table, waving at Lu Yang. She pulled out a bag of dried potato chips from her sleeve and crunched away.

"Want some?" Yun Mengmeng then took out another packet of potato chips from her sleeve.

"That’s a great idea, lighting up a laurel branch to bake potatoes really does make a difference," Lu Yang remarked.

The baked potato chips were already delicious, and with Lu Yang’s tips, the ones Yun Mengmeng made were even tastier.

"Then give me some, wait a minute—where did you get those potato chips from?" Lu Yang noticed Yun Mengmeng continuously pulling things out from her sleeve as if there were infinite space inside.

Yun Mengmeng bragged with a giggle: "I just learned Universe within the Sleeve from Xiao Zhi. I don’t need to take out snacks from the Storage Ring anymore; I can just reach into my sleeve, it’s so handy."

"Universe within the Sleeve..." Lu Yang’s eyes lit up; this was the spell he wanted!

"Sister Mengmeng, can you teach me?" he asked.

"Sure," said Yun Mengmeng, licking the potato chip crumbs off her fingertips. She clapped her hands and readily agreed.

"I remember you know Space Spells, so it should be fairly simple for you to learn," she noted.

Yun Mengmeng led Lu Yang to the middle of Heaven’s Gate Peak Mountain, where the terrain was relatively flat, making it an ideal place to practice magic skills.

Both sat on small stools, teaching one-on-one.

Yun Mengmeng cleared her throat. Her expression changed, becoming serious—a stark contrast to her usual lively and cheerful demeanor:

"As the saying goes, ’Universe within the Sleeve’ means the sleeve forms its own world, capable of holding everything... How does Xiao Zhi describe it after that, forget it, let’s skip this step."

Yun Mengmeng remembered when she learned this magic skill, Xiao Zhi spent half a day explaining the origin, significance, and combat applications of the skill, which confused her at the time.

She wouldn’t let Second-in-command suffer through the same ordeal, so she decided to teach him directly how to use the magic skill.

Yun Mengmeng returned to her usual demeanor, "Follow me, Heart-Calming Qi Gathering, breathe naturally."

"Recite the incantation: ’Expand Sleeve to Contain Heaven, gather items to oneself, Observing Qi to the Micro, Detecting the space without traces...’"

"Close your eyes, concentrate on the subtle fluctuations around you, feel those ’fluctuations’—that is the fluctuation of space."

Lu Yang’s talent for learning magic skills needs no description, he calmly concentrated his energy, recited the incantation, and in the blink of an eye had completed these two steps. He then dedicated himself to feeling out his surroundings and quickly sensed the space fluctuations all around.

"Sister Mengmeng, I’ve sensed the space fluctuations."

Yun Mengmeng had long heard about Second-in-command’s exceptional talent for magic skills, universally praised by all, and today she saw that it was even more exaggerated than she had imagined

"Not bad, you’re even faster than I was when I learned."

"Maintain this feeling, and follow the rules of spiritual essence circulation and incantation I taught you, imagine your sleeve merging with the space, the sleeve able to hold all things..."

Before long, a whole day had passed, and Lu Yang looked spirited, feeling he had learned enough, "I’ll give it a try."

Universe within the Sleeve, a renowned Great Divine Skill in the realm of space, requires an extremely high talent in Space Spells, and if you don’t possess this talent, even if you are at the Body Integration Realm or Transcendance Tribulation Stage, you still wouldn’t be able to learn it.

For someone like Lu Yang to learn it in just one day, at the Divinity Transformation Stage, no one could surpass this learning speed.

Immortal Fairy also knew this skill, and since Yun Mengmeng had no issues with the steps taught, there was no need for her to interject.

"Go on, try it, try it!" Yun Mengmeng said excitedly, feeling a sense of accomplishment as this was her first time teaching someone.

Yun Mengmeng took out several bags of roasted potato chips and signaled for Lu Yang to gather them with his sleeve.

"Expand Sleeve to Contain Heaven, gather items to oneself... Universe within the Sleeve!"

Lu Yang extended his sleeve, sweeping it over the roasted potato chips on the ground.

Yun Mengmeng eagerly watched the roasted potato chips, and all of a sudden, everything went dark before her eyes, as if even light was sucked into the sleeve, turning this space into a land without light!

"Could it be that I’ve also been taken into Second-in-command’s sleeve?"

Lu Yang was also panicked, realizing he couldn’t see anything either.

Light shone from above, and as they looked up, they saw an immense piece of fabric blocking out the sun, now being lifted by Eldest Senior Sister.

Eldest Senior Sister looked helplessly at her good sister and good Junior Brother, "Which one of you conjured up such a huge piece of clothing that it’s covering half of Sky Gate Mountain?"

She was peaceably sitting on the Emperor’s Chair drinking tea when suddenly it darkened, leading her to think someone had invaded.

Lu Yang: "..."

Yun Mengmeng looked up to Second-in-command with admiration, "I get it now, if the sleeve becomes bigger it can hold everything, so this is the true Universe within the Sleeve!"

...

"Old Meng, can you show me your Dharma Aspect?"

"For what?"

Meng Jingzhou looked puzzled at Lu Yang, especially baffled, yet still he displayed the Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image, immense and awe-inspiring, simply standing there was enough to deter any petty criminals.

"Does your Dharma Aspect lack clothing?"

Chapter 1062: 1061: Law of Heaven and Earth

Meng Jingzhou saw Lu Yang taking out a set of huge clothes, full of question marks.

"Where on earth did you get this set of clothes from, kid?"

"Cough cough, this is the Supreme Treasure Clothing that I’ve diligently cultivated for many days, exhausting countless efforts to refine. Your Dharma Aspect looks indecent, bare-assed, so I specially refined it for your Dharma Aspect to wear."

Actually, it was because Lu Yang found out that the result of ’Universe within the Sleeve’ made the clothes grow bigger, so he kept practicing magic skills to revert the clothes, aiming to master the true ’Universe within the Sleeve’.

However, he didn’t master the true ’Universe within the Sleeve’, and in the end, he found that his clothes, which had grown repeatedly bigger, could no longer shrink back.

Having such a large piece of clothing was of no use to Lu Yang, so he might as well offer a bargain to his good brother.

Meng Jingzhou rolled his eyes, "I’d have to see a ghost to believe your nonsense."

But wearing the clothes wouldn’t hurt.

Meng Jingzhou had the Dharma Aspect wear the clothes, and truth be told, it fit quite well.

Originally imposing, wielding a shield in one hand and a spear in the other, the Dharma Aspect, upon wearing the clothes, gained a refined scholarly air, like a well-read scholar heading into battle.

The Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image surged with terrifying Pure Yang Light Flame on its body, a single strand of which could burn a host of little demons to death, its power terrifying. Ordinary clothes that touched the Dharma Aspect would turn to ash in an instant.

But how could the clothes worn by Lu Yang be ordinary? These garments were impervious to knife and fire, water and flame, unafraid of the Pure Yang Light Flame.

Turns out this was a free gift from Lu Yang, and he was at a loss.

"Not bad," Meng Jingzhou was very satisfied with the current appearance of the Dharma Aspect, much more refined. His previous Dharma Aspect looked like a butcher, which didn’t suit the identity of Young Master Meng at all.

On the way back, the more Lu Yang thought about it, the more something felt off. He had spent so much time learning ’Universe within the Sleeve’, but ended up gaining nothing, instead letting Old Meng benefit.

"Should I also make myself bigger? Fairy, do you know how to enlarge one’s body?"

Recalling his encounters with High-Level Cultivators in battle, they usually moved mountains and seas, and fought by enlarging themselves. Learning how to enlarge his own body would be useful for future battles.

"Then learn ’Law of Heaven and Earth’."

’Law of Heaven and Earth’, a Divine Ability even more renowned than ’Universe within the Sleeve’. Not many Integration Period Cultivators could learn it. Lu Yang, only at the Spirit Transformation Late Stage, was even less likely to learn it, but considering Lu Yang’s exceptional talent, the Immortal Fairy thought it wouldn’t hurt to try.

Anyway, if he practiced it incorrectly, it had nothing to do with her.

"Then let’s learn this!"

Lu Yang returned to the place where he had previously learned ’Universe within the Sleeve’, with the Immortal Fairy personally instructing him.

"’Law of Heaven and Earth,’ as the name suggests, is about imitating the power of ’Heaven’ and ’Earth’, achieving a state of integration with the natural laws of heaven and earth, thereby making the body immensely gigantic, grand like heaven and earth!"

"You need to form the Heaven-Earth seal in your heart, the hand gesture is like this..."

"Little Yang, calm your mind, feel the heaven and the earth."

The reason ’Law of Heaven and Earth’ is difficult to learn is crucially because there are no shortcuts; it relies solely on enlightenment.

Great Cultivators who mastered ’Law of Heaven and Earth’ usually specially refine a huge set of clothes to wear after enlarging. Lu Yang had no such concerns.

Night fell, cold silvery rays scattered over the lawn, reflecting a peaceful and tranquil scene.

Lu Yang sat cross-legged on the ground, assuming the Five Centers Toward Heaven position, took a deep breath, and exhaled slowly, as if at that moment, all distractions were excluded, leaving only a dialogue with heaven and earth.

A breeze passed by, mixed with Spiritual Energy, bringing a fresh scent from distant mountains and forests, as if the Spiritual Energy was responding to his call.

Lu Yang also felt the pulse of the earth, as if synchronizing with his own heartbeat, solid and powerful, containing endless strength.

Three days went by in a blink, Lu Yang neither ate nor drank nor slept, completely merging with heaven and earth.

Noon sunlight streamed through the clouds onto Lu Yang’s cheeks; he opened his eyes and stood up, completely rejuvenated, the blend with heaven and earth was an unprecedented experience that left him deeply moved.

Lu Yang pointed at the sky, trying to imitate the Worldly Venerable’s posture of ’above all under heaven, I alone am honored’.

"Law of Heaven and Earth!"

Lu Yang disappeared, leaving only his clothes on the ground.

He felt like a Heavenly God high above, looking down on all creatures, who seemed like mere ants in his eyes.

"Is this what becoming a giant feels like? Indeed, it’s a completely different sensation."

Immortal Fairy flew up to the sky to find Lu Yang.

"Fairy, I succeeded!" Lu Yang said excitedly, "It wasn’t easy, after studying magic for so long, I’ve finally gotten it right once."

Immortal Fairy scratched her head, unsure how to describe Little Yang’s condition.

Immortal Fairy conjured a mirror with a spell, Lu Yang looked into the mirror, but could not see himself.

Immortal Fairy finally knew how to describe Little Yang’s condition, "Second-in-command, you seem to have turned into spiritual energy."

"!"

A cluster of spiritual energy was floating in the sky, and it was Lu Yang who had transformed.

Frightened, Lu Yang hastily dispelled the magic skill, reverted to his naked human form, and quickly flew back to get dressed.

"I was scared to death, I thought I was going to die," Lu Yang broke out in cold sweat, as if he had just taken a walk past the gates of hell.

For a cultivator to turn into spiritual energy, that’s called transforming the path and it happens only after one dies.

"It must have been a mistake in the steps of the magic skill I used just now. I’ll try it again."

Cultivating magic skills is never absolutely safe, but as a cultivator, as a seeker of the path, Lu Yang would not let this minor incident stop him.

"Law of Heaven and Earth!"

Unlike the last time, Lu Yang felt as if he had merged with the earth, which was very reassuring.

Immortal Fairy watched from the side, she saw Little Yang disappear and in his place, a heap of soil appeared.

"So, was it Law of Heaven just now, and now it’s Earth?"

Immortal Fairy hissed, Little Yang really imitated heaven and earth thoroughly.

Upon learning the truth, Lu Yang tried several times, only to find that he either turned into the sky or the earth, but never back into a human.

Lu Yang lay on the ground, spread-eagled, staring blankly at the sky, wondering if becoming a giant was really that difficult.

Just as Immortal Fairy was about to step forward and comfort Lu Yang, he suddenly sat up.

"Hey, I’ve got an idea for a solution!"

Then, using the Taotie Clan’s innate divine ability "Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth," Lu Yang crazily absorbed spiritual energy, expanding his own size.

"Release!"

Lu Yang dispelled the magic, reverting to his human shape; this time, he had transformed into a giant!

"Haha, I knew this would work!"

Thrilled, Lu Yang jumped up. His idea was right, using Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth to absorb spiritual energy would transform him, consuming soil when he became soil, till transforming back into a giant human form!

Atop Sky Gate Mountain, a naked giant hopped around, completely unbothered by his condition.

...

"Old Meng, show your Dharma Aspect, let’s have a one-on-one duel."

After so many days, Meng Jingzhou looked healthy and his strength had recovered to normal; it wasn’t bullying for Lu Yang to seek him out now.

"You think you can beat my Dharma Aspect?" Meng Jingzhou scoffed with disdain. He had been absorbing Sun Power from the sun every day, and his Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image had evolved to a terrifying level.

"If you’re scared, then don’t fight."

"Who’s scared of you?" Upon hearing this, Meng Jingzhou showed no courtesy, immediately displaying his Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image, and the majestic Pure Yang Divine Man protected him.

"Law of Heaven and Earth!"

Lu Yang turned into a small hill.

"Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth!"

Lu Yang frenziedly devoured the soil at the entrance to Meng Jingzhou’s abode.

"Release!"

Lu Yang dispelled the Law of Heaven and Earth.

"Universe within the Sleeve!"

Lu Yang made his clothes grow larger.

The enormous Lu Yang appeared in front of Meng Jingzhou, matching the height of the Dharma Aspect.

Meng Jingzhou looked at his good brother with a strange expression.

"Why are there so many steps for you to get big?"

Chapter 1063: 1062: The Flaws of the Law of Heaven and Earth and Ways to Compensate

"It’s just you who talks too much!"

Lu Yang, enraged, thought what was wrong with having more steps, even additional steps were part of the Law of Heaven and Earth!

After he transformed into a several-hundred-foot-tall giant, his voice also changed, booming like thunder, echoing in the mountain ravine.

Lu Yang drew out the Green Peak Sword, an Immortal Sword, which could change size along with Lu Yang.

Meng Jingzhou didn’t hesitate either, his Dharma Aspect’s shields and spears collided, creating ripples through space, while the Green Peak Sword emitted a sharp, resonating hum.

The two starkly different sounds collided in the middle of the battlefield, causing a massive explosion, shaking the entire space!

The Dharma Aspect’s eyes shot out foot-thick beams of red light, akin to magma; even before approaching Lu Yang, he could feel the raging power within the beams.

"Open!"

With a swing of the Green Peak Sword, he split the red beams in two, which skimmed past on either side of him, instantly reducing the white sandalwood forest next to Meng Jingzhou’s abode to ashes, carving two deep trenches in the ground that glowed with molten glass.

The Dharma Aspect strode forward, each step leaving a ball of flame on the ground. With a forceful stomp, the earth cracked, and its massive form leaped into the air.

With the sky above its head and the earth beneath its feet, the Pure Yang Flames swept across the sky, staining it crimson as if the heavens were bleeding.

The spear tore through the sky, carrying a torrent of fire as it collided with the Green Peak Sword, the sound of metal clashing like sudden thunder, followed by another shield whistling towards Lu Yang’s ribs.

Lu Yang flicked his left hand, and the pitch-black Inheriting Shadow Sword appeared, blocking the fierce blow.

With a flick of his wrist, he sent out a barrage of sword shadows, each strong enough to level mountains, forcing Meng Jingzhou to pay close attention.

"Cease War Skill!"

Meng Jingzhou’s method of using the shield and spear was no longer chaotic as before; it had become rhythmic.

The Cease War Skill was a combat technique created for Meng Jingzhou by Meng Junzi.

Meng Junzi emerged as a renowned fighter at the end of the Great Yu Late Period, with countless battles both large and small, his combat experience immensely rich. He developed the Cease War Skill for Meng Jingzhou’s Dharma Aspect, a technique to stop fighting with fighting, to cease bloodshed with bloodshed!

A raging storm of spiritual essence swept through, and the actions of the two giants at the center of the battlefield could reshape the terrain. The entrance to Meng Jingzhou’s abode had been disfigured beyond recognition; no one coming here would be able to tell it was Meng Jingzhou’s abode!

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand!"

Countless blades suddenly sprouted beneath Lu Yang’s feet, sticking out from the ground haphazardly like small hills, restricting Meng Jingzhou’s movements.

"Big Sun Magma Arrow!"

The massive form of the Dharma Aspect oozed boiling magma that bubbled, steamed the land, scorched the space, and was so hot in temperature that cultivators of the same level dare not refine such heated spiritual essence for fear of burning their meridians.

This magma hovered in the air, spinning, transforming into a barrage of arrows, shooting out violently!

"Let them come!" Lu Yang’s eyes lit up, knowing Old Meng still had interesting moves up his sleeve.

Blades gathered in front of Lu Yang, forming a shield that sliced through the molten arrow feathers as they made contact, shattering them with the released Sword Qi.

Meng Jingzhou seized the opportunity to charge with his shield raised; the two shields collided and both were shattered by the impact.

Both were flung backward by the other’s force; Meng Jingzhou steadied himself first, taking advantage of Lu Yang’s unsteady stance to rush towards him once again!

The Dharma Aspect leaned in, pushing past Lu Yang’s guards; the two were so close that Lu Yang was forced to abandon using his sword and engage in a physical duel.

Lu Yang’s punch landed on Meng Jingzhou’s Dharma Aspect, finding it much harder than his own flesh and unable to break its defense. When the Dharma Aspect hit Lu Yang, it caused him acute pain.

Lu Yang used Law of Heaven and Earth to simply enlarge his body, whereas Meng Jingzhou’s Dharma Aspect was based on his own flesh, further enhanced. Meng Jingzhou’s physical body was already slightly stronger than Lu Yang’s, and now, augmented by the Dharma Aspect, this led to Lu Yang being at a disadvantage when it came purely to strength and defense.

"How about that, Old Lu? You’re better than me with weapons, but in close combat, you’re no match for me," Meng Jingzhou laughed heartily, knowing that Old Lu had yet to use the devious Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array, signifying that Lu Yang was testing for flaws in the Law of Heaven and Earth.

The flaw had been exposed, and it was time to finish this!

"Earth-Shaking Eight Styles!"

The Dharma Aspect exhibited the Earth-Shaking Eight Styles that could be described as capable of destroying heaven and earth, causing Lu Yang to retreat step by step. Even if Lu Yang could block it, his physical strength was insufficient, so blocking was futile.

"Lacking defensive techniques..."

Lu Yang recalled the techniques he had learned, most of which were offensive, and very few were defensive.

"Seems like there really is one!"

"Old Meng, don’t think you’re amazing just because your Dharma Aspect is tough. I also know defensive techniques, Sixteen-Foot Golden Body!"

A sixteen-foot golden body suddenly materialized, bringing a sudden silence to the fierce battle between the two.

"Hahaha—" Meng Jingzhou couldn’t hold back and burst out laughing until he was wheezing for breath, barely able to maintain his Dharma Aspect.

Lu Yang’s eye twitched. He had transformed to several hundred feet tall using the Law of Heaven and Earth, and the Sixteen-Foot Golden Body was not even the size of his palm, making its high defensive power seemingly useless.

"I’ve got an idea!" A flash of brilliance struck Lu Yang.

"Sixteen-Foot Golden Body, Sixteen-Foot Golden Body, Sixteen-Foot Golden Body..."

In one go, Lu Yang created a multitude of Sixteen-Foot Golden Bodies, sticking them onto his body as armor, covering him from head to toe, securely guarded.

Lu Yang had grown large by swallowing the earth and Spiritual Energy. The enormous size of his Spiritual Energy Pool had increased several times over, allowing him to repeatedly cast the Sixteen-Foot Golden Body.

"How’s that?" Lu Yang flaunted his suit of armor. This time, his defense was definitely strong enough.

Meng Jingzhou’s eye twitched. Old Lu was covered in little figures, looking stranger by the minute. Even he felt it was excessively weird, to the point where calling Old Lu a giant figure of the Demon Path wouldn’t seem out of place.

"Earth-Shaking Eight Styles!"

Despite the ridiculous appearance, the defense was genuinely solid. As Meng Jingzhou struck Lu Yang with the Earth-Shaking Eight Styles, the Sixteen-Foot Golden Bodies absorbed most of the force, and Lu Yang himself was unharmed.

"Hiss, it really does seem to work." Meng Jingzhou hadn’t expected the defense provided by the Sixteen-Foot Golden Bodies used as armor to be so strong; it was almost outrageous.

"With this, the Law of Heaven and Earth is now the complete version," Lu Yang he-he’d with a smile, finding that testing out techniques with Old Meng was indeed a way to identify and address shortcomings.

"My White Sandalwood Forest! What are you two brats doing, is this a place for you to duel?"

The Third Elder noticed the commotion and flew into a rage. The power of the two young cultivators’ clash was comparable to a Body Integration Realm duel, leaving the land and forest completely devastated!

The ground, scorched by intense heat, had even turned into glass, not to mention the trees, which had long become charcoal ash.

Our Sect has so many open spaces, and you two just had to duel here, didn’t you?

Especially this forest; it was his favorite White Sandalwood Forest, which he had carefully tended for many years, and now it was all destroyed in a blaze!

True, this was Meng Jingzhou’s Abode, but this mountain also belonged to the Third Elder.

In his furious state, the Third Elder didn’t hold back and sent one punch each their way, hammering giant Lu Yang and the Dharma Aspect Meng Jingzhou into the ground, leaving only their heads exposed.

The Third Elder glared menacingly at Meng Jingzhou: "I need to teach you a lesson, or are you trying to rebel against the heavens? You go do volunteer work at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect for two months before coming back!"

The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect in Great Xia was specifically responsible for dealing with the aftermath of cultivators’ duels and restoring the landscape.

Meng Jingzhou nodded repeatedly.

Lu Yang was a disciple of Daoist Buyu, so the Third Elder couldn’t do much about him.

After the Third Elder left, the two crawled out of the dirt, looking disheveled and dusty.

"It’s all your fault for coming over and challenging me for no reason."

"It’s not such a big deal, it’s just two months of learning at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. I’ll go with you."

Chapter 1064: 1063: Delayed a Bit on the Way

Lu Yang’s conscience was not yet lost. He knew that Old Meng was punished because he had proposed a duel, and that he bore some responsibility for the destruction of the White Sandalwood Forest.

Therefore, he actively offered to also go to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to perform two months of voluntary labor.

"That sounds more like it."

Before going to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, the two had to first restore the ruins at the entrance of Meng Jingzhou’s abode back to their original state.

From afar, the area around Body Refining Peak looked like it had been chewed up by a dog, completely bare, and indeed quite an eyesore.

Lu Yang had never thought about using Tang Chuanwu’s ability to reverse time. He had asked Tang Chuanwu to reverse time during the murder case in order to save people and catch the murderer.

This time was different. Lu Yang certainly couldn’t ask Tang Chuanwu to sacrifice himself just because he had made a mistake. Even if Tang Chuanwu could be revived, dying once was not a pleasant experience.

Lu Yang had realized early on that if he resorted to asking Tang Chuanwu to sacrifice himself whenever he faced an issue, he would easily become indifferent to Tang Chuanwu’s life, treating him as nothing more than a tool.

The two of them got to work together. After shaving off several layers of the ground, they finally cleared away all of the scorched earth and glass. Then they shoveled some soil from the sect’s grasslands, filling in the large pits.

"What about the White Sandalwood Forest, do we use the Tree Planting Spell?"

Lu Yang was also somewhat troubled. After pondering for a moment, he suggested, "Since the White Sandalwood Forest was cultivated by the Third Elder, if you simply use the Tree Planting Spell to restore it, that would effectively erase all traces of the Third Elder’s efforts. It would be best for us to plant White Sandalwood tree seeds and let the forest grow naturally."

"That’s true."

The two bought a large bag of White Sandalwood tree seeds from the Task Hall. Lu Yang used his sword to dig holes, and Meng Jingzhou dug with his hands. Huffing and puffing, they finally finished the job.

I have to say, doing this does bring a sense of accomplishment.

...

"We’ve finally arrived at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect." Lu Yang stretched out mightily. This journey had been exhausting, but fortunately, it had been safe and without any alarming incidents.

Initially, he and Old Meng thought it made sense for them, as cultivators in the Spirit Transformation Late Stage, not to need a flying boat—they could simply fly directly to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

Then, on their way, they happened upon a Secret Realm that only opened once a century. The Secret Realm specified that only Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators could enter. Going with the thought that since they had already come this far, they entered and consecutively passed through three trials. Finally, at the end of the third trial, they encountered the Secret Realm Spirit.

Dissatisfied with their method of passing the trials, it battled with them for hundreds of rounds. After realizing it couldn’t defeat the two, it resorted to telling them the origin of the Secret Realm to distract them and used its authority over the Secret Realm to expel them, forbidding them from ever entering this Secret Realm again.

After being randomly transported out by the Secret Realm Spirit, they found themselves in the forbidden area of a hermit family, who had settled there for generations to suppress an Evil Demon. To maintain the seal, the hermit family had been sacrificing young men and women every hundred years for over a hundred thousand years to extend the life of the Array Method with their blood.

The Evil Demon was the malevolent spirit of a Transcendance Tribulation Stage being from long ago. The hermit family were descendants of this Transcendance Tribulation Stage individual.

After Lu Yang and his companion saved the young men and women who were about to be sacrificed, and after battling the Evil Demon for hundreds of rounds, they decided to report it to the government office, allowing the Governor of Youzhou to send people to deal with it.

After the governor dealt with the Evil Demon, he recommended that the hermit family not bear their burdens alone and commended Lu Yang and his companion for their actions.

After the commendation, Lu Yang sensed that someone was using the small wooden sword he had gifted. Following the trace of the Sword Qi, he found the Fu Ling sisters that had been born by the Twin River.

In Chunjiang City, after dealing with the Void Venerate and the Nightmare Supreme, Lu Yang had given the Fu Ling sisters a small wooden sword for protection. Now, they had encountered troublemakers and had been forced to use the small wooden sword to fend off the aggressors.

They claimed that the sword was a gift from Lu Yang, but the evildoers, while afraid of Lu Yang’s reputation, were somewhat skeptical of the sword’s origin. They boldly continued to harass the Fu Ling sisters until Lu Yang, appearing at the critical moment, rescued them and punished the evildoers, gifting the sisters a new small wooden sword.

"Next time we go out, let’s just take a flying boat. It might be slower for traveling, but it’s less likely to encounter problems," Meng Jingzhou suggested.

"We could try that," Lu Yang nodded in agreement, remembering his experiences traveling by flying boat where even if something happened, it didn’t affect the journey.

"The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect really knows how to pick a location." Meng Jingzhou also breathed a sigh of relief upon reaching their destination, finding time to comment and feeling the air was indeed fresher here.

The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect is nestled deep within the mountains, beside a river, surrounded by a beautiful environment... that is, if the mountains stay still.

The existence of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect is quite special; as a sect under the rule of the Dynasty, it doesn’t have a hierarchical classification.

They are mainly responsible for the aftermath of cultivator battles, restoring maps, organizing mountain ranges, planting trees, and so forth. Therefore, to join this sect, one must at least have Earth Attribute or Wood Attribute in their Spirit Root.

From outside the sect, the two saw mountains moving to and fro, which was the sect disciples practicing the Mountain Shifting Skill.

"Greetings, Daoist friend, we are disciples from the Seek Dao Sect. Please deliver this letter to your sect leader; we have a matter to discuss."

Representing the face of the Seek Dao Sect while traveling abroad, the two rushed over, looking dusty and disheveled. After tidying up their appearance, they arrived at the entrance of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect and handed over the letter of introduction from the Third Elder to the gatekeeping disciple.

"Lu Yang? Meng Jingzhou?" The gatekeeping disciple recognized their identities at a glance and was thrilled, his face flushed with excitement.

"Did you also destroy the landscape in battle and seek our help to restore it?"

That’s why everyone comes to their sect.

Lu Yang said solemnly, "We’ve heard that your sect is responsible for the aftermath of cultivator battles, which is very arduous, often resulting in a shortage of manpower. After learning about this, my brother and I decided to contribute our modest efforts and share some of the burden for your sect."

Meng Jingzhou said nothing but nodded solemnly.

Surely, the two were just as the storytelling described them, full of integrity. The gatekeeping disciple grew even more respectful.

"I will notify the sect leader right away."

Soon, the gatekeeping disciple returned and invited the two to enter the sect and meet the sect leader.

The sect leader of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was named Kong Liansheng, a middle-aged man with a long beard and a scholarly demeanor.

Sect Master Kong sat in a grandmaster’s chair, with the introduction letter placed to the side.

"We greet Sect Master Kong."

Sect Master Kong stood up, stroking his beard and smiling warmly as he looked at the two, his eyes full of appreciation.

"Indeed, famous at a young age, young heroes indeed."

"Third Zhang said in his letter that he hopes you two can stay in our sect for two months to help us out. This isn’t the first time Third Zhang has sent people to me. What did you two do to provoke him?"

Meng Jingzhou owned up to their mistake honestly, "In a fight between us, we accidentally destroyed his White Sandalwood Forest."

Upon hearing this, Sect Master Kong laughed heartily, "So, it was the destruction of the White Sandalwood Forest that got you sent here. Everyone has their youthful impetuosity, understandable, understandable."

Sect Master Kong seemed to recall something and waved the introductory letter, "Speaking of which, the letter mentioned you should have arrived in early July. Why did it take until mid-August for you to get here?"

"We were delayed by some minor issues on the way."

Chapter 1065: 1064: Drawing the Scene Based on the Map

Although Sect Master Kong wanted to know what the "few small matters" mentioned by the two were, he was not a cultivator with a heavy sense of curiosity, and he did not inquire further.

"So are you two planning to stay in our sect for two months, and after two months, I just send a letter back with Third Zhang saying you two have been doing quite well here, or do you want to do something else?"

Sect Master Kong wanted to know the real thoughts of Lu Yang and his companion. They were not the first batch of disciples sent to their sect. Besides the Seek Dao Sect, the other Four Great Immortal Sects, as well as Super-grade and First-Grade sects, all had a custom of sending disciples who had made mistakes to undertake obligatory labor here.

Among them, some disciples accepted their punishment willingly, while others worked reluctantly. For the latter, Sect Master Kong found it troublesome to make arrangements and simply let them stay at the sect until their time was up and then return on their own.

Meng Jingzhou bowed respectfully and said earnestly, "Since we have made mistakes, we should be punished, and this is also a rare experience. My brother and I are willing to make some contributions to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect."

"Not bad," Sect Master Kong nodded, appreciating that the two not only had extraordinary cultivation talents but also demonstrated commendable character. They were indeed rare good seeds.

At such a young age, they had already cultivated to the Spirit Transformation Late Stage, unmatched in the world.

"How much do you two know about our sect?"

At this point, Lu Yang and his companion exchanged glances, their eyes revealing some embarrassment—they knew very little about the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

Seeing their expressions, Sect Master Kong understood what they were thinking and smiled, easing the awkward atmosphere: "Very few cultivators know about our sect, so it’s normal not to be familiar with it."

"Despite our sect’s lack of fame, it’s actually very useful for cultivators. Let me tell you a few things, and then you’ll understand the importance of our sect."

"During the celebration in Qingzhou, it was unfortunately disrupted by the Heavenly Calamity Sovereign, and several mountains were flattened or even vanished during the battle. It took our sect a great deal of effort to restore those mountains to their original forms."

"When the sixth Emperor Yu, Wu Youdao, attacked Hanshui City in the Wild State and faced Heavenly Venerate Dou from the Ancient Heavenly Court, he was defeated and retreated. Some houses in Hanshui City were damaged, and the area outside the city was destroyed beyond recognition. Today, Hanshui City has been restored to its original state, which is the result of our sect’s hard work."

"There’s also Chunjiang City, which was pulled into the Void Realm by the Void Venerate. Although the city itself was unharmed, the citizens who witnessed the battle between the Semi-Immortals were terrified, and it was our sect that provided psychological counseling to comfort them."

"Take the Immortal Battle in the Imperial City as another example. When Meng Junzi fought the Great Yu National Preceptor, many landmark buildings in the Imperial City were reduced to ruins, riddled with scars. It was still our sect that acted to give the Imperial City a new lease of life, restoring it to its original condition."

"Then there’s the Western Paradise Temple that was destroyed by an avatar of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and still has not been restored. If our sect took on the task, the temple would have been fixed long ago."

Sect Master Kong listed the major events that the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had handled over the years, with a tone inevitably filled with some pride.

As Lu Yang and his companion listened to the introduction, they nodded and gained a new understanding of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. They were eyewitnesses to these great battles and knew how difficult it was to deal with the aftermath. The sect truly had its own unique strengths.

In the midst of their conversation, a young man walked in and, seeing Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, showed a slightly surprised expression but did not say much. Instead, he focused on the matter at hand: "Master, a task commission has arrived."

"What’s the content?"

"It’s a duel arranged by two Divine Transformation Late Stage cultivators in three days at the Qingniu Mountain Range. They hope that our sect will restore the appearance of the Qingniu Mountains after the battle is over."

The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, in addition to fulfilling tasks assigned by the dynasty, also took responsibility for the cleanup after battles between ordinary cultivators.

The latter was divided into two scenarios: either the sect would be commissioned after the battle, or it would be commissioned before the battle. The fee for the second scenario was lower than the first.

After all, the first scenario required the sect to find the pre-battle state through other channels, while in the second, they could survey the area in advance, saving a lot of trouble.

"In three days, then," Sect Master Kong looked at Lu Yang and his companion. "That would be a good opportunity to familiarize you two with our sect’s workflow."

"Master, are these two Fellow Daoist Lu Yang and Fellow Daoist Meng Jingzhou?" the young man asked.

Nowadays, besides those cultivators who are in constant seclusion, there’s no one under heaven who doesn’t recognize Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

"Right, I haven’t introduced you two to each other yet."

"This is my eldest disciple Fang Jin, who has just entered the Body Integration Realm and has already mastered the Law of Heaven and Earth."

Sect Master Kong stroked his beard with a smile, very satisfied with his big disciple. It was important to know that a divine ability like the Law of Heaven and Earth couldn’t be learned by ninety percent of cultivators in the late Body Integration stage, yet Fang Jin had mastered it in the early stage of Body Integration, which spoke volumes about his talent.

In the future, there would be no objections to him taking over the position of Sect Leader.

"Greetings to Elder Brother Fang Jin."

"Junior Brother Lu, Junior Brother Meng, I’ve heard about you from Elder Brother Dai. He always praises you for your upright character, courage, and righteousness, and for punishing the evil and promoting the good. He often thinks of handing over the position of Task Hall Master to you both right now!"

Lu Yang’s eyes twitched. Was Elder Brother Dai praising them? It didn’t quite feel like it.

"Fang Jin, take these two around our sect. They will be staying with us and helping out for the next two months."

"Yes."

The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was vast, encompassing various terrains. It was a land specially designated by the dynasty to facilitate the disciples’ practice of the art of mountain moving and sea filling. The sect also featured a variety of architectural styles, and repairing these structures was likewise one of their tasks.

Walking through the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect felt like travelling across all of Great Xia, witnessing all sorts of cultures and customs.

Three days later, to show his importance, Sect Master Kong personally led a team, including Lu Yang and the others, to the Qingniu Mountain Range.

Along the way, all the disciples were curiously whispering about Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou standing to the left and right of Sect Master Kong. They couldn’t believe they were witnessing these two legendary figures in person.

What was even more significant was that these two would be staying in the sect for two months, so in the future, they could proudly tell their peers that they had met Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

Once the group arrived at the Qingniu Mountain Range and the two Divine Transformation Stage cultivators had yet to arrive, everyone followed the predetermined arrangements, dividing the tasks and cooperating in an organized manner, and started their investigation, taking out Recording Orbs to map the scene.

"With the Qiongqi Clan’s Recording Orbs, this job is so much easier."

"Yeah, it’s said that the Recording Orb comes from an ancestor of the Qiongqi Clan, quite remarkable indeed."

The disciples of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect couldn’t stop praising the Recording Orb; mapping the scene used to be a very arduous task.

Fairy Immortality looked at the bustling scene with some emotion, "You all are drawing maps based on the scene now, aren’t you? Back in our day, we used to alter the landscape according to the map."

"...Altering the landscape according to the map?" Lu Yang found this concept strange.

"Back then, the Yingtian Immortal said he felt the terrain wasn’t rugged and imposing enough for them. The five of us became immortals, and with countless Semi-Immortals and Transcendance Tribulation cultivators, it was surely a vibrant era to come."

"But the current terrain didn’t match their Cultivation Levels. The mountains were too low, the seas too shallow, the trees too short."

"The Time Immortal and the other two agreed with the Yingtian Immortal’s view. The four of them devised a brand new map and, according to it, moved various races to habitable areas and then used swords and axes to change the landscape."

"Fairy, what you mean is ’radical reform.’"

"Oh, and then they used great swords and broad axes to change the terrain, creating mountains that reached into the universe and oceans that delved deep into the earth’s heart."

"They even moved several veins of top-grade spirit stones to a planet, claiming that it could give birth to life forms composed of top-grade spirit stones."

"The Twenty-Eight Constellations, the Beidou Seven Stars, were also shaped by the Yingtian Immortal and the others moving the stars into formation."

Chapter 1066: 1065: Immortal Axe’s Divine Craftsmanship

Originally, Lu Yang felt the starry sky was vast and the universe mysterious, and he lamented that cultivators of the box world could not use the power of the stars.

Now, after listening to Fairy Eternity’s description, he suddenly felt that the Beidou Seven Stars were nothing special.

"Are the Yingtian Immortals just idling?" Lu Yang couldn’t help but speak his mind, "If you four really have nothing better to do, why not ponder on how to ascend to transcendence?"

"Not at all, they are very busy. Every day, if they aren’t giving lectures, they are altering the landscape. I’ve invited them to my home for dinner, and they never come, unlike Little Ling Lianyi and her company, who are quiet and always make time to attend,"

Lu Yang felt it was understandable that Ying Tian and his fellows were busy.

"Once the landscape is all set up, cultivators in duels will be extremely cautious, fearing they might ruin the hard work of the four Yingtian Immortals."

In reality, Sect Master Kong, looking over his bustling disciples shaping mountains and rivers, couldn’t help but remark emotionally.

"Little Lu, Little Meng, look at this mountain range, how beautiful it is. This mountain resembles a bull’s head, here is the body, and that one resembles its tail, hence it’s called Qingniu Mountain Range."

"Every time I see such scenes, I marvel at nature’s incomparable craftsmanship, capable of creating such bizarre and beautiful landscapes."

"This is also why I chose to join the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect in the first place. I don’t want to see cultivators destroying nature’s masterpieces with their own hands."

Lu Yang, standing behind Sect Master Kong, hesitated to speak. He wanted to say that these were not the works of nature but those of the Four Ancient Immortals, yet he worried that the truth would break Sect Master Kong’s heart.

Even Elder Brother Dai, under a thousand years old, couldn’t bear a few words of exaggeration, so what about Sect Master Kong who was over two thousand years old? Perhaps he was also somewhat sensitive?

"It’s not nature’s craftsmanship; it’s the craftsmanship of immortals. It was all carved out by Yingtian Immortal with his axe,"

Immortal Fairy thought Sect Master Kong was really uneducated, even misusing idioms; at least, Little Yang was literate.

For example, she just mentioned an axe and didn’t specify which kind, yet Little Yang knew it was a broad axe.

In contrast, this Mr. Kong, who appeared scholarly and full of ink, showed his ignorance the moment he spoke. Compared to Little Yang, the difference was clear.

The preparation work was completed, and two cultivators in the Spirit Transformation Late Stage finally arrived at the Qingniu Mountain Range.

On seeing Sect Master Kong, they were stunned first at why such a high-level cultivator would come here, but then, realizing the reason, they were elated.

To think that their reputation was so great that even Sect Master Kong came specially to watch their battle!

"Xiao Jin, what’s the background of the two fighters in this battle?" Sect Master Kong called over his chief disciple, Fang Jin, to ask.

"These two are old ancestors from a nearby influential family of a thousand years, one named Song Junhao, the other named Ning Zihao. When they were young, they teamed up and made a name for themselves, known as the ’Twin Talents.’

"Now, people respectfully call them `Twin Senior Talents`."

Sect Master Kong hesitated for a moment, "Are you sure that’s a term of respect?"

"At least it is locally."

"Alright then."

Sect Master Kong didn’t say much else. He waved his hand, signaling everyone to stand back. They were here to tidy up after the battle and didn’t want to interfere with the Twin Senior Talents’ fight.

Knowing that Sect Master Kong was present, the Twin Senior Talents seemed to revert back to their youthful, spirited selves, their cultivation techniques operating faster than usual.

"Old Song, make your move!"

"Then I won’t hold back—Hidden Dragon Do Not Use!"

"Green Wave Technique!"

Two veteran Cultivators at the late stage of Spirit Transformation took action in an extraordinary manner, as a earth Dragon burrowed out from below the ground, issuing bursts of dragon roars while the lake water churned endlessly.

Perhaps it was due to the stimulating presence of Sect Master Kong, both felt an unprecedentedly good condition, their minds more active than before, producing moves that effortlessly countered the opponent’s, each move concealing layers of deep calculations, strategically poised for victory, fully revealing their cunning!

The magic artifacts they brought out each had their wondrous uses, capable of producing unexpected effects at critical moments.

The two battled for a hundred rounds, fighting vigorously and passionately, yet still no victor was decided!

As spectators, Lu Yang and another stealthily yawned— the duel of the Twin Senior Talents had nothing new to offer, lacking enough power; even their battles at the Nascent Soul Late Stage were more impressive.

Finally, during the three-hundredth round, Song Junhao had a breakthrough in his mind state at a critical moment, touched the threshold of the Divinity Transformation Stage, reaching the Half-step Spirit Transformation Stage.

"I lost," Ning Zihao admitted defeat despondently.

Song Junhao said coldly, "According to the agreement, your Ning Family will cover the repair costs of this battle."

"Ah—" Ning Zihao sighed heavily. This would be a heavy financial blow.

The Twin Senior Talents wanted to ask Sect Master Kong for his comments on the battle, but after looking around, they found no one from the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect and had to give up and return to their respective families.

After the battle, the members of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect emerged from the shadows, having hidden to avoid interfering with the fight.

The Qingniu Mountain Range was a sight of devastation, mountaintops shaved flat, lake water flowing backward, countless trees toppled—if anyone familiar with Qingniu Mountain Range were here, they wouldn’t recognize it.

This highlighted the importance of the disciples from the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. They took out Recording Orbs, reconstructing the mountain range to its state before the battle.

Some disciples lifted up the lake water that had seeped into the soil with their hands and returned it to the lake, placing back fish and shrimp affected by the battle into the water as well, replenishing with fry where necessary as some were killed during the fight.

Another disciples, carrying shovels, dug up broken tree roots, then pinched tree seeds, flicking them into the prepared holes. Under the influence of the "Tree-Planting Sutra," the seeds grew swiftly into towering trees, the authentic Tree Planting Spell filling Lu Yang with envy.

Such proficiency clearly showed mastery of the Tree Planting Spell.

Sect Master Kong, noticing the unusual look in Lu Yang’s eyes, asked with a smile, "What, would you like to give it a try?"

"What would I need to do?"

Sect Master Kong pointed to a mountain that had moved more than ten meters and said, "Sword Cultivators excel at Sword Flight and are noted for object control; you can try controlling that mountain and move it back to its original place."

"Actually, the best way would be to use the Law of Heaven and Earth or Dharma Aspect, but you are only at the late stage of Spirit Transformation, not yet time to master these Supreme Great Divine Abilities..."

Then, Sect Master Kong witnessed an unbelievable scene, he saw Lu Yang transform into a wisp of clear gas and drift into the sky, an image full of divinity, akin to the legendary Feather Transformation Ascension!

Suddenly, Sect Master Kong felt the Spiritual Energy around him disappear completely, and the heavens and earth came alive, using the Cycle Power to sustain Lu Yang!

Then, with a thunderous sound, Lu Yang, hundreds of feet tall and dressed impeccably, landed!

The giant Lu Yang grinned at Sect Master Kong, "I just happen to know the Law of Heaven and Earth."

Lu Yang’s landing made such a commotion, it was hard not to notice him, causing all disciples of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to stare open-mouthed at the giant Lu Yang, half-believing they were seeing an illusion.

"Isn’t it said that only a few in the Body Integration Realm can learn the Law of Heaven and Earth? Elder Brother Lu Yang is only in the Spirit Transformation Stage!"

"Yes, and Senior Brother Fang learned the Law of Heaven and Earth at the Early Integration Stage, already hailed as a genius that appears once in a thousand years."

Fang Jin looked at Lu Yang incredulously. Knowing the Law of Heaven and Earth was surprising enough, but what stunned him even more was how Lu Yang managed to grow while simultaneously enlarging his clothes as well.

He couldn’t help but doubt his own knowledge: "Could it be the Law of Heaven and Earth I learned is incomplete, and Junior Brother Lu has mastered the complete version?"

Chapter 1067: 1066: Sincere Advice

It was no wonder Fang Jin doubted his own learning, as the process of Lu Yang growing larger was indeed easy to deceive from an outsider’s perspective. Only Meng Jingzhou, who knew the ins and outs, understood how preposterous the process of Lu Yang’s growth was.

He still remembered when Lu Yang had to eat the soil from half an acre of land at the entrance to his abode to grow larger.

Meng Jingzhou couldn’t stand seeing Lu Yang steal the spotlight and stamped his foot with a cold snort, "Who can’t grow larger just the same."

The Supreme Dharma Form that reached heaven stood erect, holding a spear, clad in white robes, appearing like a scholar who had abandoned his pen for the military life, drawing gasps from the crowd.

"It’s a Dharma Aspect!"

"A Dharma Aspect never seen before!"

The disciples of Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had seen many high-level cultivators battle with magic, and had seen or heard of various Dharma Aspects, but they had never seen the Dharma Aspect displayed by Meng Jingzhou.

The Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image evolved from the Pure Yang Dharma Aspect into an even more extreme form, a Dharma Aspect uniquely mastered by Meng Jingzhou alone.

Two giants appeared in the Qingniu Mountain Range, and just by standing there, they emanated a pressure far greater than that of the Twin Senior Talents by dozens of times.

The two giants stared at each other, the atmosphere deadly, poised on a knife-edge.

Suddenly, the huge figure of Lu Yang moved. He fiercely collided with the mountains, pushing them back to their original positions.

Meng Jingzhou’s Dharma Aspect, on the other hand, scooped up a mound of dirt and piled it onto the flattened mountain top.

With two giants intervening, the restoration process was much faster than anticipated.

Upon seeing this scene, Sect Master Kong showed an expression as if deep in thought.

"These two haven’t reached the Body Integration Realm yet, but have already mastered the Law of Heaven and Earth and Dharma Aspects; they really are suitable for our Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect."

"It seems Third Zhang sending these two youngsters to me was not solely for punishment, but also to practice their Law of Heaven and Earth and Dharma Aspects."

"It looks like Elder Brother Dai wasn’t exaggerating when he praised these two junior brothers’ exceptional talents, unrivaled since ancient times," Fang Jin inwardly marveled, remembering that no cultivator he knew of had ever managed to successfully practice the Law of Heaven and Earth during the Divinity Transformation Stage.

Even Master of All Laws, famed as peerless genius, couldn’t achieve this.

"It’s just that I always feel there’s something off about Elder Brother Dai’s tone when he praises them, or am I overthinking it?"

As the disciples of Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect were already highly efficient, with the help of Lu Yang and the other, they restored Qingniu Mountain Range to its original state in less than two hours.

After returning to the sect, Fang Jin pondered for a while, wrote a letter, and sent it to Dai Bufan to inquire about the exact situation of the two Lu Yangs.

Sect Master Kong called the two Lu Yangs to the back mountain. Stroking his beard, the more he looked at the two, the more fitting he found them for their Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, it was a pity they had joined the Seek Dao Sect.

"I didn’t expect you two to be capable of the Law of Heaven and Earth and Dharma Aspects, that really was beyond my expectations."

"Earlier, I noticed your movements while moving the mountain were a bit unpolished, which suggests you are not very familiar with these two grand divine abilities."

Sect Master Kong, valuing talent, was willing to teach Lu Yang and the other some skills.

"These two grand divine abilities are fundamentally the same, both involve enlargement, enhancing oneself in all aspects, it’s just that one can inevitably feel uneasy after such enlargement," he explained.

The two Lu Yangs nodded in agreement, as they had felt it themselves.

"It just so happens that I have dabbled in these two aspects of Great Divine Skill, and I can teach you a few tricks,"

"However, ultimately, the best way is to use your Dharma Aspects more and complete large-scale restoration tasks. It’s just that such tasks are rare—you might wait two months and still encounter none if you’re unlucky. But it’s not a big problem; our sect has many different terrains, you can practice here..."

At this moment, Fang Jin approached, "Master, I’ve just received a mission from the Dynasty. Two cultivators in the early stages of the Integration Realm fought using their magical powers, and it’s said the battle was fierce. Mountains turned into plains and rivers changed their courses. The Dynasty wants our sect to handle the aftermath."

Sect Master Kong was slightly surprised, "This is exactly the kind of large-scale restoration task I was talking about. Little Lu and Little Meng, go give it a try."

"Yes."

On the way following Fang Jin, Lu Yang and his companion came to understand the situation.

The cause was one of the Integration Period Cultivators who had obtained a mysterious Jade Scroll at an auction, unaware of the scroll’s origin, contents, and how to activate it. The other cultivator realized that this Jade Scroll was the legendary one containing heritage from a Pass Tribulation Cultivator, and after the auction concluded, he developed a desire to seize it.

After the battle had erupted, the Dynasty arrested the latter cultivator for robbery.

This act of legitimate defense meant that the government would fund the cleanup, which in practice meant the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect would step in.

Under Fang Jin’s guidance, they used the Law of Heaven and Earth and Dharma Aspect Mountain Shifting to alter the river’s course and perfect the restoration.

"Sect Master Kong, we have handled it," Lu Yang and his companion returned to the sect to continue their studies.

"Well done, your speed was fast. Where did I stop last time? Oh right, I was saying there is a trick to magnification..."

"Master, a Void Refinement Stage Cultivator has successfully passed his tribulation, rising to the rank of Unification True Monarch, but during the Thunder Tribulation, what was supposed to be a six-stage tribulation turned into nine stages. The added stages of the tribulation destroyed the protective Array Method, ruining the landscape for hundreds of miles, turning pasture into desert, and they’ve requested our intervention."

Sect Master Kong clicked his tongue, "Alright then, you two go there again, and when you come back, I’ll continue with the tricks."

Lu Yang and his companion smoothly completed the task. Just as they arrived at the gates of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, they saw Sect Master Kong waiting with a piece of paper for the two of them.

"I just received another mission, saying that two Void Refinement Stage Cultivators have started fighting."

"These two Void Refinement Stage cultivators are half-brothers, both geniuses bearing unique talents. They were born with anomalies; the elder brother was born with an Immortal Bone in his finger, naturally containing Divine Abilities. The younger brother possessed an innate physique. Their father and the younger brother’s mother were true loves, hence he favored the younger brother, transplanted the elder brother’s Immortal Bone into the younger, giving him both the innate physique and the Immortal Bone."

Lu Yang roughly guessed what happened next, "Did the elder brother, even without the Immortal Bone, still rise to challenge his younger brother to prove he was the strongest?"

"No, instead, the elder brother pressed charges, and the government office transplanted the Immortal Bone back, arresting their parents. I estimate their parents won’t leave prison in their lifetimes."

"Feeling that his brother went too far, the brothers bore grudges, and their conflicts intensified, culminating in a life-and-death duel during their Void Refinement Stage, despite the inherent risks of their cultivation stage,"

"Who wins or loses is not our concern. You two go clean up the scene."

"All right."

Watching their departing figures, Sect Master Kong stroked his beard, his eyes flashing with a peculiar light.

"Strange, there weren’t so many high-level cultivator fights or large-scale restoration tasks before. How come there have been so many lately?"

He had always wanted the opportunity to teach the tricks, but never had the chance.

Meanwhile, Elder Brother Dai’s reply also arrived for Fang Jin, who opened the envelope, finding it contained only a sincere and urgent piece of advice which confused Fang Jin.

"Hurry up and buy insurance for your sect!"

Chapter 1068: 1067 Risk Assessment

"Buy insurance?" Fang Jin lifted the letter, tilting his head as he stared at it for a long time, trying to discern something else from the contents.

He found nothing.

"What does Elder Brother Dai mean? Is it some kind of code? But that shouldn’t be right, this isn’t some secret operation, why would there be a code?"

Fang Jin curbed the urge to soak the letter in water or roast it with fire. It was a regular letter, Elder Brother Dai probably wasn’t using a code.

"So he really wants me to buy insurance for the sect?"

Fang Jin was still baffled—he merely asked Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou what was going on, and somehow Dai Bufan just responded with "buy insurance."

With such a large sheet of paper, couldn’t you write a bit more? Is writing expensive for you?

Although confused, out of trust for Dai Bufan, Fang Jin dutifully headed to the Jingzhou Branch of the Land Money Trade Association.

The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was a Dynasty sect, and Fang Jin, holding the rank of Third Grade in official status, represented the sect externally. Additionally, being in the early stages of the Body Integration Realm, his visit to the Jingzhou branch warranted personal reception by the branch president.

"Brother Fang means your esteemed sect wants to purchase insurance?" The branch president was rather taken aback when he understood Fang Jin’s intention.

He had never heard of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect encountering any accidents. Why would they suddenly want to buy insurance?

"Brother Fang, what level of compensation are you considering for the sect insurance? We have five tiers here—Alpha, Beta, Gamma, Delta, and Epsilon. Normally, Gamma, which compensates sixty percent, is sufficient. If you’re determined, Beta compensating eighty percent is also an option, although very few opt for Alpha..."

"I’ll take Alpha, for a year first. How many spirit stones?"

Branch president grew more doubtful at Fang Jin’s decisiveness, and replied with a forced smile, "Brother Fang, you know the rules—we need to evaluate your sect’s risk over the past year before we can set the price."

"Don’t worry, brother, it’ll just take two days to get the results."

"Alright then."

After Fang Jin left, the branch president dispatched seven of the most professional evaluators to the Mountain and Sea Filling Sect. These seven evaluators were the veterans of the Jingzhou branch, abundantly experienced, and would have moved to the headquarters long ago if not for their attachment to the branch.

The dispatch of these seven cultivators illustrated the branch president’s consideration for the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

Two days later, the risk assessment results were ready.

The branch president grabbed a thick report, flipping directly to the conclusion on the last page:

"...The establishment of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect began twenty thousand years ago when an ancestor of the Dao Seeking Sect, acting as the governor of Qingzhou, ostensibly moved a flourishing nearby city into Qingzhou under the pretense of combat. This led the Dynasty to establish policies for terrain restoration and the founding of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect..."

"...Over the twenty thousand years, the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect faced no external invasions nor internal power struggles..."

"...The recent significant change in the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect is that Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou from the Dao Seeking Sect are currently staying there."

"There are countless stories about Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou in the martial world, with hundreds of storytelling scripts available. However, according to investigations, most of these tales are fabricated by the Dao Seeking Sect Master, Daoist Buyu, for promotional purposes, and the real Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou did not experience these stories."

"Although Lu Yang participated in the Shu State Sword Tower’s Sword Inquiry Convention, during which the Sword Tower was destroyed by an attack from the Yingtian Immortal—a compensation claim that became a classic example of insurance failure for the Shu State Branch in the last hundred years—Lu Yang’s presence was merely coincidental and unrelated to the attack on the Sword Tower."

"Therefore, the appearance of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou does not increase the risk for the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect."

"The conclusion is... extremely low risk?"

The branch president frowned—this didn’t make sense. If there was no risk, why would Fang Jin buy insurance?

However, the risk assessment report could not be falsified. Since all seven elders unanimously agreed on the extremely low risk, there must be no risk.

Fang Jin managed to buy a first-class Sect insurance at the lowest price.

...

These days, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou had been on their feet nonstop; each time they returned to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, they received new tasks, hurrying off in haste, unaware that the risk assessment cultivators had visited or that Elder Brother Fang had heedfully purchased insurance.

The two brothers initially enjoyed their efforts immensely, but gradually, the non-stop work wore them out both physically and mentally.

"This Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect is really that busy."

"The Dynasty is so stingy, not even sending extra help here."

Lu Yang shook his head, deeply disappointed with Prime Minister Emperor Xia’s management style of the Great Bean Dynasty; luckily, they were here as volunteers. If they hadn’t come, who knows how busy the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect would have been.

"Let it go, think positively; maybe Great Xia is just as short of people as our Great Bean Dynasty." Lu Yang consoled himself, his words expressing staunch loyalty to the Great Bean Dynasty, witnessed by heaven and earth, illuminated by the sun and moon.

However, Lu Yang had a special status, holding dual nationality; when Fairy Eternity was around, he was with the Great Bean Dynasty, and when Eldest Senior Sister was around, he was with the Great Xia Dynasty.

At this moment, Lu Yang transformed into a giant and began transplanting seedlings with a bent posture, as if he were arranging a mini landscape.

He first poked a hole in the ground with a finger, then cautiously planted a sapling into the hole, covering it with soil and watering it, being very careful to control his strength throughout the process, as a single mistake could ruin everything.

"Sect Master Kong is really right, practicing more is more effective than any technique."

Initially, Lu Yang couldn’t control his strength well and snapped the saplings, but now, as long as he was attentive, the saplings remained unharmed.

But that wasn’t enough; he aimed to plant trees effortlessly, with complete control over his strength.

Meng Jingzhou’s progress was similar to Lu Yang’s; as long as they were careful, there were no issues, but distractions led to failure.

After planting the trees, both of them had to move the mountains back to their original locations, which was also a meticulous task.

With their current strength, simply pushing the mountains back was no problem, but doing so would cause irreparable damage to the mountains, thus they also needed to control their strength when moving mountains.

"Finally done. Where’s our next stop?" Lu Yang wiped off his sweat, deflating like a balloon, spiritual essence leaking out, gradually shrinking back to his normal size.

Meng Jingzhou first helped his Dharma Aspect to take off clothes and fold them neatly before retracting the Dharma Aspect and picking up the clothes.

"Why is it so troublesome to dress and undress this Dharma Aspect?" Meng Jingzhou felt it was really inconvenient.

Lu Yang sighed, "What does this tell you? It shows that although those cultivators using Law of Heaven and Earth appear majestic in battle, once they revert, they have to sneakily and honestly fold their clothes."

This highlighted the advantage of Universe within the Sleeve, where clothes could be resized directly.

"Next stop is quite far, probably about twenty thousand miles from here."

As they spoke, intense fluctuations of spiritual energy came from above their heads.

Looking up, there were two Integration Period Cultivators battling, having flown from somewhere and stopping right there above them.

The might of Integration Period Cultivators was immense; to ordinary people, it might seem like the end of the world had come, causing them to tremble with fear.

These two Integration Period Cultivators battled fiercely, each move capable of tearing through the sky, crushing mountains, and evaporating lakes!

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were immediately anxious; after all the hard work they had put into organizing the terrain, letting it be destroyed by continued fighting was unacceptable!

"Liu Tianyang, today you and I must determine a victor!"

The two Integration Period Cultivators faced off with relentless ferocity, each move merciless, stirring ripples in the fabric of space that radiated outward in expanding circles.

They fought as they flew, eventually arriving at a location of pristine mountains and clear waters, where they simultaneously stopped flying and chose this place for their battle.

Being Integration Period Cultivators was a rarity. Moreover, as a disciple of Daoist Buyu, Lu Yang had early on come to recognize the faces of almost all Integration Period Cultivators in Great Xia, these two included, known as Liu Tianyang and Ye Zheng, both at the Integration Mid-Stage, one specializing in Earth Magic and the other in Water Based Magic.

The two also recognized Lu Yang and his companion.

The disciple of Daoist Buyu, Lu Yang, and Meng Family’s Young Master, Meng Jingzhou.

Although Liu Tianyang and Ye Zheng recognized their identities, they still had no intention of stopping. They were locked in an intense magical duel, and one misstep could mean total defeat. Whoever paused first would surely lose.

Moreover, even though Lu Yang and his companion had big reputations as geniuses, they were ultimately juniors. What right did they have to interfere in their matters?
