Lu Yang watched, his eyes wide as the carefully tended landscape was destroyed. Merging Three Flavored True Fire with Golden Bird True Flame into the ultimate True Flame, he flew to the middle of Liu Tianyang and Ye Zheng and detonated it.

The casting process was interrupted, and the Earth Dragon, barely poking its head out of the ground, crumbled to dust.

Lu Yang indeed had some skill, but he was not nearly as powerful as described in the stories, certainly not stronger than himself!

Originally full of fighting spirit, Liu Tianyang and his ally were stunned by the sight. It somehow made Lu Yang look like a leader of the Demon Path who had gone to the Buddha Country, slaughtered a group of monks, and then draped the monks around himself like trophies of war.

Not even Old Buyu had such sinister moves.

Lu Yang, indifferent to his own appearance, knew that as long as it could enhance his combat capabilities, it was good enough. Now cloaked in his Dharma Aspect and adorned with the Sixteen-Foot Golden Body, this combined technique bestowed upon him astonishing defensive power. Not even cultivators of the Body Integration Realm could make a dent in their defense.

Lu Yang infused his Dharma Aspect with layers of Three Flavored True Fire and Golden Bird True Flame, then unleashed the Arhat Fist, forcing Liu Tianyang and his ally to retreat step by step.

Each of Lu Yang’s punches broke the sound barrier, causing a deafening roar.

"My hair!" Liu Tianyang and his partner cried out in alarm; they fought while retreating and losing hair in the process — their appearance couldn’t get any worse.

"Law of Heaven and Earth, Three Flavored True Fire, Gold Crow True Fire... indeed quite capable, truly worthy of being geniuses. However, you shouldn’t have laid hands on us!"

They tried to restrict Lu Yang’s moves with rules, eager to see what he’d do next!

Lu Yang’s fists rained down like a hailstorm, pummeling Ye Zheng and his ally to the point where their hair littered the ground.

Chapter 1070: 1069: Advice

Liu Tianyang and Ye Zheng were suddenly speechless, so you two were waiting for us here?

Just now, you two were the fiercest in fighting; the damaged parts of the mountain range were all caused by you both. We’ve been getting hit all this time, and now it turns out we have to repair it?

"Actually, we could pay the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to help with the repairs." When under someone’s roof, one must bow their heads, Liu Tianyang said rather unwillingly, negotiating in a soft voice, reluctant to take matters into his own hands. After all, he is an Integration Period Cultivator.

They couldn’t imagine that after implementing the Law of Heaven and Earth and the Dharma Aspect Integration, their offensive and defensive capabilities had increased enough to contend with the Integration Realm, let alone how Lu Yang could now be immune to rules?

What kind of sorcery is this?

"That won’t work, we are currently helping the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect with their tasks, and in the end, it still means we would have to do the work."

"I don’t care, I’ve given the solution and I can provide the Spirit Stones." Liu Tianyang had the attitude of a dead pig not fearing boiling water, not believing that Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, these two juniors, could do anything to them.

Lu Yang sighed and kindly persuaded, "Both seniors should know that the ’Cultivation Monthly’ is published by our Seek Dao Sect. Unfortunately, I am in charge of the newspaper’s content."

Liu Tianyang was startled; he knew that the Cultivation Monthly was published by the Seek Dao Sect, but he didn’t know it was managed by Lu Yang. Suddenly, he had a bad premonition.

"I think the two seniors wouldn’t want to see in the newspaper, ’Integration Cultivator Liu Tianyang, Integration Cultivator Ye Zheng, facing personnel from the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect urging restraint, they refused to listen and assaulted them, resulting in severe damage to the Vast Wave Mountain Range, and boasting about having plenty of Spirit Stones to make the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect responsible for repairing the mountain range.’

Liu Tianyang’s right eye twitched; he heard that Speechless rarely returned to Seek Dao Sect and had hardly taught Lu Yang, how come this kid’s rotten ideas were even more outrageous than Old Buyu’s?

He and Ye Zheng are well-known figures, if this report were to get out, would they still have any reputation left?

Meng Jingzhou stepped forward and added, "On the other hand, if the two seniors were to repair the Vast Wave Mountain Range, the newspaper would read ’Faced with the damaged Vast Wave Mountain Range caused by others, Integration Cultivators Liu Tianyang and Ye Zheng take initiative, actively engage, and voluntarily repair the mountain range, setting a prime example for fellow cultivators.’

Sometimes, when Lu Yang was in seclusion and could not catch the newspaper release, Meng Jingzhou would be responsible for the content part, the two cooperated seamlessly, and their newspaper business was on the rise.

Ye Zheng: "..."

Before I met you two, I always thought that the reports in the newspaper were true, wait, it seems both types of reports are actually true.

"Fine fine fine, we’ll repair, we’ll repair," Liu Tianyang hastily said, fearful that Lu Yang might go with one of the reports.

Ye Zheng thought to himself, I knew it Old Buyu, your storytelling never told the true story, these two kids here are definitely craftier and trickier than in the stories.

Liu Tianyang, skilled in Earth Magic, found it easy to repair the environment; Ye Zheng was skilled not only in Water-Based Magic but also knew some Wood Element Techniques, more than enough to repair the mountain range.

Liu Tianyang and his companion couldn’t remember what the original Vast Wave Mountain Range looked like; luckily, the two youths from Lu Yang’s side had just repaired it and knew the original appearance of the mountain range.

Lu Yang, looking at the backdrop of the two Combining Great Abilities working hard, felt that the Great Xia Cultivators fought carelessly, often causing earth-shattering movements, significantly increasing the workload of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

"This must have been how the Third Elder felt back then; he sent us to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to let us experience his feelings at that time."

Lu Yang, seeing the situation from another perspective, realized the Third Elder’s purpose.

Little did he expect that the Third Elder, seemingly reckless, was actually a meticulous thinker.

"Is this really what the Third Elder thought? It doesn’t feel right," Immortal Fairy wondered, being a Guest Elder of the Seek Dao Sect, she too could refer to the Third Elder.

She felt the Third Elder seemed reckless and was indeed reckless.

Lu Yang’s expression turned serious, as the Emperor’s Master it was necessary to correct Immortal Fairy’s wrong views: "Your Majesty, you are judging by appearances, looks do not represent one’s character. For instance, Your Majesty, you look lovely and lively, yet you are a meticulous, scheming wise monarch, aren’t you?"

Immortal Fairy thought about it, that indeed was the case; she was very cute yet also quite intelligent. Little Yang’s judgment of people was accurate, it seems the Third Elder and I are of the same kind.

"By the way, I forgot to ask the two seniors, do you both have some disagreement, to the point of fighting fiercely over it?" Lu Yang suddenly remembered to ask.

Ye Zheng slapped the mountain summit, "No disagreement, we are both from the Tianhe Sect. Our old Sect Master wants to step down and nominate a successor, both of us could potentially become the Sect Master, hence we proposed to have a battle, the winner becomes the Sect Master."

Liu Tianyang glanced at Lu Yang, his smile somewhat sinister: "According to the rules, it should be you who becomes the Sect Master."

"Me? No way, no way," Lu Yang hurriedly declined, how could he become the Sect Master of the Tianhe Sect.

Seeing Lu Yang’s flustered state, Ye Zheng burst into laughter: "Just kidding, don’t take it seriously."

"Today, we both have depleted too much energy, we agree to another battle on another day."

The Vast Wave Mountain Range was repaired for the second time, after bidding farewell to Ye Zheng and Liu Tianyang, Lu Yang and his companion moved on to the next location.

While flying, Lu Yang thought of something and discussed with Meng Jingzhou: "So many cultivators fighting without any care, damaging the landscape, recently, the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was fortunate to have our help, barely managing to cope. What will happen to Sect Master Kong and the others after we leave in a month and a half? "

"Indeed," Meng Jingzhou nodded.

Both were men of strong loyalty and sentiment, although they had only been in the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect for just half a month, they already considered themselves part of the sect.

"How about this, whenever we see cultivators fighting, let’s try to persuade them, it might somewhat change the cultivators’ reckless behavior of fighting and damaging the environment."

"Okay."

...

After completing their tasks, they successfully returned to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

Sect Master Kong, looking at the task report they submitted, was full of confusion.

"Do you mean to say, during your task completion journey, you just happened to encounter some cultivators fighting, and after your advice, these cultivators recognized how troublesome it is to fight and damage the environment, hence they voluntarily proposed to fight in the heavens or share friendships through literary meetings?"

"Yes." Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou nodded; their mission could indeed be considered a full merit.

Sect Master Kong was full of question marks; since when were the Great Xia’s cultivators so easy to talk to? Despite his public calls and attempts at persuasion several times, it never worked. How come it worked when it came to Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou?

Was his status as the Sect Master of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect considered inferior to the status of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou?

"You didn’t threaten them, did you?"

"Senior Kong, what are you saying? We were just lucky to meet reasonable people. Old Meng and I agree their exemplary actions deserve to be publicized in the newspaper, rightfully promoted."

Chapter 1071: 1070: The Sect Might Not Be Peaceful Lately

In a certain palace, figures successively took their seats, numbering over ten individuals.

These individuals held different identities—some were local overlords, others were Elders of Sects, and some were the elders of influential families, all of them Body Integration Realm powerhouses.

"You all should be clear about the reason for summoning you here," said the master of the palace, the organizer, speaking slowly.

"Isn’t it just because Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou used the newspaper to threaten us, asking us to hold back a bit during battles?" one of the powerhouses said indignantly, his voice halting as he struggled to continue, unable to exert his full strength.

"It’s not just the newspaper—those two youngsters have mastered some unknown Forbidden Technique; the rules are ineffective against it."

"The younger generation is indeed fearsome."

"Even Old Buyu couldn’t achieve this; truly, blue is extracted from the indigo plant but is more vivid than it."

"We can no longer treat them as mere Divine Transformation Stage juniors."

"So, do any of you have good ideas? We can’t possibly be threatened by mere juniors!" the palace master asked angrily, revealing the true purpose of the assembly.

"Why not get serious and teach those two a lesson about respecting their seniors, respecting strength!"

"The problem is it seems like we can’t beat them, especially once they assume the Golden Body Arhat form; it looks heretical, but the defense is really something else. Even with all our might, we just can’t break through."

"Then why not let them report whatever they want, and we’ll just insist that what’s in their newspaper is false news!"

"I’m afraid that won’t work; the common people believe what’s printed in the newspapers."

"What kind of lousy idea is that? Of course, it won’t work. We might insist all we want, but who will hear us? Does anyone have a way to spread our voices across all of Great Xia?"

"Then should we also publish a newspaper?"

"It’s useless; the other four Immortal Sects and the Dynasty are already publishing newspapers, and the best-selling one is still the Seek Dao Sect’s paper, isn’t it?"

"Who can say otherwise? I hear Seek Dao Sect has even mastered a special method of printing newspapers, much faster than the other powers, and they’re not sharing the technique."

The group discussed it in a circle, looking at each other, finding that Lu Yang and his comrade were like hedgehogs, untouchable, even trickier than Daoist Buyu.

...

Perhaps it was because Lu Yang and his partner were keen to help others, often intervening to prevent battles, information that had spread far and wide, or maybe it was just a coincidence given the timing of recent events.

In any case, ever since Lu Yang and his partner had triumphantly completed their mission and returned, the major tasks of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had diminished significantly—it wasn’t worth having Lu Yang and his partner involved anymore.

Seeing that there was finally time to teach Lu Yang and his partner the minor techniques they would need after growing larger, Sect Master Kong discovered that during the mission, they had already mastered the Law of Heaven and Earth and the Dharma Aspect, to a degree of proficiency where his minor techniques were unnecessary.

The missions they had undertaken recently covered various uses of the Law of Heaven and Earth and the Dharma Aspect, which were exceptionally beneficial for practice. Even a teaching program designed by Sect Master Kong himself couldn’t achieve such a perfect synergy.

"It seems you two have indeed been quite fortunate," Sect Master Kong couldn’t help but remark, thinking that perhaps this was what it meant to be Geniuses born of fortune.

"There’s something else—an uneasiness is brewing in the Sect; you should be mindful of your safety."

"Uneasiness?" Lu Yang and his partner were puzzled. What could possibly go wrong with the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect?

"Xiao Jin mentioned he had a bad premonition, feels like something is going to happen to the Sect, and even took out a full insurance policy. As to where exactly this premonition came from, Xiao Jin did not explain clearly," Sect Master Kong said, his worry apparent. Trusting his senior disciple, he did not pry further into Fang Jin’s reasons.

"Alright, we’ll be careful," Lu Yang and his partner replied, expressing their gratitude with a bow.

In the days that followed, sect members of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect could often see two impressive figures walking around the Sect grounds. The duo could even lift an entire river with their mana, without letting a single drop of water fall—their control over their strength and mana grew ever more refined, leaving Fang Jin in awe.

Though he had a high aptitude in the Law of Heaven and Earth, he could not match Lu Yang’s rapid progress.

He walked over with a smile and greeted them, "Junior Brother Lu, Junior Brother Meng."

"Senior Brother Fang, what brings you here?" Lu Yang and his partner set down the river they were holding and walked step by step toward Fang Jin.

With each step Lu Yang took, dense spiritual essence hissed around him like white fog, and his stature gradually shrank in the process.

By the time Lu Yang came up to Fang Jin, he had returned to his original size, with the mysterious white fog around him making his spiritual essence look profound and unfathomable.

Fang Jin hesitated for a moment before revealing the purpose of his visit, "Here’s the thing: I heard from our master that you two are eloquent and have dissuaded many cultivators from their battles, significantly reducing our Sect’s task load."

"Master had thought about persuading them in the past, but it was never effective. I had a similar idea before, but hearing that even master had failed, I gave up the notion."

"But now that we have you two as examples, I was thinking of learning from you how to persuade cultivators, so that I might have some reference."

"Right now, there’s a scheduled task where two Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators want to battle. I was wondering if you two could go and try to persuade them, allowing me to observe and learn by your side?"

"This..." Lu Yang and his partner hesitated, as their method seemed something Senior Brother Fang might not be able to learn.

Saying so outright could hurt Senior Brother Fang’s confidence, couldn’t it?

Over the past month, Senior Brother Fang had been exceedingly good to them, doing his best to accommodate any requests; a blatant rejection would be too disrespectful.

Meng Jingzhou gritted his teeth and said, "Well, we might as well give it a try. However, our methods might not always work, so please be prepared for that, Senior Brother Fang."

"Of course," Fang Jin responded.

There wasn’t much to prepare, and the trio set off immediately. On the way to the mission location, Fang Jin filled them in on the details.

"The mission involves two Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators, one named Chen Yachong and the other He Wu. Chen Yachong, who has a penchant for gambling, has accrued a significant debt and even his cultivation resources are insufficient because of it. He ended up borrowing a million Spirit Stones from his friend He Wu."

"Unsurprisingly, Chen Yachong could not pay back the money when it was due, and simply proposed that He Wu could use him as a punching bag in lieu of repayment; if not satisfied, He could hit him a few more times."

"He Wu feels that just beating him won’t vent his anger; he insists on pinning Chen Yachong to the ground and battering him in the river to feel appeased. That’s why he approached the Sect in advance, asking the Sect to pick up the pieces afterward."

Lu Yang found himself in a dilemma, detecting a similar reaction from Meng Jingzhou.

Seeing their expressions, it didn’t seem like the usual methods of persuasion would suffice. Surely they couldn’t expect Old Meng to pay off Chen Yachong’s million Spirit Stones; that wouldn’t exactly be setting an example, would it?

"Why don’t you pay the Spirit Stones!" Meng Jingzhou exploded.

Lu Yang was startled, "How did you know what I was thinking?"

"It’s all written on your face, you rascal!"

"I’ll be more careful next time."

Chapter 1072: 1071: Resolving the Dispute

Chen Yachong and He Wu resided in a well-known large city within Yun Province called Yuntie City, under the rule of an Integration Period Cultivator as the City Lord.

When Lu Yang and his companions found Chen Yachong, it just so happened that He Wu was also looking for him.

"Chen Yachong, I tell you, don’t think that just because I’ve beaten you twice this matter can be considered over, one million Spirit Stones of principal and two hundred thousand in interest—you must pay me all of it!"

In the luxurious residence, He Wu cornered Chen Yachong, with blades levitating ominously behind his back, emanating a chilling coldness.

He Wu was also a Sword Cultivator.

"Please hold on for a moment, you two. May I interrupt?" Fang Jin landed in the courtyard, drawing the alert attention of He Wu and Chen Yachong.

"Who are you!"

"Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, Fang Jin."

Upon hearing Fang Jin introduce himself, He Wu’s pupils constricted sharply. Wasn’t he only at the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation? Was it really necessary for the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to send the Integration Period’s Fang Jin?!

Seeing Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou behind Fang Jin, he was even more stunned.

"You, you are Talent Lu!" shouted He Wu excitedly, "I saw you at the Sword Tower’s Sword Inquiry Ceremony!"

Lu Yang’s performance at the Sword Inquiry Ceremony had made him a target for all Sword Cultivators to aspire to.

"So, you are Fellow Daoist He Wu," Lu Yang pretended as if he had just recognized He Wu, when in fact there were thousands of Sword Cultivators at the Sword Inquiry Ceremony, and he couldn’t possibly recognize him.

"Then you must be Fellow Daoist Chen Yachong, right?"

"Yes."

Knowing the identity of Lu Yang and his companions, He Wu and Chen Yachong reined in their pride befitting those of the Divinity Transformation Stage.

"Fellow Daoist Chen, I’ve heard that you have debts that you haven’t repaid, is that so?" Lu Yang stepped forward and asked, having roughly thought of a solution on the way here.

"That is the case, but I really have no money right now."

Lu Yang nodded with a smile, indicating his understanding. Then he asked He Wu, "Fellow Daoist He, if Fellow Daoist Chen were to repay the money, would that mean he no longer has to be beaten?"

"Naturally."

"It’s no problem if you don’t have the money. Fellow Daoist Chen, you should know that our Seek Dao Sect produces the Oblivion Pill every year, and it sells well, more demand than supply."

"The biggest issue with the Oblivion Pill right now is insufficient production."

"To be frank, the Oblivion Pill is refined using the blood of cultivators. All that’s needed is for Fellow Daoist Chen to accompany me back to our sect to regularly take medicine and let blood. In less than ten years, you could earn the one million Spirit Stones."

"Of course, letting blood might be somewhat painful, but with Fellow Daoist Chen’s perseverance, I believe you can endure it."

Fang Jin looked at Lu Yang in shock. So, this was how the Oblivion Pill was made? This didn’t sound like a proper method!

Observing Meng Jingzhou’s expression, which was one of indifference, it was clear he saw nothing wrong with this method!

"Junior Brother Meng, isn’t there something wrong with how you produce the Oblivion Pill?" Fang Jin secretly transmitted a message to Meng Jingzhou.

"There’s nothing wrong. Look, we’ve been producing it this way for years now, and Senior Brother Fang, you haven’t heard of anyone coming out and accusing our Seek Dao Sect of inhumanity, have you? That shows everyone is voluntary."

Fang Jin’s expression turned odd. Is this how you two always persuaded people?

"No, no, no, I just don’t have the money right now," Chen Yachong broke into a cold sweat the moment he heard Lu Yang’s proposal, hastily claiming he had the money.

"Where is the money?"

"Uh, it’s invested with Zhao Tianyi. He said he has a way to make money, promising a thirty percent return within a year. It hasn’t matured yet, so I can’t retrieve the funds. Once the money returns, I can repay He Wu the principal plus interest."

Zhao Tianyi has a cultivation level in the Divinity Transformation Stage and is the Sect Leader and founder of the Tianyi Sect, a Third Grade sect in Yuntie City.

"How about this—I’ll put this map up as collateral. I guarantee I can repay the money!" Chen Yachong produced a piece of Jade Silk with several symbols drawn on it.

He Wu disdainfully responded, "Who needs this worthless map? You think I don’t know that you’ve bid on this lousy map at auction? Hundreds of years have passed and you still haven’t found the other two pieces."

"What if you get lucky," Chen Yachong said sheepishly.

"May I take a look?"

Lu Yang, out of curiosity, took the Jade Silk Map. The map was very simple and completely indecipherable.

Fang Jin, who was well-versed in maps, craned his neck for a closer look and surmised, "It seems to be a map from the early days of Great Qian. There should be three pieces in total of this map, and only when all three pieces overlap will the real map be revealed."

"How much for this map? I’ll buy it," Fang Jin was fond of ancient maps, especially one from the early days of Great Qian—it was a rare find.

"If you like it, I’ll give it to you," Chen Yachong was not particularly attached to the map. Hundreds of years ago, when he was only at the Foundation Establishment Stage, the items he could get from a Foundation Establishment Level auction wouldn’t be worth many Spirit Stones. It was better to give a favor where it flowed.

Fang Jin solemnly accepted the Jade Silk Map, determined that no matter what, he must help Chen Yachong resolve the issue.

If worst comes to worst, when Chen Yachong was sent to the Seek Dao Sect to let blood, he would have to ask Junior Brother Lu to go easy and not let Chen Yachong feel pain.

"Zhao Tianyi? Then let’s go find him for the money."

The five of them approached Zhao Tianyi with grandeur, and it truly gave Zhao Tianyi a fright, especially when he saw Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

In the storytelling, these two often punished the wicked and evil, making villains tremble just at the mention of their names.

After clarifying their purpose, Zhao Tianyi breathed a sigh of relief. It turned out they just came for money... Wait, coming for money isn’t exactly good news either.

"Brother Chen, I’m not unwilling to give you the Spirit Stones, but your Spirit Stones were all invested, you know. The Spirit Stones I have on hand are all used for the daily expenses of the sect. You can’t possibly want my sect to go bankrupt, can you?"

"If you’re not in a hurry, just wait a bit. The Spirit Stones will be retrievable soon, and that’s a profit of twenty percent!"

Chen Yachong interrupted Zhao Tianyi’s promise, "Hold on, the profit you mentioned to me was thirty percent!"

Zhao Tianyi realized he had misspoken and said with an embarrassed smile, "I was just worried that you wouldn’t invest if the profit seemed too low. Anyway, a twenty percent profit is still a gain, right?"

Seeing Chen Yachong still frowning, Zhao Tianyi hurriedly added, "Right, right, here’s a wooden box I brought back from the Extreme Northern Lands. It should contain something good inside; it just lacks a key. I’ll give it to you as an apology."

Chen Yachong rolled his eyes. What use did he have for this lousy wooden box?

Seeing the situation, Lu Yang stepped in to smooth things over, "Alright, alright, Fellow Daoist Zhao means well. Brother Chen, just let this go."

Chen Yachong, giving face to Lu Yang, huffed a few times and let it pass.

Zhao Tianyi, seeing this, happily handed the wooden box to Lu Yang.

"Who did you invest the Spirit Stones with?"

"Duan Qinghe. That guy has many ways to make money."

Duan Qinghe, also an Integrator from Yuntie City, was not familiar to Chen Yachong, but Zhao Tianyi knew him well.

The group didn’t stop their search and soon found Duan Qinghe.

Duan Qinghe’s reaction was very much like Zhao Tianyi’s. Seeing such a big group, he thought he had gotten into trouble, even alarming Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

"So it’s about the one million Spirit Stones you owe me, Zhao Tianyi. What’s the rush? Don’t you want the ten percent profit anymore?"

"Ten percent? You told me it was a twenty percent profit!" Zhao Tianyi exclaimed.

Duan Qinghe scratched his head, "Isn’t it just because I was afraid you wouldn’t lend me the money if I said too little? Anyway, a ten percent profit is still a profit."

"Then who did you invest the money with!"

Duan Qinghe pointed at He Wu standing behind, "With He Wu. Isn’t the interest on his loans one and a half percent? I thought I’d keep a half percent for myself when the money comes back, and give you the ten percent."

He Wu: "..."

"Hold on, I have an IOU here."

Duan Qinghe took out a piece of beast skin from his Storage Ring, which clearly stated that he borrowed the money from He Wu at an interest rate of one and a half percent per year.

"After all, one million Spirit Stones is not a small amount. I specifically used old beast skin from the Demon Territory to make a note of it."

Fang Jin painfully held his forehead, "All of you stop talking, let me sort this out."

"He Wu is the lender, with an annual interest of twenty percent."

"Duan Qinghe, wanting to make money, told Zhao Tianyi he would give him twenty percent, when he could actually only give ten percent."

"Zhao Tianyi told Chen Yachong he could make him thirty percent, when in reality, it was only twenty percent."

"Chen Yachong, wanting to make money, borrowed from He Wu at an interest rate of twenty percent."

Finally, Fang Jin couldn’t help it any longer and slammed the table, infuriated, "Do any of you ever tell the truth!"

Lu Yang thought about it and realized that only He Wu and Chen Yachong had told the truth, but since Senior Brother Fang was furious, it was better to hold back.

Meng Jingzhou also summed up, "So Chen Yachong only needs to pay He Wu fifty thousand Spirit Stones for the half-percent interest, right?"

Fang Jin took out fifty thousand Spirit Stones and handed them to Chen Yachong as the price for the map, eager to get this matter settled quickly.

In recent years, Fang Jin had made a significant profit from several big events organized by the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, like the Qingzhou Festival and Hanshui City affairs. To him, fifty thousand Spirit Stones were just a drop in the bucket.

All debts cleared, Duan Qinghe’s beast skin IOU was no longer useful, so he simply gave it to Fang Jin, the final financier.

After wrapping up, Fang Jin, Lu Yang, and Meng Jingzhou returned to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. Fang Jin was somewhat caught between laughter and tears over this ridiculous situation.

"Senior Brother Fang, sometimes luck can solve problems," Lu Yang comforted.

Fang Jin thought about it and realized it was true, and his trip wasn’t entirely fruitless. He took out the Jade Silk Map, deciding to regard it as reclaiming an old map, just unsure when he would find the remaining old maps.

Suddenly, the Jade Silk Map, the box, and the beast skin all vibrated, as if triggering a resonance.

The box opened, and inside was a Jade Silk Map.

The beast skin tore, and out flew a Jade Silk Map.

The three Jade Silk Maps overlapped, revealing the true appearance of the real map.

Fang Jin: "..."

What was going on? Wasn’t it said that Chen Yachong had spent hundreds of years without being able to collect them all?

When had his luck become so good?

Chapter 1073: 1072: The Time Immortal’s Immortal Artifact

"This means that you, Senior Brother Fang, possess enormous good fortune."

Lu Yang stepped forward to congratulate him very tactfully, even happier than when he had gathered the map himself.

"Chen Yachong spent hundreds of years without obtaining the other two pieces of the map, yet Senior Brother Fang, you collected all of them in just one trip. What does this tell us? It shows that this map is fated to be yours!"

"Precisely, indeed, this must be an extremely auspicious sign." Meng Jingzhou also stepped forward to offer his congratulations; the two of them were so eloquent in their praise, lauding Fang Jin as if he were the chosen one.

The three pieces of the Jade Silk Map overlaid together revealed the true appearance of the map.

The map displayed a place called the Qinglian Mountain Range, with a circle drawn in the middle of the mountain range, which probably indicated something of significance there.

"Where is Qinglian Mountain Range?" Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou looked at each other in confusion. They were not familiar with the names of various mountain ranges. Where was it located in Great Xia?

"Qinglian Mountain Range..." Fang Jin furrowed his brows, vaguely recognizing the name.

"If I remember correctly, the Qinglian Mountain Range was a name from the Great Qian Period."

"During the Great Yu Era, it was called the Jiangbei Hills."

"During the Great Xia Period, it was called the Tianhe Plain."

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou wore odd expressions; it seemed like the changes involved more than just the name.

"That’s quite normal. Have you not considered? During the Great Qian and Great Yu periods, there were many cultivators, and their battles were even more chaotic, often altering the landscape," explained Fang Jin.

"Over three hundred thousand years driven by human forces, seas turned into mulberry fields; the maps of those times have long since changed."

Fang Jin was well-versed in ancient maps; he remembered clearly all the changes in geography from the Great Qian to the Great Xia periods.

This ancient map was useful only in the hands of Fang Jin; other cultivators who obtained it could only stare at it helplessly.

"Let’s go check out the Tianhe Plain." With the ancient map in hand, Fang Jin was enthused.

Since he had gathered the ancient map through incredible coincidences, perhaps as Junior Brother Lu had said, the treasures were destined to be his.

The Tianhe Plain was abundant with water and grass, with cattle and sheep everywhere. When the three of them landed, they even startled a flock of sheep that scattered, leaving behind a field covered with droppings.

Some of the lambs unafraid, bleating as they charged towards the three men, were quickly snatched away by the ewes.

Fang Jin gestured; the site marked on the ancient map was rather large: "It should be around here."

He released his Spiritual Sense, scanning every inch of land within the range and discovered clues beneath the ground.

"It seems there is a Secret Realm underground; let’s dig and have a look," he suggested.

"Why bother digging? Just have Old Lu consume the dirt," proposed Meng Jingzhou.

Lu Yang kicked over; this place was full of sheep droppings, I’ll eat your grandpa.

Fang Jin and Lu Yang both employed the Law of Heaven and Earth while Meng Jingzhou deployed his Dharma Aspect; the three giants squatted down to dig the earth.

After digging about two thousand meters deep, the three of them encountered a stone that seemed embedded into the space itself, static even when the earth below was removed. They tried moving the stone but to no avail.

Fang Jin channeled spiritual essence into the stone, and the stone revealed complex patterns, ancient and mysterious. The stone exerted a powerful suction force, pulling all three of them in.

When the three regained their senses, all was pitch black around them.

"It seems that this place suppresses Spiritual Sense; we can’t utilize it here."

Meng Jingzhou looked up and ignited the Pure Yang Fire to illuminate the space.

"A cave?"

The three discovered they were in a damp and closed cavern, with no hint of a breeze, indicating the cavern was sealed.

"Is there a treasure in here?"

Just as Fang Jin was about to take a step forward, he suddenly heard Lu Yang’s voice.

"Senior Brother Fang, don’t move forward!"

Fang Jin abruptly stopped in his tracks, "What’s wrong?"

"There’s a restriction in front of you, if you continue, it will trigger the restriction, and it’s very dangerous!" Lu Yang warned solemnly.

Fang Jin jumped in fright and quickly retracted his left foot, crouching down to inspect the ground, he indeed found some incomprehensible runes, although they were fragmented.

"It seems this cave is filled with restrictions, but someone forced their way in and destroyed these runes," Lu Yang said, passing off the words of Immortal Fairy as his own speculation.

Even the runes that had been damaged contained an extremely terrifying power that was beyond their capability to touch.

"This method of setting up restrictions is like that of the Time Immortal," Immortal Fairy pondered, stroking her chin, "Since his true form is a tree, he always likes to bury things underground, making them very troublesome to find."

"Little Yang, you go ahead and walk. An idiot has already explored the way for you, and the obstructive restrictions have been destroyed. Just avoid touching the restrictions, and you’ll be fine."

"The trinket at the end of the cave should be created by the Time Immortal."

Lu Yang carefully bypassed the restrictions, leading Fang Jin and Meng Jingzhou to the deepest part of the cave, finding a small metal box roughly the size of a fist, placed on a high plinth.

Fang Jin used spiritual essence to form a large hand, taking the metal box from a distance. The box felt heavy in his hands.

"Is this made of Immortal Gold?!"

Fang Jin was astonished, for if it was made of Immortal Gold, then the metal box was at least a half-immortal crafted Immortal Treasure, and there was even a tiny chance it was crafted by an Immortal!

"Fairy, what is the purpose of this metal box crafted by the Time Immortal?"

Immortal Fairy pondered with frustration, "The little gadgets made by the Time Immortal look quite similar, and I always get them mixed up."

"However, judging by the style of this metal box, it seems to be an artifact that can slow down time."

Lu Yang was startled; an artifact that could slow down time sounded extraordinary. As expected of the Time Immortal, the effects of his crafted artifacts were so powerful.

"To use this box, there is an incantation, ’Immortal as Supreme, Time as King’, no, that’s not right. It’s ’Stars revolve as I command, the four seasons change in the blink of an eye.’ Also not right, what was it?"

"Why not just ask the Time Immortal?" suggested Lu Yang, thinking that if remembering was difficult, why not ask the person himself.

"No need, that’s too much trouble. Just open the ’Immortal’s Quotations’, pick out the words the Time Immortal has said, and read them one by one; the incantation will definitely be in there."

"...Alright then."

Fang Jin flipped through it for a long time, not knowing how to use the metal box, "Forget it, the master and the Supreme Elder have seen much and know much, let’s take it back for them to see."

The metal box belonged to the Time Immortal, and logically should be returned to its owner, but with Lu Yang’s status, it wasn’t appropriate for him to keep the box; it needed the Sect’s high-level members to step forward.

Lu Yang thought that he would have to speak to the Eldest Senior Sister and have her figure out a way to return it to the Time Immortal.

"Old Lu, there’s nothing dangerous about this metal box, is there?" Meng Jingzhou asked, uneasy.

"There shouldn’t be any danger. This is a great item left behind by the Time Immortal."

"That’s good then."

...

"Xiao Tian, do you think I was too harsh by making Little Meng and Little Lu do two months of pointless work at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect as a punishment?" The Third Elder had been troubled recently, wanting to discuss it with Yun Zhi, but since she couldn’t be found, she instead went to consult with the Third Senior Sister, Gan Tian.

Gan Tian thought to herself that she had assumed the Elder was using this opportunity to send them to learn the Law of Heaven and Earth and Dharma Aspect at the sect, but in reality, was it just a punishment?

"Should I call the Second-in-command back? I’ll go call her!" Yun Mengmeng volunteered eagerly.

Chapter 1074: 1073 What’s the use of this broken box!

Recently, Si Lei God Lord has been rather restless, and it’s not because his good friend Si Chen God Lord has disappeared without a trace, nor is it a big deal that the treasure map he drew was lost.

The biggest problem he faces now is that he doesn’t know the location of the Ancient Secret Realm.

Thirty thousand years ago, he came across a secret realm left by Fairy Eternity from ancient times, but the realm was so heavily restricted that he dared not touch it, yet he couldn’t bear to give up on such an opportunity.

Due to the long duration of his slumber, coupled with the influence of the Power of Faith, there was a possibility of partial memory loss upon awakening.

Therefore, he drew a map before his deep sleep and split it into three parts, scattering it among the common folk to ensure that even if someone got their hands on one part, they wouldn’t be able to piece together the complete map.

Naturally, he also had a secret method to know the whereabouts of the map.

Upon awakening, he remembered the location of that Ancient Secret Realm, situated in Lotus Mountain; thus, the map became something he could take or leave.

"Where is Qinglian Mountain rage?"

After waking up from a long sleep, not only had dynasties changed twice, but the very landscape had undergone drastic transformations — without any landmarks, remembering the location of Qinglian Mountain was utterly useless.

"Can’t those cultivators restore the original geography after their battles!" Si Lei God Lord was furious; he didn’t revert the landscape himself because he was a Semi-Immortal, but why couldn’t those cultivators beneath the level of Semi-Immortal restore it!

The secret realm left by the Time Immortal ah, it’s very likely to contain Immortal Artifacts related to time! If he were to possess a Time Immortal Artifact, who among the Semi-Immortals could be his match?

He had recently heard some good news that might lead him to find Qinglian Mountain.

"It is said that the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect houses historical maps of the Central Continent from all dynasties; they surely have the location of Qinglian Mountain!"

Si Lei God Lord left the Fairy Palace and headed straight for the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

"Is this the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect? They sure picked a nice place." Si Lei God Lord saw that the sect was situated by a mountain and water, in a beautiful environment, and he even felt like living there.

As a Semi-Immortal, his whereabouts were a mystery; the sect had no idea he had arrived.

He walked into the Scripture Pavilion, where books were piled up like mountains; it would be impossible to find anything about Qinglian Mountain quickly.

The cultivator guarding the Scripture Pavilion was a bit interesting to watch, but that was about it.

"Just at the Early Tribulation Stage." Si Lei God Lord casually laughed, and even as he walked past the old guardian of the Scripture Pavilion, the elder didn’t notice him.

In the Great Qian Period, the Early Tribulation Stage would barely qualify to be his subordinate.

However, since it was still the Transcendence Tribulation Stage, Si Lei God Lord didn’t dare to make a big commotion and, not willing to search with his Spiritual Sense, had to flip through the books one by one.

When he reached the fourth floor, he noticed some commotion below.

"Master, do you recognize this iron lump?" Sect Master Kong, holding an Immortal Gold Box, went to the old man guarding the Scripture Pavilion, Jin Wenzhang, the Supreme Elder of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect.

Fang Jin, Lu Yang, and Meng Jingzhou followed behind him.

"What is this thing?" The Supreme Elder examined the Immortal Gold Box, knocking and tapping it, and even when he injected spiritual essence into the box, he got repelled.

"You don’t recognize it either?" Sect Master Kong could not identify or figure out how to use the Immortal Gold Box and had sought the Supreme Elder; he hadn’t expected that even the Supreme Elder would not recognize it.

"This iron lump, together with Lu Martial Nephew, and Meng Martial Nephew, was accidentally found on an ancient map from the Great Qian Period. The map indicated that something was buried in Qinglian Mountain Range. It was Xiao Jin, who reads a lot, who recognized that the Qinglian Mountain of the Great Qian Period is the current Tianhe Plain, and that’s how they found this Immortal Gold Box."

"It seems you’ve not wasted your time soaking up knowledge in the Scripture Pavilion, you little guy." The Supreme Elder chuckled, stroking his beard. He had a very favorable impression of this junior.

Qinglian Mountain!

Upon hearing the familiar term, Si Lei God Lord immediately became agitated and inadvertently leaked his presence.

"Who’s there!" The Supreme Elder raised his head and roared like an enraged old lion, unable to believe that someone had dared to sneak into the Scripture Pavilion right under his watch!

Roar—

Buddhist Lion Roar!

"This object should be mine by right!"

Si Lei God Lord was equally furious; after searching for years and failing to find the location, to think that a junior had found it and even taken the Time Immortal Artifact that was meant for him!

Unforgivable!

Si Lei God Lord transformed his hand into golden thunder, reaching out to grab the Immortal Gold Box; the Supreme Elder, however, had no intention of letting him have his way.

"Tianqing!"

The Supreme Elder bellowed in anger, and a metallic dragon with a gleaming luster burst forth from the ground. The dragon raised its head high and directly topped the Si Lei God Lord out of the Scripture Pavilion!

If they were to fight in the Scripture Pavilion, no matter who won, the Scripture Pavilion would be destroyed in an instant!

"Everyone, retreat!" Sect Master Kong knew that a battle at the Tribulation-Passing Level was not something he could handle. All he could do now was to have his disciples quickly leave the sect to avoid being affected!

Si Lei God Lord didn’t regard the Supreme Elder as a concern at all.

"Thunder as heavy as mountains!"

Thunder roared and lightning flashed, and in an instant, the entire Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was engulfed in thunder, with thick as water jars thunderbolts rampaging through the sect, leaving huge pits upon striking the ground and even causing the land to crack, the intermittent flashes lighting up Si Lei God Lord’s countenance in an especially daunting way.

He possessed the outline of a Tribulation Dao Fruit. As long as the Yingtian Immortal remained absent, he was the embodiment of thunder, the nemesis of all cultivators!

"A Semi-Immortal?!" The Supreme Elder instantly recognized that this kind of thunder could not be unleashed by someone at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage; it must be the power of a Dao Fruit Outline.

"So what if it’s a Semi-Immortal? Even the Half-Immortals of Great Qian don’t dare to run wild within the bounds of Great Xia!" The Supreme Elder, despite knowing the gap between them, showed no fear at all. He too had a fierce temper when he was young and would often get into fights with those who gave assignments.

The Supreme Elder grew taller against the wind, transforming into a giant nine thousand zhang high, with the metallic dragon entwined around his arms.

The giant’s feet stomped on the earth, his head towered into the thunder, holding a massive hammer, he charged at Si Lei God Lord to bring it smashing down!

"Law of Heaven and Earth, Heaven and Earth Flipping!"

This one strike would flatten even a hundred thousand mountains!

"Ignorant youngster, you do not recognize the might of heaven!" Si Lei God Lord looked on coldly at the Supreme Elder. What use was increasing one’s size? Without sufficient realm, no technique could compensate.

In front of the outline of a Tribulation Dao Fruit, before the might of heaven, everything was useless.

"Nine Heavens Demon Sweeping Divine Thunder."

Nine layers of the vast sky appeared, each layer higher than the last, with the Demon Sweeping Divine Thunder sweeping mightily across, cleansing the world of its filth and demons.

And who was deemed a demon, was decided solely by the Si Lei God Lord.

"You are the demon."

Thus he decreed, as if he had ordained the identity of the Supreme Elder, with the Nine Heavens Demon Sweeping Divine Thunder locking onto the Supreme Elder, nine different types of Divine Thunders fell one after another!

Lu Yang was reciting nervously from the "Immortal’s Quotations," the words of the Time Immortal.

According to Fairy Eternity, as long as one could recite the incantation correctly, one could use the Immortal Gold Box. If the Supreme Elder had the help of the Immortal Gold Box, he would certainly be able to contend with Si Lei God Lord.

With the second to last Divine Thunder falling and the Supreme Elder seemingly unable to resist, Lu Yang gritted his teeth and directly threw the Immortal Gold Box over, blocking the Divine Thunder.

He recited the final phrase of the Time Immortal from the "Immortal’s Quotations" in the Ancient Language.

"The Great Dao is boundless, but time alone is supreme."

The Immortal Gold Box surface instantly lit up with various mystical patterns, imbued with the power of time, drawing in anyone who glanced at it, unable to extricate themselves.

A burly figure emerged from the Immortal Gold Box.

"Finally able to come out."

The last Divine Thunder fell, striking the burly man.

"Who the hell is striking me!"

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, upon seeing the burly figure, were stunned in unison, rubbing their eyes, almost thinking it was an illusion.

"Celestial Master Guiyuan?"

Fairy Eternity, upon seeing the patterns lighting up on the Immortal Gold Box, finally remembered the purpose of this box.

"This is a box made by the Time Immortal while studying the Time Dao Fruit. The flow of time inside the box is different from the outside; one day of Cultivation inside it is equivalent to a year in the outside world."

Just as Lu Yang was about to nod in admiration of how incredible the box was, he suddenly realized something was amiss.

What’s the use of this damn box!

Chapter 1075: 1074: The Stubborn Celestial Master Guiyuan

"Fairy, what use is this box?"

"Of course it’s useless, if it were useful, why would Time Immortal bury it?"

"He just felt the box was fairly useless, destroying it would be a pity, so he placed it where no one could reach, and look, he set up many restrictions in that secret realm."

"So the Ancestor is..."

"I guess he was being bull-headed, not following the rules, forcibly entered the secret realm, broke the restrictions, and triggered some kind of fail-safe, getting sucked into the box."

"..."

Lu Yang looked bewildered. Hadn’t Eldest Senior Sister mentioned that Celestial Master Guiyuan had vanished more than eighty-thousand years ago, without leaving a trace? Was it true that relying on his ability, he barged into the secret realm and ended up confined?

...

"Who’s there!" Si Lei God Lord watched warily as the burly man walked out of the Immortal Gold Box.

Withstanding the Divine Thunder and only getting his hair tips singed, this man’s strength was definitely no small matter!

"You strike me and then ask who I am?" Celestial Master Guiyuan immediately flared up with anger.

He had been trapped by this damned box for over two hundred years, thinking that he had finally been freed, only to be struck by lightning the moment he took a step out. Fortunately, he was physically robust, or else if it had been anyone else, they would have been reduced to ashes!

"You are the Celestial Master Guiyuan from Seek Dao Sect!" Si Lei God Lord remembered. The appearance of the three Ancestors of Seek Dao Sect was no secret, and during the celebration ceremonies, statues of the three Ancestors were even brought out for worship.

He was also a Semi-Immortal, quite active during the Great Yu Late Period, and after the establishment of Great Xia, he was one of the founders of the Five Great Immortal Sects. His status was not below that of Si Lei God Lord’s own.

"What is your relationship with Time Immortal, and why were you confined in the box!"

"Enough with the nonsense, just stand still and take my punch!" Celestial Master Guiyuan didn’t care about Si Lei God Lord’s identity, nor the current situation.

His reasoning was simple: I was struck without warning and now stand still and take a punch from me. Let’s level the playing field first before talking about anything else.

Celestial Master Guiyuan, speaking thus, sprang forth like a Flood Dragon across the sky, throwing a punch at Si Lei God Lord. His fist seemed to burn, bursting forth with endless rosy light!

How could Si Lei God Lord honestly stand still and take a beating? From his palm burst endless grey-white thunderous light, and with a push of his palm, he shattered space, as quick as lightning.

Upon fist meeting palm, with a boom, the thunder was smashed to pieces, turning into a scorching blaze, amidst which Si Lei God Lord was knocked flying!

Si Lei God Lord was in shock; as soon as he touched Celestial Master Guiyuan’s fist, he knew he had underestimated his opponent. The man’s body was terrifyingly hard, not something he could confront head-on!

Even a Body Cultivator at the Pass Tribulation Peak shouldn’t possess such a physique. How did he achieve this? Was it the power of his Dao Fruit Outline?

"Fairy, what’s going on with Ancestor Guiyuan?" asked Lu Yang.

According to Si Chen God Lord’s account, the Great Qian Dynasty harnessed the Power of Faith to create four Dao Fruit Outlines corresponding to the Four Ancient Immortals.

Si Chen God Lord and Si Lei God Lord were two of them. Si Lei God Lord possessed the Tribulation Dao Fruit Outline and, apart from the Dao Fruit Outline, he was also a Pass Tribulation Peak cultivator.

How could such a person be sent flying by Ancestor Guiyuan’s punch?

What was Ancestor Guiyuan’s Dao Fruit Outline?

"If I had known I would meet Ancestor Guiyuan, I would have inquired about the Ancestor’s Dao Fruit Outline from Eldest Senior Sister." Lu Yang felt a tinge of regret.

"As it happens, I’ve heard our old ancestor mention Ancestor Guiyuan’s Dao Fruit Outline." Meng Jingzhou came over.

"What is it?"

"The Unity Dao Fruit Outline, with the ability to merge anything into one entity."

"That explains it." Fairy Eternity felt clarified after hearing Meng Jingzhou’s description.

"No wonder the lad’s physique is so peculiar. He has integrated his flesh with Immortal Gold, clearly a power of the Unity Dao Fruit Outline."

Having merged flesh and Immortal Gold into one, one could only imagine how terrifyingly strong Celestial Master Guiyuan’s body had become.

Lu Yang took a deep breath. No wonder Ancestor Guiyuan was so audacious; he truly had formidable abilities.

"You still dare to block!" Celestial Master Guiyuan was displeased. This one was most dishonorable; you strike me and can’t even say sorry, and when I punch you, you dare to block.

Doesn’t reason apply anymore?

In the eyes of Si Lei God Lord, Celestial Master Guiyuan had gone mad. "You’re attacking me, can’t I block it?"

The two engaged in battle once again.

Taking advantage of their battle, Supreme Elder Jin Wenzhang unlocked the Law of Heaven and Earth, reverted to his original form, and took the opportunity to slip away.

"Are you alright, Ancestor?" Sect Master Kong rushed forward to support the Supreme Elder, helping him back to the Scripture Pavilion. He had been struck by lightning in the sky for so long that now he could barely stand.

Under the guidance of Sect Master Kong, the disciples of the sect finished evacuating.

The Scripture Pavilion had a defensive array method and was the safest place in the sect.

Fang Jin, Lu Yang, and Meng Jingzhou were also very concerned about the Supreme Elder’s condition.

"I’m fine, just expended too much energy earlier, a little rest should do the job." Having said that, the Supreme Elder stuffed an elixir into his mouth.

After regaining his strength, the Supreme Elder reflected on how these events had occurred: "What exactly happened? How come all of a sudden there are two Semi-Immortals here, one of whom is Celestial Master Guiyuan? Our sect will have to be rebuilt."

Gazing upon the pockmarked, damaged sect, the Supreme Elder felt a heart-wrenching pain. As the protector of the sect, his inability to safeguard it was a grave dereliction of duty.

"Do not panic, Ancestor. Our sect has bought insurance in advance."

"Insurance? Who bought it?" The Supreme Elder was surprised but took this as good news.

"It was Xiao Jin’s foresight, he purchased the insurance in advance."

"Nicely done." Learning about the insurance, the Supreme Elder’s mood visibly improved. Turning to Fang Jin, he asked, "How did you think to buy insurance?"

In the two-some thousand years since their sect was established, they had never encountered such a disaster. Generally, buying insurance for the sect would be a pure loss.

"Hang on, isn’t the appearance of two Semi-Immortals fighting in our sect because of the Immortal Gold Box you brought back, young man?"

The Supreme Elder suddenly realized that both, Si Lei God Lord and Celestial Master Guiyuan, had become involved because of the Immortal Gold Box.

Fang Jin did not know what facial expression to make. He had bought insurance, and then he brought back the Immortal Gold Box to the sect, which led to the battle between the Semi-Immortals.

Somehow, this didn’t seem right?

"Explain where this box came from in detail?"

Fang Jin recounted truthfully that he set out with Lu Yang to resolve conflicts, inadvertently collected the ancient map, and found the Immortal Gold Box.

"So you’re saying finding the Immortal Gold Box was purely by chance?"

The Supreme Elder believed it was a coincidence. Aside from the fact that Fang Jin had no reason to conspire against the sect, simply finding the Immortal Gold Box was quite fortuitous.

Fang Jin looked lost in thought. Could it be that Dai Bufan mastered divination, saw through the letter sent to him that Fang Jin would bring bad luck, and thus suggested he buy insurance?

In the sky, Celestial Master Guiyuan was still battling Si Lei God Lord.

Knowing he couldn’t directly confront Celestial Master Guiyuan, Si Lei God Lord opened up distance between them.

"So you’ve got a tough body, have you? I’d like to see just how tough it can be!"

The thick five-colored divine thunder pierced through the heavens and earth, shattering the void as if giant stars were falling, tilting the sky and slanting the earth, causing tremors within ten thousand miles!

Crack—

A bolt of divine thunder struck the Scripture Pavilion, causing its defensive great formation to fracture, uncertain how much more it could withstand.

The Supreme Elder’s eyes twitched at the sight; had that thunder struck him, his chances of survival would be slim.

"You think it’s impressive just because you can summon thunder!" Celestial Master Guiyuan scoffed.

Just as the five-colored divine thunder descended, Celestial Master Guiyuan activated the Unity Dao Fruit Outline, merging the entire space within a hundred miles with the five-colored divine thunder completely!

This meant that the power of this bolt of five-colored divine thunder was evenly distributed across this hundred-mile space, reducing the thunder that struck Celestial Master Guiyuan to nearly nothing!

Chapter 1076: 1075: Incarnate as Lightning

Celestial Master Guiyuan’s flesh had fused with Immortal Gold, making it indestructible. The dispersed Divine Thunders had no effect when they fell on him.

But these were Five-Colored Divine Thunders, and even after dispersal, their power remained extremely terrifying. Ordinary buildings couldn’t withstand such Divine Thunders; within a hundred miles, save for the Scripture Pavilion, all was reduced to scorched earth!

"Divine Thunder descends from the ninth heaven!"

"Boundless Thunder Cloud!"

"Dutian Divine Thunder!"

The Si Lei God Lord, refusing to believe in curses, released different forms of Thunder Tribulations. Their patterns varied, but each had astonishing power, beyond what those at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage could contend with.

Facing different Thunder Tribulations, Celestial Master Guiyuan still used the Outline of the Unity Dao Fruit to disperse them. No matter how powerful the tribulations were, it was futile, enraging the Si Lei God Lord to the point of grinding his teeth in fury.

Since direct attacks with Thunder Tribulations were ineffective, it was time to try a different method.

"Heavenly Thunder Embodiment!"

With a light shout from the Si Lei God Lord, his form transformed, and his flesh entirely turned into lightning.

The Si Lei God Lord, in his lightning form, moved with inconceivable speed. In an instant, he was in front of Celestial Master Guiyuan, who, without a second thought, threw a punch.

But that punch passed directly through the Si Lei God Lord, hitting nothing but air!

"Haha, I’d like to see how you’re going to hit me!"

The Si Lei God Lord laughed heartily. In this state, he feared no tangible attack. No matter how sturdy Celestial Master Guiyuan’s body was, if he couldn’t touch him, any move would be useless.

At this moment, he was like the Thunder Deity patrolling on behalf of heaven, with the life and death of all living beings in his grasp, controlling all calamities.

"The boy from Great Qian is quite clever, knowing to transform into lightning," Immortal Fairy Eternity laughed.

"The Yingtian Immortal has used this move a few times. It’s slippery and elusive, like an eel. He bragged to me when he figured out this move."

"How then shall this move be countered?" Lu Yang asked, watching as it seemed Ancestor Guiyuan was falling into a disadvantage.

"Blow it up a couple of times and it’ll behave."

"..."

Ancestor Guiyuan can’t use that method, can he?

The sophistication of the Heavenly Thunder Embodiment went beyond this. Lightning is tangible yet intangible, penetrating everywhere. The Si Lei God Lord circled around Celestial Master Guiyuan, attacking intermittently with lightning.

Even if Celestial Master Guiyuan were stronger, he couldn’t possibly defend against all angles and block all of these strikes.

The Outline of the Unity Dao Fruit also couldn’t merge the Si Lei God Lord, in his Heavenly Thunder Embodiment form, into the space. That was something only a true Dao Fruit could achieve.

"I patrol on behalf of heaven; how could you possibly defeat me? Hand over the Immortal Box quickly!" the Si Lei God Lord arrogantly said. In his state as lightning, he indeed had the right to say such words.

"Palm Thunder!" Celestial Master Guiyuan flipped his palm, and a golden thunder appeared in the center of his palm, causing the Si Lei God Lord to want to burst out laughing.

Was he actually planning to attack his own self, possessing the outline of the Tribulation Dao Fruit, with lightning?

However, Celestial Master Guiyuan made an unexpected change.

"Unity!"

The Palm Thunder merged with Celestial Master Guiyuan, and he too transformed into lightning!

"I don’t believe I can’t touch you, you damned thing!"

Celestial Master Guiyuan struck with a slap, and the enormous palm nearly fanned the Si Lei God Lord into unconsciousness.

Both now in the form of lightning, they could finally touch each other.

"Ignorant and laughable; do you not know that I can control lightning?"

Though the slap from Celestial Master Guiyuan came unexpectedly, the Si Lei God Lord quickly realized the advantage was still his.

If Celestial Master Guiyuan had become lightning, then he was now within his realm of control!

With a mere thought, he could manipulate Celestial Master Guiyuan’s body at will, making him do anything he wished, even suicide!

"Stop!"

With a single word, the Si Lei God Lord pinned Celestial Master Guiyuan in place, unable to lift even a finger.

Just when the Si Lei God Lord was about to make Celestial Master Guiyuan hand over the Immortal Gold Box, he heard Ancestor Guiyuan bellow.

"Unity!"

The Si Lei God Lord’s pupils dilated suddenly. He found his body moving uncontrollably, flying towards Celestial Master Guiyuan and colliding directly into him, merging into one, with only their heads yet to merge completely!

One body with two heads, it looked bizarre and terrifying.

In this way, if the Si Lei God Lord wanted to harm Celestial Master Guiyuan, he would be harming himself.

Si Lei God Lord had never seen such a situation before and didn’t know how to control the body; Celestial Master Guiyuan seized the opportunity to take control and punched Si Lei God Lord in the head, knocking out two teeth.

"I told you to run, now let’s see how you’ll run this time!" Celestial Master Guiyuan said viciously, not looking the least bit benevolent.

This could not go on, Si Lei God Lord’s mind raced, seeking a way to break the deadlock.

His Dao Fruit Outline was very integrated, so why didn’t he merge the two from the beginning—was there some flaw in this move?

"Unbind!"

Si Lei God Lord unbound the "Heavenly Thunder Embodiment," reverting to his physical form, and as he had predicted, it forcibly disengaged the Unification.

There was a flaw in Celestial Master Guiyuan’s ability to Unify with a person—if both were in physical forms, it required mutual agreement to Unify due to the rigidity of physical bodies, making the process restrictive.

If it wasn’t in physical form, but rather a form like the Heavenly Thunder Embodiment with a loose composition, then forced Unification was possible.

Seeing Si Lei God Lord revert to his original form, Celestial Master Guiyuan also stopped embodying lightning to act as a living target.

"You want the Thunder Tribulation to scatter across a hundred miles radius, well then, what if I attack you everywhere within a hundred miles radius!"

"Ruishui Catastrophe!"

Although the Si Lei God Lord’s divine title was "Si Lei," what he possessed was the Tribulation Dao Fruit Outline, controlling not just Thunder Tribulation, but Heavenly Tribulation.

Ruishui surged, flooding the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. Seeing this, the Supreme Elder did not stay hiding in the Scripture Pavilion any longer and immediately dragged everyone out towards the outside of the sect.

Preoccupied with battling Celestial Master Guiyuan, Si Lei God Lord had no capacity to pay attention to others.

Facing the Ruishui, which could not lift a feather, Celestial Master Guiyuan merged with the earth, allowing the Ruishui to wash over him.

The ground bulged as a giant emerged with the blue sky overhead and mountains on its back, making the already towering figure of Celestial Master Guiyuan appear even more massive.

The Ruishui that engulfed the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect only reached up to Celestial Master Guiyuan’s knees.

Semi-Immortals in combat, a devastation of heaven and earth.

Even though Celestial Master Guiyuan had the advantage, Si Lei God Lord was no easy opponent. After thousands of exchanges and when both had nearly exhausted their strength, Si Lei God Lord finally realized he could not defeat Celestial Master Guiyuan and made a hasty escape.

"To think of running away without apologizing, what a lucky idea!" Celestial Master Guiyuan said, perking up with energy. How could this man not understand decorum? It was one thing to not acknowledge defeat after losing, but not even apologizing first!

"Are you insane? Isn’t it enough that I don’t want the box anymore?"

Driven to desperation, Si Lei God Lord once again transformed into lightning and quickly distanced himself from Celestial Master Guiyuan; Celestial Master Guiyuan couldn’t force a Unification if the distance was too great.

At that moment, Lu Yang noticed that in the path of the pursuit, Yun Mengmeng was hastily approaching.

Initially, Yun Mengmeng had heard that the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was in a remote and hard-to-find location, worried she might not be able to find it, but upon nearing the area, she saw that the battleground was indeed the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect without a doubt, and headed straight there without hesitation.

Lu Yang quickly sent a telepathic message to Yun Mengmeng, "Sister Mengmeng, stop the person flying towards you!"

"No problem," Yun Mengmeng didn’t expect to be entrusted with an important task immediately upon arrival and was determined to give it her all.

Boom——

Yun Mengmeng self-detonated, something Si Lei God Lord had never seen before, blowing apart his lightning embodiment and seriously injuring his true form.

Celestial Master Guiyuan, exhilarated, shouted and rushed forward, "Fellow Daoist, well done! Help me catch this scoundrel!"

Yun Mengmeng was startled; why was another one flying towards her?

Boom——

Following the orders of the Second-in-command, Yun Mengmeng was to stop anyone who flew at her.

Celestial Master Guiyuan didn’t expect Yun Mengmeng to self-detonate a second time, and was caught off guard. Which side are you on, fellow Daoist?

He had just gone through a great battle and had little energy left, so how could he withstand another self-detonation?

"Fellow Daoist, you..."

Boom boom boom——

Seeing that Celestial Master Guiyuan still had energy, Yun Mengmeng continued to self-detonate, knocking Celestial Master Guiyuan unconscious.

Soon after, Yun Mengmeng was holding Celestial Master Guiyuan in one hand and Si Lei God Lord in the other, presenting them to Lu Yang for commendation.

"Second-in-command, just like you instructed, I’ve captured them all!"

Chapter 1077: 1076: Is This Called Extremely Low Risk?

"Hey, hey, hey, Sister Mengmeng, don’t just go saying things; I never asked you to do that." Seeing Yun Mengmeng running over with Celestial Master Guiyuan, Lu Yang instinctively denied any involvement.

"Ah? Did I grab the wrong person? Second-in-command, don’t you want this one?"

Lu Yang pointed at Celestial Master Guiyuan, and Yun Mengmeng made as if to throw Celestial Master Guiyuan aside, frightening Lu Yang so much that he hurriedly stopped her.

"Don’t throw, don’t throw, I want him."

"So, I didn’t grab the wrong one after all," Yun Mengmeng said delightedly, feeling that she had finally managed to be of some help to the Second-in-command and very accomplished.

"Junior Brother Lu, who is this?" Fang Jin cautiously inquired about Yun Mengmeng’s identity. He had seen everything—the predecessor had knocked out both Celestial Master Guiyuan and Si Lei God Lord.

Although two Half-Immortals had exhausted themselves greatly, knocking them out didn’t seem like something achievable even in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"She’s my boss." Yun Mengmeng declared proudly, then paused, feeling that something was off.

Oh, I misspoke. Chief Housekeeper is the boss.

"Second-in-command is my old..."

"Fellow sect member, we’re fellow sect members," Lu Yang quickly interjected. Sister Mengmeng, you really do dare say anything.

"Fellow sect members?" Fang Jin looked puzzledly at Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng. Would fellow sect members call each other "Second-in-command"?

You all really aren’t from a bandit den?

Fang Jin didn’t dwell on this too much, capturing Si Lei, the main culprit, was better than anything else, no need to worry about Si Lei seeking revenge anymore.

Although their Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect was just the victim and supposedly had nothing to do with Si Lei, judging by Si Lei’s behavior, he might hold a grudge against their sect after failing to defeat Celestial Master Guiyuan.

"The Third Elder said you two can return to the sect, no need to do unpaid work at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect anymore," Yun Mengmeng recalled the purpose of this trip; capturing two Half-Immortals was just a side task, the main goal was to call them back.

"Speaking of which, where is the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect? The Third Elder said the scenery of this sect is beautiful, with stunning views all year round."

Yun Mengmeng, resting her hand on her forehead and standing on tiptoe, looked around, unable to find a place that could be described as "beautiful scenery."

Lu Yang silently turned his head, looking toward the ruins of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect’s sect ground, inundated by Ruishui River, with mountains collapsed and earth cracked, feeling that even if he and Old Meng wanted to volunteer here, there would be no chance.

Moreover, now that they had found Ancestor Guiyuan and captured Si Lei God Lord, with these two major tasks at hand, they needed to return to their own sect anyway.

"Sister Mengmeng, let’s go back."

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou reluctantly said their goodbyes to the Supreme Elder, Sect Master Kong, and Fang Jin.

"Senior Jin, Senior Kong, Senior Brother Fang, we take our leave."

Sect Master Kong watched Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou depart, feeling very guilty. The two had almost met with misfortune in their sect; if something had actually happened, he wouldn’t know how to face Third Zhang.

"Master, what should we do now?"

Sect Master Kong thought for a bit and said, "Let’s first console the sect members. Leave the sect ruins untouched for now; I’ll send someone to inform the Jingzhou Branch to come here and assess our losses."

"The sect hasn’t been maintained for a long time; it’s a good opportunity to renovate it."

Knowing that he wouldn’t have to pay out of his pocket and that insurance would cover it, Sect Master Kong felt much better, as if he was just shutting down for a few days for renovations.

"It’s just the books in the Scripture Pavilion that are a bit troublesome. After being soaked in Ruishui, I don’t know how many are still readable."

"That’s alright." The Supreme Elder said cheerfully, "Of the three of us, you read the least. Chap Fang and I have memorized all the books from the Scripture Pavilion, so even if some books are unreadable, it won’t matter."

...

At the Jingzhou Branch of the Land Money Trade Association.

Upon hearing his deputy’s report, the Branch Head’s mind went blank, immobile like a statue, and it took him a while to come back to his senses.

"You mean there was a battle involving Half-Immortals at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect?!"

He shouted incredulously, scarcely daring to imagine the chain reactions this event would trigger. The Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had numerous buildings and unique landscapes, and though its value was unknown, it certainly would be more expensive than Sword Tower!

Sword Tower only had one building!

The list of failed investments was about to get one longer.

"How could this be possible, how could the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect have such an incident, weren’t the risk assessments for the seven elders showing extremely low risk?"

"And those seven immortals, where are they!"

The branch president immediately pulled out a gleaming machete, ready to confront the seven elders, but the deputy quickly stopped him.

"President, you forgot, the headquarters mentioned that several of their seasoned assessors had retired, forcibly transferring all seven elders away."

Only then did the branch president remember that all seven elders had gone to the headquarters, and being a branch president, he quickly calmed down to analyze the situation.

Damn, the insurance was bought just half a month ago, and the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had an incident?

Insurance fraud?

"Explain in detail, what exactly happened to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect!"

The deputy reported the incident truthfully, and after listening, the branch president could not find any suspicious details; it all seemed like a mere coincidence.

One side was Heavenly Venerate Si Lei from Great Qian, and the other was Celestial Master Guiyuan, who had disappeared for eighty thousand years; the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect simply didn’t have the capability to find these legendary figures.

If they had such capabilities, they wouldn’t need to commit insurance fraud.

"Just an accident?"

"Forget it, let’s have someone assess the damages to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect first."

A day later, the branch president held a Recording Orb, which recorded the dreadful conditions of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. Viewing it almost gave the branch president a heart attack.

It was utterly destroyed, not a trace left.

Thunder purification, Ruishui River cleansing, the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect couldn’t even find a single intact item!

"President, should we compensate?" the deputy asked quietly.

The branch president’s body trembled, and he squeezed out a word through clenched teeth, "Compensate—"

"Ah, wait, we have an accountability system, the Shu State Chamber of Commerce compensated the Sword Tower without pursuing accountability, because they couldn’t locate the parties involved in the conflict."

"This time is different, the responsibility lies with Heavenly Venerate Si Lei, a Great Qian Half-Immortal’s savings should be enough to cover the compensation, right?"

The deputy had to remind on the side, "President, it’s true that the Great Qian Half-Immortal possesses Spirit Stones, but don’t forget that the Laurel Fairy Palace has captured the Demon-Subduing God Lord and the Demon-Removing God Lord of Great Qian, and according to the intel they provided, their savings are all stored in the Fairy Palace."

"Regarding the location of the Fairy Palace, they’ve been placed under a restriction, they can’t speak about it, and soul searching is useless, no one knows where the Fairy Palace is."

"Heavenly Venerate Si Lei might be facing a similar situation."

The branch president’s stomach ballooned with rage several times, but he finally slumped down, although it wasn’t good news, everything the deputy said was correct.

The deputy was level-headed and always made the right judgments; it seemed his position was truly well-deserved.

"President, I have an idea about this incident."

"Let’s hear it."

"Last time the Sword Tower had an incident, Junior Brother Lu was there; this time the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect had an incident, and Junior Brother Lu was also present. Do you think it’s possible that it’s related to Junior Brother Lu?"

The branch president looked at the deputy, his eyes expressing some surprise, as though saying how could you think of such a thing.

"Tom, now I must say a few words to you."

"Please do."

"We need evidence when dealing with matters. The reports show that in both incidents, Junior Brother Lu was merely a spectator. Be it a battle of Immortals or Half-immortals, none relate to Junior Brother Lu."

"Especially this time, you also saw the report, finding the Immortal Gold Box was purely coincidental."

"But President, we must also consider probabilities."

The branch president hissed.

"...Now that you mention it, it does seem somewhat reasonable."

Chapter 1078: 1077: Elder Brother Dai, we brothers have gathered the three ancestors of the Seek Dao Sect for you!

Lu Yang and his group transformed into streaks of light and rushed forward, passing through the gate, through the bamboo forest, straight to the Task Hall.

"Sister Mengmeng said Eldest Senior Sister went to visit a friend and isn’t at the Sect now, so we can only report to Elder Brother Dai first," she said.

The meandering stream flowed through the bamboo forest, where River Spirit, utterly bored, blew bubbles, showing only half of its head.

It yawned and from the corner of its eye saw Lu Yang and his group fly past, spotting the person carried on Lu Yang’s shoulder, its mouth agape for quite a while.

It rubbed its eyes vigorously, but by the time it looked again, Lu Yang had already flown away.

"Did I see a ghost?"

...

In the innermost room of the Task Hall, the acting master, Dai Bufan, finished handling the last task report.

"All done~"

Dai Bufan stretched lazily, feeling content as his official duties were completed, marking the most relaxing time of his day after a hard day’s work.

He used a magic skill to wash the ink from his brush, twirling it between his fingers.

"I wonder how things are going at Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, those two little rascals didn’t cause any trouble, did they?"

Recalling the recent issue of *Cultivation Monthly*, which praised more than ten cultivators from Great Xia for taking the initiative to restore the terrain, Dai Bufan knew it must have been Lu Yang’s doing.

However, he was pleased with this, as it was a good thing.
