"Though there should be no major issue, Junior Brother Fang Jin also mentioned in his reply that he had bought insurance."

Dai Bufan pulled out Fang Jin’s reply, which contained only two words—bought it.

Even more concise than his own letter.

"Good that he bought it."

"Elder Brother Dai, are you there?" Lu Yang’s polite knock sounded from outside the door.

"Come in."

Lu Yang entered the room with a beaming smile, puzzling Dai Bufan, "Weren’t you and Junior Brother Meng at Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect for a two-month study? It’s not even a full month; how come you’re back already?"

"Elder Brother Dai, we have great news! Overwhelmed with excitement, Old Meng and I came to report the joyous news to you!" Lu Yang said excitedly, his eyes sparkling.

"What great news?" Dai Bufan toyed with his brush as he wondered about the good news brought by their trip.

"Old Meng, bring him in." Lu Yang called out to the doorway.

"Right away."

Soon, Meng Jingzhou entered the room, dragging a mat with a burly, unconscious man lying on it.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou said in unison to Dai Bufan, "Elder Brother Dai, we have found the third ancestor of the Seek Dao Sect for you!"

"Gui... Ancestor Guiyuan?!"

Seeing Celestial Master Guiyuan, who had been missing for eighty thousand years, appear alive before him, Dai Bufan was so shocked he flung his brush away and leaned back, crashing to the ground with his chair with a loud clang.

He scrambled over the table to check on Ancestor Guiyuan’s condition.

Alive, but unconscious.

"Why is he unconscious?"

At that moment, Yun Mengmeng dragged in Si Lei God Lord and said, "The Second-in-command told me to blast him."

"I clearly told you to stop him!"

Yun Mengmeng stood firm, "But this is the only move I know; other moves wouldn’t stop him."

After spending time together, Yun Mengmeng had grown smarter and was able to understand the implied meaning in Lu Yang’s words, equating stopping someone with blasting them.

Hearing this, Dai Bufan felt his blood rush to his head, his brain surged with blood, his steps became unsteady, his body swaying, Lu Yang rushed to support Dai Bufan without bothering to discuss the situation with Yun Mengmeng further.

"Elder Brother Dai, are you alright?"

"Don’t call me Elder Brother, you are the Elder Brother, no, you are my ancestor."

He had previously overheard Daoist Xiantian saying that as soon as he met them, Lu Yang and his companion had hung him up and threatened him with a whip.

Now, upon meeting him again, Lu Yang had knocked out Celestial Master Guiyuan.

This man was truly a formidable ancestor.

Celestial Master Guiyuan, who had been sleeping on a straw mat, stirred. He groaned a few times, opened his eyes, and locked gazes with the curious Yun Mengmeng scrutinizing him, startling him into jumping up defensively against Yun Mengmeng.

"Whose side are you really on!"

"I’m on the side of the Second-in-command."

"Who is the Second-in-command?"

Hearing about the Second-in-command, Celestial Master Guiyuan grew even more wary. If Yun Mengmeng was this powerful, what kind of person was the Second-in-command?

Meng Jingzhou stepped forward to smooth things over and lighten the mood, "It’s all a misunderstanding, Ancestor. We’re all on the same side here."

"Same side?" Celestial Master Guiyuan looked skeptically at the people in the room: Si Lei God Lord, Yun Mengmeng, Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou, Dai Bufan.

Eventually, his eyes settled on the Identity Jade of Lu Yang and his companions.

"The jade of the Dao Seeking Sect, you two are disciples of my Dao Seeking Sect?"

"Ancestor, this is the Dao Seeking Sect," they replied.

"This is the Dao Seeking Sect?" Celestial Master Guiyuan skeptically released his Spiritual Sense to inspect the surroundings.

"It really is the Dao Seeking Sect, but how has it changed so much in just over two hundred years?"

This place resembled the Dao Seeking Sect he remembered, but many details were different.

It was up to Lu Yang to explain, "Ancestor, you entered an Immortal Box crafted by the Time Immortal. In the box, a day passes for every year outside. You spent over two hundred years inside, while eighty thousand years have passed in the outside world."

"Eighty thousand years?!" Celestial Master Guiyuan was shocked and quickly pulled out the Immortal Gold Box, learning its effects, he wished he hadn’t barged right in.

"You seem a bit conflicted."

Celestial Master Guiyuan was indeed conflicted, "I guess I’ll keep this box. It doesn’t seem very useful, but then again, it’s a rare artifact crafted by the Time Immortal. It’d be a pity to throw it away."

Lu Yang really wanted to say that it wouldn’t be a pity to discard it. If he thought the box was rare, he could introduce him to the even rarer Time Immortal.

"Ancestor, how did you get into the Immortal Gold Box?" Meng Jingzhou asked curiously.

"At the time, I stumbled upon a Secret Realm and entered. At the end of the Secret Realm, there was a small box that seemed precious, so I wanted to take a look. The Restriction was troublesome though. I thought, ’My body is so sturdy, even if I trigger the Restriction, I won’t mind,’ so I just barged in."

Upon saying this, Celestial Master Guiyuan regretted it deeply and slapped his thigh: "If I had known this damned box was so unreasonable, consuming me as soon as I triggered the Restriction, I wouldn’t have barged in!"

"It sent me straight to eighty thousand years in the future, and as soon as I stepped out of the box, I was struck by lightning. The person who struck me didn’t even apologize. After a hard-won victory when I was about to catch up to him, I got bombed several more times!"

Celestial Master Guiyuan felt he was truly cursed.

Lu Yang hung his head, too guilty to meet Celestial Master Guiyuan’s eyes.

"Eighty thousand years, the world has changed so much, I’m afraid there’s no one left in the Dao Seeking Sect that I recognize."

Celestial Master Guiyuan said somewhat dejectedly.

"Uh, actually, our sect might still have a few people you know," Lu Yang reassured.

"Who? River Spirit?"

"River Spirit is one, and there’s also..."

Just then, the door was knocked on again.

"Elder Brother Dai, do you know where Junior Brother Gu is?"

Qi Tongtian pushed the door and entered, saw a room full of people, and felt he had come at a bad time, wanting to back out.

Lu Yang hurriedly pulled Qi Tongtian over to introduce him, "You must recognize this one, this is our sect’s founding ancestor, Daoist Xiantian!"

"Huh?"

Seeing the somewhat shy Daoist Xiantian, Celestial Master Guiyuan sank into deep doubt.

Am I in the future or have I returned to the past?

Chapter 1079: 1078: I Never Expected My Seek Dao Sect to Have 3 Semi-Immortals

Celestial Master Guiyuan felt as if he hadn’t fully woken up.

Before being trapped in the box, he had been the Ancestor of the Seek Dao Sect, the oldest among them.

How had his seniority dropped after he came out of the box?

How had Daoist Xiantian appeared?

"Wait, how come I remember that Daoist Xiantian died? I’ve been paying respects at his tomb every year, and both his appearance and cultivation level don’t match!" Celestial Master Guiyuan had a belly full of questions.

Initially, he had felt sad, thinking that there was no one he recognized in the Seek Dao Sect anymore, but lo and behold, the founder himself popped up, leaving no room for sadness.

"Daoist Xiantian faked his death. He just woke up not long ago and had to Start Over after Disbanding Cultivation to escape being pursued due to some special circumstances," Lu Yang explained, being the first to discover Daoist Xiantian.

"Hello, I am Qi Tongtian." Daoist Xiantian greeted him honestly. Celestial Master Guiyuan’s statue was still on display in the Sect’s grand hall, so without any introduction, Qi Tongtian knew this was Celestial Master Guiyuan.

"I’ve seen Ancestor Xiantian. I’m Wang Daoyi; just call me Xiao Wang from now on." Although Celestial Master Guiyuan liked proving his strength and challenging stronger opponents recklessly, he was also a cultivator who knew his etiquette.

"I heard you disappeared for eighty thousand years, where did you go?" Daoist Xiantian was very curious about Celestial Master Guiyuan’s past, after all, he was the one who truly brought the Seek Dao Sect into the ranks of the Five Great Immortal Sects.

"Uh—" Celestial Master Guiyuan felt that bragging about barging into a Secret Realm, triggering a restriction and being locked up for over two hundred years was not exactly something to be proud of.

Especially when mentioning it in front of an Ancestor.

Lu Yang saw Celestial Master Guiyuan’s awkward situation and stepped forward to explain:

"Eighty thousand years ago, Senior Guiyuan broke into an Immortal’s Secret Realm and was accidentally trapped in a rift in time. However, it turned out to be a blessing in disguise since he did not have to worry about his lifespan running out. He reappeared in the modern era, fought the Si Lei God Lord of the Great Qian Dynasty after escaping, and saved the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect from disaster. It was a heroic act indeed, but unfortunately, he was severely injured afterward and fell into a coma. We brought him back to the Sect and he has just woken up."

"Ah, right, right, that’s exactly it." Celestial Master Guiyuan nodded vigorously, thinking that the young Lu Yang was truly commendable.

"The person who struck me was the Si Lei God Lord of the Great Qian?" On hearing Lu Yang, it was only then that Celestial Master Guiyuan knew the identity of Si Lei God Lord. He had only wanted Si Lei God Lord to apologize to him when they fought.

When Celestial Master Guiyuan was blasted, Si Lei God Lord had also fallen into a coma, though he did not escape the fate of being blown up either. His physical condition was no match for Celestial Master Guiyuan, and he was still in a comatose state.

"You just mentioned that there are a few people in the Sect that I know, aside from Ancestor Xiantian. Who else is there?"

"Would you like to meet Senior River Spirit?"

"Let’s go." He and River Spirit could be considered old friends with a deep friendship.

...

"Senior River Spirit, we brothers haven’t seen each other in a while, huh." Celestial Master Guiyuan felt a warm familiarity on seeing an old acquaintance, and even the axes in River Spirit’s hands looked endearing.

River Spirit treaded over the waves, stepping out of the stream, swinging a gold and silver axe straight at him.

"Disappearing for eighty thousand years without a word, you’ve got some nerve!"

Celestial Master Guiyuan, knowing he was at fault, did not dare to fight back but only dodged the axes with an apologetic smile.

After chopping for a while and knowing he could not hit him, River Spirit simply stopped.

Seeing Celestial Master Guiyuan again, River Spirit didn’t quite know how to feel.

If anyone in the Seek Dao Sect had the best relationship with River Spirit, it had to be the third Ancestor. He had opened his Spiritual Wisdom thanks to Daoist Xiantian’s help. Celestial Master Guiyuan had been active for twenty thousand years, and Vast Sea Daoist Lord for fifty thousand years, making their interaction times quite lengthy.

He saw Daoist Xiantian steadfastly alive, Celestial Master Guiyuan disappear; although he didn’t show much outwardly, deep inside he felt melancholic, a sense of the long road of longevity with old friends departing one after another — perhaps an inescapable fate for immortals.

But lo and behold, the two who disappeared had popped up again, and now his sadness seemed unnecessary.

Seeing that Celestial Master Guiyuan and River Spirit were about to have a merry old time with drinks, not planning to leave until drunk, Lu Yang softly reminded from the side, "Senior, you haven’t met everyone yet?"

"There’s more people?" Celestial Master Guiyuan was somewhat surprised. How could there be more acquaintances of his in the Seek Dao Sect?

...

"Vast Sea, it’s not that I want to say anything, but giving your Dao companion Spirit Stones as a festival gift, what kind of idea is that?" On the Great Snow Mountain, Chasing Moon True Person sighed, shaking her head helplessly, as Vast Sea Daoist Lord still wondered why his Dao companion was upset.

Chasing Moon True Person hated this topic most and promptly activated the outline of her Seven Emotions Dao Fruit, "You talk too much—cry!"

Chasing Moon True Person got goosebumps from listening and quickly canceled the power of her Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, and Celestial Master Guiyuan returned to normal.

Meng Jingzhou and Lu Yang watched from the side, feeling that their Ancestor was not at the same level as Chasing Moon True Person.

Yun Mengmeng raised her hand to clarify her position: "Senior, you are mistaken, although I listen to the Second-in-command, I’m not from the Seek Dao Sect."

"I’m here visiting relatives."

Chapter 1080: 1079: Twin Ancestors Unification!

"Visiting relatives, Little Lu?" Celestial Master Guiyuan saw Yun Mengmeng coming all the way calling ’Second-in-command’, his first reaction was that Yun Mengmeng came to the Seek Dao Sect looking for Lu Yang.

"No, it’s Xiao Zhi, oh, her full name is Yun Zhi, she’s my good sister and also the Eldest Senior Sister of the Second-in-command, especially strong."

"Especially strong? Stronger than you?" Celestial Master Guiyuan was surprised. Lu Yang’s Eldest Senior Sister is still a disciple of the Seek Dao Sect, isn’t she? She hasn’t even reached the Elder level, how could she be considered "especially strong"?

"Stronger than you too."

"Oh, really strong?" Celestial Master Guiyuan couldn’t quite accept this statement.

He always believed that strength and realm were not absolutely related, whether one was strong or weak was something known only after clashing in battle. He had challenged Jiang Pingan, who had already become immortal, with this belief ten thousand years ago.

Regardless of the outcome of the battle, the most important thing was to stick to one’s own principles.

Ten thousand years ago, he had also wanted to challenge Meng Junzi, who had also become immortal, but unfortunately, Meng Junzi had not accepted the fight.

Although at that time, Meng Junzi insisted that he had not become immortal, but was a Semi-Immortal, he had become disheartened after losing to Jiang Pingan and stopped fighting altogether.

Celestial Master Guiyuan didn’t believe Meng Junzi’s tale at all.

If your spirit is disheartened, shouldn’t you go out, pretend to be weak and challenge others to regain your confidence? What are you doing staying at home all day? Are you the type of person, Meng Junzi?

Meng Junzi must have become an immortal and was harboring some evil plan to trap others.

Understanding this, Celestial Master Guiyuan didn’t bother with Meng Junzi anymore.

"Grandmaster, calm down, you really can’t beat the Eldest Senior Sister, even the Vast Sea Ancestral Master lost to her." Lu Yang hurriedly tried to pacify, Grandmaster Guiyuan had suffered enough from being chopped and blasted in the box, it would be terrible for something else to happen.

"You’ve formed the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit, and you can’t beat that young one called Yun Zhi?" Celestial Master Guiyuan turned and asked.

"Can’t beat her." Vast Sea Daoist Lord openly admitted. Ever since he and Ao Ya became a Daoist couple, he had come to terms with it. Being unable to defeat Yun Zhi was not something shameful; it shouldn’t be taken to heart too much.

"Where is Yun Zhi, I want to meet her."

"Xiao Zhi has gone out, but judging by the time, she should be back soon." Yun Mengmeng replied whenever asked.

Lu Yang thought to himself, Sister Mengmeng, you’re really fanning the flames here, why on Earth would you tell Grandmaster that the Eldest Senior Sister is coming back?

"Grandmaster, we still have old acquaintances to see." Lu Yang quickly changed the subject, trying to draw Celestial Master Guiyuan’s attention.

"There are more?"

"Actually, there are quite a few Semi-Immortals in our sect, not just Three Semi-Immortals in one Sect; there are some familiar faces amongst them."

"Is that so? Let’s take a look." Celestial Master Guiyuan was indeed intrigued by Lu Yang’s words.

Then Lu Yang led him further and further away, until they arrived at a very familiar place—Captive Peak.

The Grandmaster Child sensed Lu Yang’s arrival and stood up with a smile to greet him: "Little Lu, you’re here; who have you caught this time... Guiyuan Grandmaster?!"

Have we actually captured the Ancestor Grandmaster? No, what am I thinking?

Captive Peak has never had so many visitors: Yun Mengmeng, Dai Bufan, Daoist Xiantian, Meng Jingzhou, and Vast Sea Daoist Lord vaulted in.

Chasing Moon True Person couldn’t leave Great Snow Mountain and couldn’t come.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord walked at the end of the group. He had finally taken Daoist Xiantian as his disciple and maintained his seniority. Now with the return of Celestial Master Guiyuan, he had become number two again.

As the one who revitalized the sect, is it so hard to be number one in the Seek Dao Sect?

"Guiyuan, you’re still alive?"

Celestial Master Guiyuan heard a familiar voice but couldn’t initially remember who it was. Following the voice, he located a person who surprised him considerably.

"Void?"

Void Venerate, a Semi-Immortal of the Great Yu Late Period, one of the continental hegemons, was defeated by both Jiang Pingan and Meng Junzi.

Celestial Master Guiyuan was bewildered at seeing the Void Venerate. Indeed, this was someone familiar, but the problem was he had been unable to find this familiar person even when he wanted to, eighty thousand years ago—how did he end up in Captive Peak now?!

"Si Lei!" Si Chen God Lord saw Si Lei God Lord being dragged in like a dead dog and regardless of the Captive Peak’s rule against loud noises, he couldn’t help but shout out in shock.

Hearing someone calling him, Si Lei God Lord sluggishly opened his eyes and saw Si Chen God Lord, who had been missing for a long time.

"Si Chen, what are you doing here?"

Si Chen God Lord’s tears flowed freely. Wasn’t it obvious that he had been captured?

Meng Jingzhou, seeing the deep affection between the two Divine Monarchs, handed Si Lei God Lord over to the Grandmaster Child, allowing the two God Lords to be neighbors in Captive Peak.

The prisoners on Captive Peak became slightly restless. It was Lu Yang again, capturing another Semi-Immortal.

Duan Qingshui and Mo Xiaochou, who had been captured from Buddha Country, had the strongest reaction. They had the lowest cultivation levels and had arrived at Captive Peak last with no witness to its gradual expansion.

Now they had witnessed it.

Being in Captive Peak had offered them more insights than they ever would have gained in the outside world.

"Grandmaster, on my way here, I happened to capture a Great Qian Mid-Stage cultivator." Yun Zhi’s voice came from outside Captive Peak.

Everyone turned around in unison and saw Yun Zhi dragging an unfamiliar cultivator into Captive Peak. Noticing so many eyes on her, she appeared slightly surprised.

However, she didn’t think much of it until she saw Celestial Master Guiyuan at the forefront, which made her react noticeably, dropping the unfamiliar cultivator to the ground and crossing her arms to give a salute.

"Junior Yun Zhi greets Grandmaster Guiyuan."

"Are you Yun Zhi?" Celestial Master Guiyuan sized her up, with a cultivation level so profound it seemed bottomless, feeling palpably stronger than Yun Mengmeng.

No wonder she was rated by Yun Mengmeng as "particularly strong".

"I hear you’re very strong?"

"Where does this talk of great strength come from, Grandmaster jests, it is merely the flattery among acquaintances."

"Whether it’s flattery or not, we will know once we try!"

"How dare I cross hands with Grandmaster? If by chance I were to injure Grandmaster, the guilt would weigh heavy on me."

Celestial Master Guiyuan, upon hearing this, laughed in spite of his irritation: "I wish to see how you might even harm me!"

Yun Zhi saw that Celestial Master Guiyuan’s intent to fight was resolute, apparently unavoidable, and with a light sigh, said, "Since it’s come to this, I won’t dampen Grandmaster’s spirits, but Captive Peak is cramped and I fear I cannot move freely. Would Grandmaster please step outside?"

"Very well!"

Considering Celestial Master Guiyuan’s cultivation level, Yun Zhi led him to the depths of the Seek Dao Sect, where mountains stretched on and they could fight to their heart’s content.

All the onlookers followed, buzzing with excitement.

"War Armor, come!" Celestial Master Guiyuan shouted loudly, and the War Armor, kept in the great hall and worshipped with incense offerings, stained with blood, heard its master’s call and flew swiftly towards him.

This was Celestial Master Guiyuan’s War Armor, also one of the foundations of the Seek Dao Sect. Those in the Body Integration Realm who donned the armor could rival those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, its power unmatched.

Celestial Master Guiyuan donning the War Armor seemed to transport him back to that era, a hundred thousand years ago, when the world was engulfed in the flames of war.

"Please begin, Grandmaster."

"You want me to strike first? Such arrogance from the younger generation!"

Celestial Master Guiyuan stomped fiercely, causing the earth to crack. He merged with the land, transforming into a titan that called forth wind and rain!

As he and the earth became one, as long as he stood upon the land, an unending power would surge forth. This move had given the God Lord Si Lei considerable trouble!

Yun Zhi lightly leaped, soaring tens of thousands of feet high, her expression unchanged, her jade fist clenched. The golden bell tied to her wrist jingled melodiously.

Bang!

Her fist hammered into the chest of the towering titan, who slowly toppled and shattered into pieces.

Celestial Master Guiyuan reverted to his original size, lying on the ground, his eyes devoid of life.

He was strong, but not strong enough to prevail.

He recalled again those glorious days a hundred thousand years past when he fought valiantly through bloody battles, breaking through boundaries in the heat of combat to become a universally renowned master, laying the foundation for the establishment of the Five Great Immortal Sects thereafter.

In those days, he called upon friends, overcoming countless insurmountable foes, filled with youthful exuberance.

Was he to show restraint here, facing a junior?

No!

Celestial Master Guiyuan sprang up with a Kip Up.

If he couldn’t beat Yun Zhi alone, then they would attack together.

"Vaste Sea, come aid me!"

"Ah? Oh." Although unsure of Celestial Master Guiyuan’s intentions, the words of a Grandmaster were to be heeded, nonetheless.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord obediently arrived at the battlefield, seeing the fervent fighting spirit in Celestial Master Guiyuan’s eyes.

Both ancestors of the Seek Dao Sect, in that moment, they had a telepathic understanding, grasping each other’s thoughts without a need for words. Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s heart blazed with renewed hope!

They were to conquer Yun Zhi, to restore the dignity of their seniors!

"Unity!"

Celestial Master Guiyuan activated the embryonic Unity Dao Fruit, uniting with Vast Sea Daoist Lord as one!

The merging of two Semi-Immortals into one being unleashed power, elevating their realm to an inconceivable height!

Vast Sea Daoist Lord had long known of the techniques possessed by Celestial Master Guiyuan, and today he truly comprehended the unfathomable strength of these methods!

Now, they felt as though they were invincible!

This was the power of unity, the power of collaboration!

When people band together, their combined strength can cut through metal—the idea that no enemy is unbeatable when united as one!

Celestial Master Guiyuan often faced foes he could not vanquish, but he was never alone; he had trustworthy companions to whom he could entrust his back!

"Yun Zhi, do not hold back out of consideration for our status, go all out..."

"Cloud Falling Palm."

The clouds above transformed into the shape of a palm. As the cloud palm descended, it pierced through the layers of the void, crashing down directly.

Boom—

As the dust settled, the unification dissolved, and the two ancestors lay at the center of a large crater, their eyes void of spirit.

Chapter 1081: 1080

Lu Yang and his companion saw Celestial Master Guiyuan and Vast Sea Daoist Lord lying side by side in a huge pit, resembling corpses, and hurried over with a cry of alarm to attend to the old predecessors.

"Ancestors, are you two alright?"

Both the ancestors looked lifeless, unable to hear anything.

That day, three ancestors from the Seek Dao Sect gathered together.

On the same day, two of the three ancestors fell.

Lu Yang and his companion cried as they lifted the two elders from the pit to the edge where they employed new techniques learned at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect to restore the terrain to its original state.

Though the two ancestors had passed on, life had to go on, and the tasks at hand still needed to be completed.

"Second Boss, let me help you." If the second-in-command was working, it would make no sense for the third boss to just stand by and watch.

Daoist Xiantian thought for a moment. Everything came at a price, so he couldn’t just spectate for nothing. Although the entire battle lasted only a few breaths, it was still a battle, so he, too, joined the effort to fill the pit as a price for watching.

In later generations, unworthy descendants carried sand and soil past the bodies of the two elders, their bodies exposed to the wilderness, uncared for—a true reflection of the world’s cold reality.

"Ancestor, don’t involve me next time there’s a beating like this."

"Oh."

The two ancestors lay on the ground for a while but finally regained their strength and stood up, their spirits revived.

"By the way, Ancestor, you possess a Dao Fruit Outline of Unity, right?"

Previously, Vast Sea Daoist Lord was influenced by the fiery zeal of Celestial Master Guiyuan, becoming rash and entertaining impractical ideas like defeating Yun Zhi.

Now, having lain on the ground as if they were corpses for a while, this fervor had subsided, and they were back to normal.

"Right, what about it?"

"Can you merge Spirit Stones together?"

"Yes, ten low-grade Spirit Stones make a mid-grade Spirit Stone, and ten mid-grade Spirit Stones make a high-grade Spirit Stone."

"Then, can ten top-grade Spirit Stones be merged further?"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord took out ten top-grade Spirit Stones.

Top-grade Spirit Stones are the highest quality among Spirit Stones, containing extremely pure spiritual essence, so much so that even high-level cultivators dare not use them repeatedly for cultivation.

Celestial Master Guiyuan released a cyan-green light from his hands, which enveloped the ten top-grade Spirit Stones.

The ten top-grade Spirit Stones reacted with each other, gathering, touching, and merging.

After the light disappeared, a unique Spirit Stone appeared in the hands of Celestial Master Guiyuan.

This Spirit Stone was crystal clear, virtually impossible to find if placed in water, and its quality far exceeded that of top-grade Spirit Stones, with frighteningly rich spiritual essence.

"It really worked." The Vast Sea Daoist Lord happily took the Spirit Stone and carefully placed it in a wooden box, which was lined with Ancestor Dragon Grass to display this unprecedented Spirit Stone.

A gift for Xiao Ya was secured.

All along the way, Vast Sea Daoist Lord had been reflecting on Chasing Moon True Person’s words. Xiao Ya, being born into the Long Clan, certainly wasn’t lacking in Spirit Stones, and his presumptuous gift of Spirit Stones truly wasn’t elegant.

But this Spirit Stone was different; it was beyond the top-grade Spirit Stones. No matter how wealthy Xiao Ya was, she wouldn’t possess this Spirit Stone.

"Ancestor, does this Spirit Stone have a name?"

"In terms of a name, let’s call it the Ultimate Spirit Stone; this is the limit for Spirit Stones. I feel that if it were to merge further, the Spirit Stone itself would not withstand it and would shatter."

"Then let’s call it the Ultimate Spirit Stone." Vast Sea Daoist Lord finally had a gift worth giving, and directly opened a Spatial Gate to transport himself to the East Sea.

Ao Ya was still angry over there; he had to hurry and appease her.

"This junior is too formidable; we can’t defeat him." Celestial Master Guiyuan recalled the recent fight—it was a pure crush. Even when he had challenged Jiang Pingan, he hadn’t lost this miserably.

"Let’s find someone to practice with. If Chasing Moon is still alive, then those few should be alive too, right?"

Celestial Master Guiyuan decided to reconnect with old friends to salvage some confidence after his defeat.

"It’s done." Lu Yang filled in the last patch of earth, restoring the terrain to its original state, making it impossible for anyone to tell that a great battle had occurred here.

"Eldest Senior Sister, are you going to interrogate Si Lei God Lord?" Lu Yang noticed Yun Zhi flying towards Captive Peak.

"Yes." Without the issue of Celestial Master Guiyuan, Yun Zhi had planned to conduct the interrogation on the spot.

"I’ll find some help to ensure that Si Lei God Lord confesses truthfully."

...

In Captive Peak, Si Lei God Lord was listening to his good friend, Si Chen God Lord, introduce the prisoners of Captive Peak. With each name mentioned, his eyes twitched.

Nightmare Supreme, Void Venerate, Master of All Laws... these were immensely famous Semi-Immortals in later generations, even more renowned than him.

These Semi-Immortals were actually imprisoned in this tiny Captive Peak.

He had thought his capture by the Seek Dao Sect was bad luck, a coincidence, merely bumping into Celestial Master Guiyuan and Yun Mengmeng.

Now, looking at it, this was no coincidence at all, but rather, the terrifying strength of the Seek Dao Sect.

His gaze fell on the cell at the end of Captive Peak, where a middle-aged man with a goatee and a thin figure sat on a chair with his legs crossed.

Just thinking of this person’s reputation made his scalp tingle.

Great Yu National Preceptor Guan Shanhai.

Even immortals are imprisoned here!

Si Chen said he was captured by Lu Yang, who seemed to be at the Spirit Transformation Stage but was actually an Ancient Immortal.

The Great Yu National Preceptor was captured by a woman named Yun Zhi.

A mere sect having two immortals, how terrifying is that?

And they, the Great Qian, had no knowledge of this information!

"I see you Great Qian Cultivators aren’t that impressive either. The ancient texts hype up how formidable you are, but now I see, you’re just this."

Master of All Laws said disdainfully. In his youth, he’d often been praised by his elders as having the capacity to match Great Qian God Lords. He disliked this comparison and believed he was stronger than the Great Qian God Lord.

But that was merely his own belief; he couldn’t find the Great Qian God Lord to compete against him to prove his might.

Now, he’d run into him in Captive Peak.

"What did you say? Say that again!" Si Lei God Lord roared in anger, thinking you could spout nonsense just because you were famous in later generations?

"Master All Laws, that’s enough," Si Chen God Lord coldly spoke.

"Is he wrong though? I don’t think he’s wrong. You Great Qian Cultivators are just trash!" Void Venerate said, although he was the one who ended the Great Yu Dynasty, he was still fundamentally a cultivator from Great Yu, so naturally, he sided with Great Yu.

"Void, it seems you weren’t convinced by our battle in Chunjiang City!" Nightmare Supreme frowned displeasingly. He had watched Wu Yao establish the Great Yu Dynasty and couldn’t stand the sight of cultivators from the Great Yu Dynasty.

"Ridiculous, I clearly had the upper hand then. Were you hit silly by me? No wonder Great Qian fell; it’s full of fools!"

"You Great Yu are nothing but a bunch of failures!"

"Go ahead, say that again!"

"I’ll say it ten more times if you want, failures, all failures!"

The feuding between the two great dynasties’ Semi-Immortals escalated as Body Integration Realm and Pass Tribulation Cultivators also joined the fray, verbally attacking each other’s dynasty.

The scale of the battle involved five Semi-Immortals, over a dozen Pass Tribulation Cultivators, and nearly a hundred Body Integration Cultivators; it was second only to the founding wars.

"Enough, all of you shut up." Guan Shanhai, annoyed by the noise, snapped his newspaper shut with displeasure.

Guan Shanhai, being the highest in cultivation level in Captive Peak, when he spoke, everyone immediately fell silent as if chilled to the bone.

"The Great Qian Dynasty is just a bunch of failures who only know how to enjoy the offerings of incense; what’s there to argue about with them?"

With these words, the cultivators from Great Yu erupted in cheers, while those from Great Qian quietly bowed their heads, not daring to contradict Guan Shanhai.

Si Lei God Lord also bowed his head silently, clenching his teeth, feeling deeply humiliated.

If only the Emperor and Lord Controller were here...

Chapter 1082: 1081: Great Yu Bullies Us, Great Qian Has No One!

Just as the cultivators of Great Yu were cheering, celebrating that Guan Shanhai had supported them to victory, and the cultivators of Great Qian were enduring humiliation, and Captive Peak was in an uproar,

Lu Yang silently appeared, and both sides instantly fell silent, not daring to speak any further.

Following closely behind him was Eldest Senior Sister, and the prisoners at Captive Peak were so frightened that they immediately bowed their heads, not daring to meet Lu Yang’s gaze for fear of being targeted by this harbinger of calamity.

Thanks to Lu Yang, they were able to enjoy highly nutritious medicinal food daily, followed by bloodletting.

"Why is it so quiet?"

Just now, Lu Yang could hear the clamor of these undisciplined prisoners from outside, but as he entered, the noise ceased abruptly, as if what he had heard earlier was an illusion.

Let it go, these are trifles, not worth concerning about.

Lu Yang opened the cell holding Si Lei God Lord, raised the Black Ancient Ring, and with a flash of dark light, captured Si Lei God Lord inside.

Lu Yang and Yun Zhi also entered the Ancient Ring, leaving behind the prisoners looking at each other in bewilderment.

Since when did Seek Dao Sect conduct interrogations inside a ring? That was not their usual practice.

Guan Shanhai silently observed the Black Ancient Ring, instinctively feeling that this ring had an extraordinary origin.

He was not skilled in refining, but Wu Yao was, and he had crafted the Green Peak Sword himself.

Was this ancient ring an Immortal Artifact?

Who crafted it? Could it be the legendary Yingtian Immortal?

Inside the Ancient Ring’s small world, Lu Yang was about to say something, such as appealing for leniency, threatening with severity, conveying that there were plenty of ways to make him talk if he didn’t confess, or asking him to look at who was present.

Before he could muster the right emotions, he saw Si Lei God Lord kneeling on the ground and earnestly speaking, "Ask me, I will confess everything."

"You’re being honest about confessing... huh?"

Lu Yang was just about to rehearse his speech when Si Lei God Lord’s first sentence disrupted his plans.

Are all the cultivators of Great Qian this honest?

Yun Zhi also rarely showed a hint of doubt; she had interrogated many but had never encountered someone so forthright.

"It seems I’m not needed?"

Only then did Si Lei God Lord notice a golden dragon circling in the sky, each scale as large as a mountain. The dragon transformed with a shake, turning into a handsome man exuding an impressive imperial aura.

At the sight of the man, Si Lei God Lord couldn’t stop trembling; this was a fear rooted in the depths of the Dao Fruit Outline, stronger than any fear induced by an emperor’s rage.

"Yi—Yingtian Immortal..." He stammered out the name of the handsome man.

Only his superior, Yingtian Immortal, who possessed the Tribulation Dao Fruit, could exert such oppressive presence!

He was created to search for the trace of Yingtian Immortal, yet he had never imagined he would encounter Yingtian Immortal in such a situation.

"It seems we will have to trouble Senior Yingtian to prevent him from using the Dao Fruit Outline and order him to speak the truth."

Lu Yang didn’t trust Si Lei God Lord and thought it best to use the power of Dao Fruit to suppress him.

Upon using the Dao Fruit, it turned out Si Lei God Lord truly harbored no ill intentions; he was indeed eager to tell the truth.

Regrettably, similar to the case of Si Chen God Lord, Si Lei God Lord was also under an incense restriction, unable to reveal the location of the Fairy Palace. Neither explicit nor implicit hints were allowed.

All the Heaven and Earth Treasures, Spirit Stones, and magic artifacts he had accumulated were also stored in the Fairy Palace and out of reach, which Lu Yang found to be a significant pity.

"Do you have any last words?" Considering Si Lei God Lord’s good attitude in admitting his wrongs, Lu Yang felt that if Si Lei God Lord had any requests, as long as they weren’t too excessive, he could fulfill them.

Si Lei God Lord angrily slapped his thigh, increasingly frustrated by the recent events: "Great Yu humiliates us of Great Qian as if we have no one capable. If the Emperor and Lord Controller were here, we would never have to suffer such indignities!"

The Emperor and Lord Controller had built their dynasty upon ruins; what a difficult and magnificent effort it was. Could Wu Yao and Guan Shanhai even compare?

Their Great Qian power was undoubtedly the strongest wherever they went; did the National Preceptor of Great Yu really think he was something special?

"Uh, I’ll do my best." Lu Yang was somewhat troubled, as the request was indeed not something he could help with just by willing it.

...

In the forbidden land of the Imperial Palace, two immortals of Great Xia faced off over a game of chess. The chessboard was a battlefield, with each black and white piece locked in deadly combat. Each move was heart-stopping, and the slightest misstep could lead to a total loss.

"Has Guiyuan emerged from some corner?" Upon receiving this intelligence, Meng Junzi was so excited he couldn’t control himself and crushed the chessboard with a slap of his hand.

Jiang Pingan looked at Meng Junzi with a resentful expression, wanting to pick up the hoe at his feet and give this shameless creature a smack.

I was about to win!

Soon after, Jiang Pingan revealed a content smile. It was indeed great news that Guiyuan was alive.

When Celestial Master Guiyuan disappeared eighty thousand years ago, even when he used the Power of National Fate, he couldn’t find a trace of him and worried something unexpected had happened.

I knew it. With his thick skin, he certainly couldn’t be dead.

Before the two could even reflect on their old friend’s return, they saw a burly figure brazenly enter the Imperial Palace Forbidden Land and laugh heartily.

"Meng Junzi, I knew you, this sneaky thing, had become immortal. Come on, let’s have a practice match!"

After regaining his confidence by defeating Mister Tiankui, Celestial Master Guiyuan didn’t hesitate to drag the life-weary Mister Tiankui with him to challenge Meng Junzi.

If it weren’t inconvenient for Chasing Moon True Person to come over, he would have wanted to drag him along too.

...

"Si Lei was captured by Seek Dao Sect?" The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was utterly astonished to hear this news.

But when he thought about his own clone falling into Seek Dao Sect’s hands, it seemed reasonable that Si Lei God Lord couldn’t defeat them.

"Why are there fewer and fewer of our people?" Mid-heaven Emperor Lord turned his head to ask the Lord Controller.

In just a few short years, not a single one of the Four Ancient Immortals had been found. Instead, several Semi-Immortals had been lost.

Even with the ability to create Dao Fruit Outlines through the faith of incense, Semi-Immortals were not that easy to come by.

"Could it be that the struggle of the Great Era has arrived?" After a long thought, the Controller finally came up with an explanation.

"No, the ancient conflicts of the Great Era always started with young geniuses. Has this time’s Great Era struggle begun directly with Semi-Immortals?"

"Perhaps the struggle of the Ancient Great Era has not yet ended?"

...

"Explosions, series of explosions..." The figure seated on the throne of bones was focusing on a detail in the intelligence that few had paid attention to.

The explosions that had left Celestial Master Guiyuan and Si Lei God Lord comatose.

"It sounds like the power of the explosions isn’t as great as the one at Western Paradise Temple, but apart from Immortal Artifacts, the only thing capable of causing continuous explosions would be the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit!"

"The person who conducted the explosions at Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect might possess the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit!"

"No, it’s not just a possibility, it’s a certainty!"

"She also made her move at the Sword Tower, enduring Liu Ningxuan’s Silent Destruction Sword Technique; I thought what she had was a Dao Fruit Outline of the Vajra Lineage."

"It turns out what she possesses is the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit!"

The figure’s tone grew increasingly excited.

Ever since the Mysterious Immortal made their move at Western Paradise Temple, they had disappeared without a trace, and the figure had been searching for clues of the Mysterious Immortal to no avail.

It was as if the Mysterious Immortal had evaporated from the mortal world.

Now that the clue to the Immortal Dao Fruit had reappeared, how could he not be thrilled!

"She claimed to be Yun Zhi at the Sword Tower? No, that’s not right, Yun Zhi isn’t that weak."

"But she must be related to Yun Zhi. This is going to be troublesome..."

...

Chapter 1083: 1082: Polished It Up

"Senior Ying Tian, you’ve worked hard," Lu Yang returned the Black Ancient Ring to Laurel Fairy Palace.

Yingtian Immortal looked at Lu Yang with a smile that wasn’t quite a smile, patted the shoulder of the junior he greatly valued, and showed a hearty smile, encouraging his cultivation.

"Little Friend Lu, your cultivation is progressing very quickly. You’ve reached the Spirit Transformation Late Stage."

"Just focus on your cultivation. When it’s time for you to breakthrough, I’ve got you covered, you’ll be absolutely safe!"

Lu Yang forced a smile, his shoulder aching from Yingtian Immortal’s pat, "Senior Ying Tian, there’s no need for special treatment on my behalf. When I’m cultivating, I’ve always believed that one cannot take shortcuts in cultivation."

Yingtian Immortal was utterly surprised, feeling that Lu Yang was being too formal, "What are you talking about? You are the immortal life-saver, it’s only right for me to look after you."

"Let’s just forget it, I’m afraid others will see and think you’re being unfair, damaging your reputation."

"For instance, I have a very close friend named Meng Jingzhou. Our relationship is very strong, we share both good fortune and hardships."

"When he heard of your deeds, he greatly admired you. Actually, he has some fate with you as well. When we found the ’Immortal’s Quotations’ in the Twin River Secret Realm, it wasn’t just me who saw the record; Meng Jingzhou saw it too."

"It’s just that this record contains the world’s essence and subtleties of the great Dao, it is far too profound. After reading it, Meng Jingzhou only exclaimed ’I don’t understand it.’"

"I’m worried that if he knows you’re treating me differently, it will affect his image of you in his heart."

Lu Yang spoke earnestly, with every word considered for Yingtian Immortal.

"Oh, is that so?" Yingtian Immortal’s eyes flashed coldly for a moment, then he showed a kind smile.

"Rest assured, Little Friend Lu, I have always been fair in my dealings. I will certainly treat you and your brother the same."

"I’m relieved to hear you say that."

With Yingtian Immortal’s support, Lu Yang was set for success. He wouldn’t have to worry about his tribulation being too easy. Even when successful, Lu Yang did not forget his promise to say some good words about Meng Jingzhou in front of Yingtian Immortal, trying to help him however he could.

...

When Celestial Master Guiyuan returned to the Sect, Lu Yang immediately sought him out and was shocked by his injuries.

Celestial Master Guiyuan’s face was a patchwork of bruises, clearly showing signs of a lopsided battle.

Celestial Master Guiyuan waved his hand dismissively, "Don’t mention it. After that Meng Junzi’s ascension, he indeed has some skills. He’s different from before his ascension – much more difficult to deal with, truly unmatched."

Lu Yang’s mouth twitched, thinking that it would be absurd for a Great Cultivator to be able to defeat an Immortal.

"Little Lu, you were saying you wanted to hear about the events of the Seek Dao Sect from a hundred thousand years ago?"

"Yes. Now we have a means of propaganda called newspapers, so I was thinking, now that you have come into the world, we could promote you and our Seek Dao Sect."

"Promoting our Sect, that’s a great idea." Celestial Master Guiyuan immediately agreed upon hearing this. He had a favorable impression of Lu Yang.

"When I was managing our Sect, I had thought about promoting it too, but I’m not articulate and couldn’t do it well. We also lacked someone with your talents back then. Outsiders would always mistakenly think our Sect was a nest of the Demon Path, leading to misunderstandings. Eventually, I stopped promoting altogether."

"A hundred thousand years ago... let me think, we would have to start from the death of the last Emperor Yu, and the fall of the Great Yu Dynasty."

Previously, Celestial Master Guiyuan had seen his life flash before his eyes after being punched by Yun Zhi, with the echoes of memories from a hundred thousand years ago resounding in his mind. Now that Lu Yang asked, Celestial Master Guiyuan did not need long to recount that period of history.

"Before the collapse of the Great Yu Dynasty, there were already signs of chaos, and it only worsened after its fall,"

"Seeing the world in such disarray, I found the perfect opportunity to make a name for myself, so I chose to descend the mountain and seek adventure."

"Just my luck, I had not adventured for long before my Storage Ring was stolen, leaving me without a single Spirit Stone on my person. I also happened to encounter two Great Cultivators fighting, moving mountains and draining seas, throwing me into such confusion I couldn’t even tell where I was."

"So I stopped whoever passed by, wanting to find out where I was. If they could take me along on their way, even better."

"Then I met Chasing Moon, Tiankui, and the other two, but I must’ve looked in rough shape because they mistook me for a highway robber and started fighting with me."

"I suppose it’s true that no acquaintance is made without a fight. After we cleared up the misunderstanding, Chasing Moon and his company found me agreeable and agreed to let me join them in making our mark in this troubled time."

"Later, we did indeed make some name for ourselves, and we also made the acquaintance of Meng Junzi and Jiang Pingan."

"Although the five of us aspired to end the chaos, we knew we weren’t capable of controlling the entire Central Continent, only Meng Junzi and Jiang Pingan were."

"In the end, after all the strife in the Central Continent, only the Meng Family and the Jiang Family remained as the main forces. You already know the outcome—Jiang Pingan established the Great Xia."

As Celestial Master Guiyuan narrated, Lu Yang fervently penned everything down, completing his notes once Celestial Master Guiyuan finished his tale.

"In those chaotic years, with conflict raging ceaselessly, Guiyuan, penniless but determined to achieve great things, sought kindred spirits. He chanced upon Chasing Moon True Person and his four companions."

"At that time, martial valor was esteemed by all, and through combat, Chasing Moon True Person and his four companions recognized the intentions of Guiyuan. Thus, the five forged friendships and ventured to make their mark upon the world."

"As five esteemed figures in a time of upheaval, they attained significant influence. Although they harbored ambitions to unify the world and bring an end to the chaos, they admitted to their limited capabilities, withdrawing from the struggle and leaving the battle for supremacy to the Meng and Jiang families, in hopes of bringing the chaos to an early close."

"Master, please review this," Lu Yang presented the manuscript to Celestial Master Guiyuan, "This is the general idea, I just polished it a bit, and it will be further refined before publishing."

After reviewing the manuscript, Celestial Master Guiyuan was full of question marks. He felt that what Lu Yang had written seemed different from his own story, yet upon closer reflection, he realized it was all based on what he had said.

"Quite good." He decided to leave the task to the professional and not to interfere.

...

A month had passed since the battle at the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, and the news of Celestial Master Guiyuan’s reappearance in the Cultivation World quickly spread. Cultivation Monthly, with Lu Yang handling the editorial, produced a special edition to extensively promote the great exploits of Celestial Master Guiyuan.

The news of Celestial Master Guiyuan’s return was first known by High-Level Cultivators, with average cultivators and ordinary mortals learning about it not quite as quickly.

Thanks to Cultivation Monthly, common cultivators and ordinary mortals became familiar with the events.

The monthly paper not only chronicled the events from ten thousand years ago but also told of how Celestial Master Guiyuan was inadvertently trapped in a temporal rift, and how, after escaping, he risked his own safety to battle God Lord Si Lei and rescue the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect. It was only because of Celestial Master Guiyuan’s intervention that the sect did not suffer casualties, only some material losses.

Everyone marveled at the power of the Seek Dao Sect, with two of its ancestors united, a sect with two Semi-Immortals, unparalleled in glory.

Among the populace, other rumors about Celestial Master Guiyuan also circulated, such as his fierce battle with Meng Junzi and his defeat by Meng Junzi.

However, these stories were not recorded in Cultivation Monthly and were likely just martial world gossip, not to be taken as fact.

The public was exceedingly intrigued by this Semi-Immortal. As soon as the monthly was published, it sold out instantly, and Guan Shanhai had to work overtime in prison to print more copies.

Guan Shanhai was busy, and the prisoners on Captive Peak did not dare to speak, fearing they would disturb the Immortal, resulting in a temporary silence.

Chasing Moon True Person lay on a chair made of piled snow, examining the edition of the paper three times over, yet it didn’t match his memory of events from a hundred thousand years ago.

"Why not have Lu Yang marry into our Fairy Palace? Our Fairy Palace could really use a talent like him."

Chapter 1084: 1083: Accidentally Let It Slip

Laurel Fairy Palace also has "Immortal Palace Monthly," but its sales have always fallen short of "Cultivation Monthly." Chasing Moon True Person thought that if Lu Yang married into their Fairy Palace, the Immortal Palace Monthly would definitely become a bestseller.

Even though the Fairy Palace was teeming with talent, it always lacked cultivators with Lu Yang’s flexible thinking.

However, this idea was just a fleeting thought. Chasing Moon True Person felt that with Lu Yang’s capabilities, even if he did marry into the Fairy Palace, he would probably cause so much commotion that he’d end up merging the Fairy Palace into the Seek Dao Sect.

Who would want to merge with Guiyuan’s crude Sect and devalue themselves?

...

"Old Meng has some good news, huh?" Lu Yang came to find Meng Jingzhou to share the cheerful news as soon as he finished up with the newspaper business.

Meng Jingzhou, stripped to the waist, was practicing boxing with his Dharma Aspect and ended up breathless and soaked with sweat, which caught Lu Yang by surprise.

"My goodness, do you cultivate without clothes under broad daylight!"

Lu Yang remembered that Young Master Meng used to be quite particular. Now, how had he become like this? The declining moral standards of society were a bad influence and shameful to associate with.

"It’s just that I sweat too much when cultivating, and it’s very uncomfortable with clothes on. Besides, my Dharma Aspect is dressed, so what’s wrong with me being shirtless?" Meng Jingzhou pointed to the neatly dressed Dharma Aspect above his head, defending himself with confidence.

"Out with it, what good news would make you think of me?" Meng Jingzhou looked at Lu Yang warily, judging from his experience that the ’good news’ Lu Yang spoke of might not actually be good news.

"It’s really good news. I met Yingtian Immortal and boldly put in some good words for you in front of him. He said he would treat everyone equally and treat you the same way he treats me during the Pass Tribulation."

"Good brother!" Meng Jingzhou felt inwardly ashamed, having wrongly doubted Old Lu. Old Lu was so considerate for his sake, and he had still doubted Old Lu’s character.

Oh, that’s not right, Old Lu’s character did warrant suspicion, but not this time.

"But..." Lu Yang hesitated, wondering whether to reveal more.

"But what?" Meng Jingzhou’s heart skipped a beat. Could there be some unexpected twist?

"Never mind; I won’t say it."

"Come on, just spit it out."

Seeing Old Meng persistently inquiring, Lu Yang felt he couldn’t keep it to himself any longer: "But when I mentioned ’Immortal’s Quotations,’ saying you didn’t understand it, Yingtian Immortal didn’t say much, but from the look in his eyes, he seemed to be displeased with your action."

"’Immortal’s Quotations,’ what is that? Have I read it?"

When Lu Yang mentioned this, Meng Jingzhou was initially confused, then remembered where he had heard the term.

In the Twin River Secret Realm, he found the study of Fairy Eternity. In the study, there was a copy of "Immortal’s Quotations," but it was written in Ancient Language, which he couldn’t understand.

"Why did you bring that up!" Meng Jingzhou grew anxious. If he had upset Yingtian Immortal, wouldn’t he be in for a tougher time during his Pass Tribulation?

"It slipped out accidentally. Look, here I am to make amends," Lu Yang said with an embarrassed smile, feeling that it was not too late for damage control.

"How do you plan to make amends?"

"Like this—I’ve always said I’d teach you Ancient Language but haven’t had the chance. I might as well take this opportunity to teach you."

As he spoke, Lu Yang pulled out a small packet from his bosom, opened it, and took out a book from within, handing it over to Meng Jingzhou with a serious expression, as if completing some ritual.

"Once you learn Ancient Language, you will be able to understand ’Immortal’s Quotations’ by yourself."

"Brilliant idea!" Meng Jingzhou took the Quotations, feeling its weighty significance. The handwritten record of the words of the Four Ancient Immortals by Fairy Eternity was undoubtedly a priceless treasure, coveted by Immortals if they were to see it.

...

Three months later, Lu Yang received a special invitation.

"Second-in-command, look, this is a letter from the Spirit Chef Alliance for you!" Yun Mengmeng waved the envelope excitedly. Though she didn’t know what the Spirit Chef Alliance was, the name alone told her there must be lots of delicious food involved.

"Spirit Chef Alliance?" Lu Yang had heard of it, an organization made up of the top spirit chefs in the world.

As the saying goes, delicious food knows no borders, so the spirit chefs of the Alliance came from all over the Cultivation World, except for the Buddha Country.

"What does the Spirit Chef Alliance want to send me a letter for?" Lu Yang wondered to himself, given that he couldn’t cook.

The only time he ever tried cooking was when Fairy Eternity possessed him and he made a dish for Qiu Jin’an, putting in twenty seconds of salt.

And then, thanks to his master’s promotion, it turned into "Qiu Jin’an excitedly pounded the table asking who made the dish."

Surely they wouldn’t invite him to join the Spirit Chef Alliance over that, would they?

"It must be because I possessed you once, and people mistook you for having the talent of a spirit chef," Fairy Eternity said smugly. Genius, it seems, could be recognized even through possession.

However, she didn’t recommend that Lu Yang join the Spirit Chef Alliance. After all, what was her status? The Ancestor of Spirit Chef—why would she need to join the Alliance to prove herself?

Lu Yang opened the envelope and quickly read through it, relieved that he didn’t have to agonize over whether to join the Spirit Chef Alliance or not.

"It turns out they’re organizing a Spirit Chef Competition and inviting me to cover it."

The whole world knew that Lu Yang had created the Cultivation Monthly, and all articles, whether written by him or not, had to be approved by him. The Spirit Chef Alliance invited Lu Yang, not to compete but to promote the competition.

"I remember the canteen used to qualify for the competition, too. But since some senior brother argued with a judge, blinded by rage, and knocked them out with a fried dough stick, the Sect’s eligibility to compete was revoked." Lu Yang couldn’t recall who had told him, but it was definitely a thing that had happened.

Lu Yang felt it didn’t matter whether the canteen entered the competition or not, as they couldn’t win anyway.

"Eh, but I heard the canteen won a competition before, even got first place," Yun Mengmeng questioned, puzzled.

Lu Yang sighed, "It did win a competition, but not a Spirit Chef Competition. It was a Refining Competition."

"It’s all competitions; it’s about the same. Second-in-command, shall we go?" Yun Mengmeng didn’t discriminate between competitions. She had good teeth and didn’t struggle to eat the canteen’s food, thinking the cooking level was quite high.

Lu Yang was a bit conflicted. He knew nothing about spirit chefs; what was he supposed to report on?

"Let’s go, let’s go, let’s go!"

Yun Mengmeng’s eyes brimmed with hope as she looked at Lu Yang with her large, watery eyes, shining brightly. With every "let’s go," she edged closer to Lu Yang. Looking into Yun Mengmeng’s hopeful eyes, Lu Yang couldn’t bring himself to say no.

"Alright, let’s go."

Last time Yun Mengmeng wanted to go to the Buddha Country, she was dissuaded by the hazy folks of that place. Taking her to the Spirit Chef Competition would make up for that.

"Yay, long live the Second-in-command!" Yun Mengmeng cheered, excited about the prospect of indulging in the delicious food at the Spirit Chef Competition.

"Wait, saying ’long live the Second-in-command’ might shorten your life." Yun Mengmeng realized her mistake; the Second-in-command was certainly stronger than her and destined to become Immortal. It wasn’t appropriate to use "long live" to describe him.

"Immortality to the Second-in-command!"

Lu Yang didn’t know whether to laugh or cry as the beautiful girl shouted for his eternal life, her eyes filled with admiration. To outsiders, it might look like cult followers greeting their master.

"Alright already, let’s not dwell on eternal life. Let’s get ready and go."

Chapter 1085: 1084 Ancestor of Spirit Chef Yingtian Immortal

After reporting his whereabouts to Eldest Senior Sister and Dai Bufan, Lu Yang left the Fourth Housekeeper to watch over the Sect and the first three Housekeepers headed to the location of the Spirit Chef Competition.

The Spirit Chef Competition was held in Demon City, the imperial capital of the Demon Nation.

It was said that Zhu Tian himself had extended an invitation to the Spirit Chef Alliance, hoping that by doing so, the world would come to know Demon City.

Having learned from the experience of trekking and flying to the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect last time, Lu Yang chose to travel by flying boat this time, accompanied by Yun Mengmeng to the Great Xia Border.

The journey was uneventful, except for Yun Mengmeng who would often exclaim loudly while on the flying boat, pulling Lu Yang to look at this and that, attracting much attention.

"Sister Mengmeng, it can’t be your first time going out, can it? You seem very excited," Lu Yang said helplessly as people around them were watching.

Over the years, Yun Mengmeng hadn’t always stayed at the Seek Dao Sect; she would often go out to search for delicious food and have a great time.

"Although it’s not the first time I’ve gone out, it’s the first time going out with the Second-in-command," said Yun Mengmeng with a giggle. Previously, she had tried to get Xiao Zhi to go out and play, but Xiao Zhi had outright refused. This time, she finally had company.

Lu Yang shook his head with a smile, knowing that going out with another person indeed felt different from going out alone.

After all, with Fairy Eternity residing in his Spiritual Space, he would never know what it felt like to travel alone.

Then, the two crossed over the Great Xia Border and entered the Demon Territory.

Lu Yang had been to the Demon Territory several times before; each visit to the Demon Territory included a trip to Demon City, so he knew the way well.

The two took the Sword Washing Pool and headed straight for Demon City.

"Second-in-command, would you like some beef jerky?" Yun Mengmeng asked, holding a bag of beef jerky. To prepare for the trip, she had purchased a heap of things from the cafeteria to munch on along the way.

Lu Yang took the beef jerky and extended his index finger, which gathered Sword Qi. With a fierce chop, the beef jerky and the Sword Qi both snapped in two.

Lu Yang handed the two pieces of beef jerky back to Yun Mengmeng.

"I have bad teeth."

"You could try infusing your teeth with Sword Qi so you can chew it," Yun Mengmeng sincerely suggested.

"Forget it, you eat it." Replying to sincerity with sincerity, Lu Yang also earnestly refused Yun Mengmeng’s suggestion.

"We’ve arrived." After some time flying, the Sword Washing Pool descended near Demon City.

Lu Yang’s appearance caused quite a stir; by now, he was well-known, his reputation shining bright as the midday sun.

"Is this Fellow Daoist Lu Yang in person?" one person exclaimed upon seeing Lu Yang, quickly walking over in long strides.

"And you are?"

"The Vice Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance, Liu Xing."

"So you’re Elder Liu Xing." Before coming, Lu Yang had obtained information about the Spirit Chef Alliance from Elder Brother Dai. This Liu Xing was one of the three Vice Alliance Hierarchs, with a Cultivation Level at the Integration Late Stage.

"I dare not claim to be your elder." Liu Xing repeatedly waved his hand in humility. Although he was uncertain about how much of Talent Lu’s rumored skill was true, even if he lacked skill, the title of founder of Cultivation Monthly was not to be taken lightly.

"And who might this be?" Liu Xing noticed the stunningly beautiful woman standing beside Lu Yang and was slightly startled; he found it somewhat difficult to discern her Cultivation Level.

"My friend." Lu Yang did not elaborate on introducing Yun Mengmeng. Using her real name would directly match the name of the Heavenly Court Sect Master and calling her Yun Zhi was even less appropriate.

"Ah, a friend of Fellow Daoist Lu Yang, surely also a person of profound knowledge and extraordinary talent. Please, both of you follow me into Demon City."

Entering Demon City, Lu Yang glanced at the resplendent palace atop the Demon City Sacred Mountain.

"That is the Demon Emperor’s palace, does Fellow Daoist Lu Yang have an interest?" Liu Xing asked with a smile.

"The Demon Emperor, oh you mean Zhu Tian." Liu Xing’s sudden mention of the Demon Emperor caught Lu Yang off guard.

"Not interested," Lu Yang shook his head repeatedly.

Why would he have any interest in the Demon Emperor’s palace? Whenever he visited Demon City, he didn’t go anywhere else but straight to the palace to find Zhu Tian.

However, to Liu Xing, it wasn’t so simple. The Demon Emperor’s palace was such a mysterious place, how could anyone not be interested?

"If Fellow Daoist Lu Yang is interested, I can get in touch with friends in the Demon Emperor’s palace, and you should be able to enter."

"Really, there’s no need."

Lu Yang couldn’t help but smile. He was familiar only with Zhu Tian’s palace; in contrast, he was not well acquainted with Demon City as a whole.

Every time he came to Demon City, he found significant changes. Under Zhu Tian’s guidance, the city as a whole displayed an architectural style that blended the wildness of the Demon Race with the civilization of the Human race.

Especially, since Zhu Tian himself was a master of architecture, the blueprints of Demon City designed by him had tremendous artistic value. Anyone who came to Demon City would marvel at its majestic splendor.

"Such a beautiful place," Yun Mengmeng exclaimed in amazement, the architectural style of Demon City was unlike any other place.

Walking the streets of Demon City, one rarely saw members of the Demon Race in their true form. Most had transformed into human shapes, retaining only a small part of their demonic features, such as ears and tails.

There were also some whose attire was distinctly different from that of the Demon Territory.

"These are Spirit Chefs and Quasi-Spirit Chefs from Great Xia, the East Sea, and the Extreme Northern Lands."

"Quasi-Spirit Chef?"

"They are Cultivators preparing for the Spirit Chef exam," Liu Xing explained with a smile.

"Only those Cultivators who pass the Spirit Chef exam are recognized as Spirit Chefs by the Spirit Chef Alliance, and the Alliance issues a certificate of proof."

"And some Cultivators, despite their culinary skills, have not passed the Spirit Chef Alliance exam; they are considered Quasi-Spirit Chefs."

"Only the true Spirit Chefs are eligible to participate in the Spirit Chef competition. Thus, before the competition begins, the Alliance will organize a Spirit Chef examination so that the Quasi-Spirit Chefs who pass can also join the competition."

"This is it." The Spirit Chef Alliance arranged the most luxurious inn in Demon City for Lu Yang, located in the city’s center.

Right in front of the inn stood a statue of the Demon Emperor – a round and headless Zhu Tian, completely red and winged, towering several dozen feet high, very conspicuous, one of the unique landmarks of Demon City.

"Of course, we need two rooms," Lu Yang rolled his eyes, Liu Xing must have misunderstood something.

After arranging accommodations, Liu Xing took out two Jade Scrolls: "The Jade Scrolls contain the detailed schedule of activities, to better arrange your time. Then, may the Yingtian Immortal bless you both."

"Hold on, Yingtian Immortal?" Lu Yang was about to offer some courteous words, but he couldn’t let this comment go.

"Does Fellow Daoist Lu Yang not know? The Yingtian Immortal is the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, the only Immortal Cook. It is said that eating the food of Yingtian Immortal can induce intoxication, as if entering an Immortal Realm, it also makes one forget their mortal past, like dreams and illusions. The effects are endless, beyond our comprehension," Liu Xing explained.

"It is said that Yingtian Immortal’s food is not of the Mortal World, and thus, cannot be contained in mortal vessels. Even kitchenware like pots and pans must be personally crafted by the Yingtian Immortal."

"Furthermore, historical records indicate that the other three immortals were willing to wait over a hundred years just to eat Yingtian Immortal’s cooking, which signifies the extent of its extraordinary taste."

Chapter 1086: 1085: Found a Good Relationship

"It is said that the Yingtian Immortal General compiled all his cooking skills into a book and named it the ’Complete Culinary Compendium.’"

"Unfortunately, the ’Complete Culinary Compendium’ has long since disappeared in the long river of history, which is utterly heartbreaking."

"The chief alchemist of the Elixir Brewing Alliance, Qingyun Zi, did find a rubbings copy of the ’Complete Culinary Compendium,’ but according to the records of the rubbings, what was produced was not food, but poison."

"We suspect that someone was envious of Yingtian Immortal’s talent and deliberately created a fake book to tarnish Yingtian Immortal’s reputation!"

Liu Xing said indignantly.

Lu Yang subconsciously touched his identity jade, feeling as if the original ’Complete Culinary Compendium’ was inside it. If he took it out, it would probably be considered a counterfeit.

When Liu Xing spoke of Yingtian Immortal, his eyes filled with admiration and sincerity. Before, Lu Yang had not interacted with genuine Spirit Chefs. Now, seeing this, he realized that Yingtian Immortal’s status in the world of Spirit Chefs was terrifyingly high.

Lu Yang didn’t need to guess to know that the title of Ancestor of Spirit Chefs being attributed to Yingtian Immortal was a result of altering history; it was supposed to be Fairy Eternity who was the true Ancestor of Spirit Chefs.

Although Yingtian Immortal was likely pleased to be praised by later generations, it was highly probable that he would not want the title of Ancestor of Spirit Chefs.

Moreover, it was highly likely that Yingtian Immortal wouldn’t want Lu Yang to know he held such a title.

Vice Alliance Hierarch, you’re harming me. Why are you telling me all this!

After sending Liu Xing away, Fairy Eternity floated out, boasting proudly in front of Lu Yang about her renown.

"Little Yang heard it, right? My status in the Spirit Chef world is universally acknowledged."

"The Chief Housekeeper is so amazing!" Yun Mengmeng praised Fairy Eternity on the side, surprised that the most incredible Immortal Cook she had been searching for all over the world was actually the Chief Housekeeper!

Come to think of it, why had she never eaten food made by the Chief Housekeeper? Could it be because she hadn’t contributed enough to the Immortal Lineage?

Yun Mengmeng resolved to continue contributing to the Immortal Lineage.

Yun Mengmeng was extremely fond of her identity as the third boss of the Immortal Lineage. Her granny had said that the outside world was full of scheming and deceit, and advised her to be cautious as a priority.

She didn’t see the outside world as bad as her granny described; at least, their Immortal Lineage was unified and harmonious.

"Fairy is so amazing," Lu Yang complimented Fairy Eternity insincerely.

"Take a break." Lu Yang yawned and lazily lay on the bed, exhausted from piloting the Sword Washing Pool all the way.

Earlier, in the presence of outsiders, Lu Yang had to put on a majestic display befitting a genius. Now that it was just his own people, he could drop the act.

It had to be said, the Spirit Chef Alliance took great care of Lu Yang’s affairs. The room they arranged for him was the best, and the Gathering Spirit Array made of high-grade spirit stones was more than enough even for an Integration Period Cultivator to use.

Just by lying there, absorbing the spiritual energy, Lu Yang could alleviate his fatigue.

He placed the jade scroll on his forehead and gained a general understanding of the Spirit Chef competition.

The current phase was the preparation stage of the competition. In seven days, there would be a Spirit Chef exam, and only after the exam would the real Spirit Chef competition begin.

All Spirit Chefs wanted to showcase their culinary skills in this contest and prove themselves. Thus, from the preparatory phase of the competition, there would be Spirit Chefs setting up stalls on the streets or temporarily renting shops to sell their delicacies.

As a result, not only spirit stones but gourmands from all over the world would rush to participate, creating a grand event.

"No wonder Xiao Zhu wants to host the Spirit Chef competition in Demon City. It’s a great opportunity to boost its reputation."

Despite Demon City’s fame, after all, the reputation of the Demon Territory wasn’t very good, and people who knew about Demon City’s existence dared not visit it.

"Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline surely is fitting for managing a country," Lu Yang mused. With the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline in hand, Zhu Tian didn’t have to worry about his subordinates defying his will; he could enforce his will resolutely.

This was precisely why the Demon Territory had developed so rapidly over the years.

"Second-in-command, do you want to go out for a walk? I saw many delicious things being sold by the roadside when I arrived."

"No, too tired," Lu Yang just wanted to lie down.

Knock knock knock.

Suddenly, there came a knocking at the door.

"Who is it!" Yun Mengmeng raised the beef jerky cautiously, ready to stab with it if the visitor meant harm.

"It’s me."

Recognizing the familiar voice, Lu Yang got up from the bed, "Go open the door."

Yun Mengmeng opened the door and saw a man in a black robe standing outside, mysterious in demeanor and clearly up to no good.

The man in the black robe stepped into the room and took off his hood.

"Nation Master Zhu, long time no see," Lu Yang stood up to greet him.

"Brother Lu is joking. I’m nowhere near worthy of being called a king. If you think highly of me, just call me Little Zhu."

Zhu Tian wouldn’t dare put on airs in front of Lu Yang; Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi called him Senior Brother, Immortal Eternity lived inside his body, and the Yingtian Immortal had said he thought highly of Lu Yang, addressing him as a young friend.

In other words, Lu Yang’s stature was on par with that of an Ancient Immortal, so calling him a big brother was a bargain, really.

"Is this Zhu Tian?" Yun Mengmeng put away the beef jerky, looked back and forth between the statue of Zhu Tian’s true form outside the window, and the human-shaped Zhu Tian.

It’s the Second-in-command who is amazing. Others come to Demon City at most to visit the Demon Emperor’s statue, but as soon as the Second-in-command arrives, the Demon Emperor himself comes over.

"And who’s this?"

"Yun Mengmeng," said Lu Yang. Zhu Tian was one of their own; there was nothing to hide.

Zhu Tian was shocked, his body shuddering. Wasn’t Yun Mengmeng the Heavenly Court Sect Master, that Immortal who initiated an immortal battle in the East Sea and emerged victorious?

Could it really be her?

Does Brother Lu always have the company of an Immortal when he goes out?!

"No, it’s not like that. The Heavenly Court Sect Master is my Eldest Senior Sister. She and Yun Mengmeng are good sisters. Wanting to help Yun Mengmeng make a name for herself, she borrowed her name for a bit."

A sister of an Immortal? That status is certainly much higher than his own.

With an ingratiating smile, Zhu Tian said, "Well, I heard from my subordinates that Brother Lu had come to Demon City. I have some stature here in Demon City, so I thought I’d fulfill the host’s duty and extend some hospitality to Brother Lu."

The moment he heard that Lu Yang had arrived, Zhu Tian immediately set out to greet him, wanting to show his sincerity.

Zhu Tian glanced around the room, noting the furnishings were far inferior to his own and the Gathering Spirit Array was average at best.

"Brother Lu, if you don’t mind, how about you honor my Imperial Palace with a visit? It’s somewhat better than here. Consider it your own home, it’s much more comfortable to live in."

Lu Yang waved his hand, declining Zhu Tian’s kind offer, "I prefer to keep a low profile. Let’s skip staying at your Imperial Palace; I like it just fine here."

"I didn’t think it through," Zhu Tian admitted.

"Hey Fellow Daoist Lu Yang, did you see those dancing girls at the door? They’re so pretty, all kinds of races represented. Looks like they’re being presented as gifts to someone." Vice Alliance Hierarch Liu Xing returned, noticing the open door and walked straight in, talking about what he had just seen.

Lu Yang and the Demon Emperor both turned to look at Liu Xing, whose voice trailed off to a whisper.

"Are you really not interested in taking a tour of the Imperial Palace, Daoist friend? I’ve sorted out the connections, in the Imperial Kitchen, uh, it seems you’ve already made some good connections..."

Chapter 1087: 1086: Feeling Good About Oneself

Liu Xing chuckled wryly, looking at Lu Yang and the Demon Emperor Zhu Tian, feeling that he had unintentionally stumbled upon a very serious matter.

That was the Demon Emperor Zhu Tian, the lord of hundreds of millions of the Demon Race. Although he himself was an Integration Period Cultivator, standing before the Demon Emperor, he was as insignificant as an ant, easily crushed at will.

"If there’s nothing else, I’ll take my leave first," Liu Xing said with a compensatory smile, backing out of the room.

"Stop," the Demon Emperor Zhu Tian spoke indifferently, activating the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline and leaving a branding on Liu Xing’s Lingtai.

"You must not mention our meeting to anyone."

Liu Xing’s eyes were vacant, and he monotonously responded with a "Yes," walking out of the inn and regaining his senses.

"Are there dancing girls?" Yun Mengmeng, with a strip of beef jerky in her mouth, peeked through the threshold, and indeed, she saw many Demon Race dancing girls in the hallway, all of whom retained some characteristics of the Demon Race, but none of whom Yun Mengmeng recognized.

"Indeed, there are quite a lot of people at the Second-in-command’s door."

Zhu Tian was sweating profusely. He had always wanted to establish a connection with Lu Yang but didn’t know what to offer, so he cultivated a group of maid girls. This time, thinking he finally had the chance to give Brother Lu the dancing girls he had raised for him, he didn’t expect Lu Yang to bring Yun Mengmeng with him on this visit.

"I was worried that Brother Lu might not be accustomed to life in Demon City, so I took the liberty of finding a few clever maids for you," he said.

"Thank you for the kind offer, Nation Master Zhu, but I’m used to having my peace and quiet. It would be best if you take the girls back," Lu Yang replied.

"All right."

Seeing that Lu Yang didn’t make a big deal out of this matter, Zhu Tian let out a sigh of relief.

"Actually, the reason I’ve come to see Brother Lu is that I have a question, something that has always puzzled me. It’s just I never found the right opportunity to ask," Zhu Tian said.

"What’s the issue?"

Zhu Tian sat up straight, earnestly seeking Lu Yang’s advice. Seeing this, Lu Yang couldn’t help feeling apprehensive, secretly guessing what Zhu Tian was about to ask him.

"Have you noticed, Brother Lu, that before I awoke, there were no rumors of Semi-Immortals in the Cultivation World, and both Great Qian and Great Yu factions were in hiding.
