But since I awoke, Semi-Immortals have started to appear, one after another, and even several unexpected Immortals have emerged!"

"Do you think it’s possible that my emergence triggered the start of a Great Era of strife?"

"Considering what you’ve said, it does seem to be the case," Lu Yang admitted. He had not thought about this before, but now, with Zhu Tian’s remark, it seemed Zhu Tian might indeed be the source of it all.

After Zhu Tian’s revival, Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi appeared one after another, and both Great Yu and Great Qian also rushed to show themselves.

"So, Brother Lu, you also think so?" Zhu Tian had always felt that he was no ordinary person. He was first among the Immortals, second only to Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, and his awakening in this era could not just be a coincidence; it must signify something.

Receiving Lu Yang’s affirmation, Zhu Tian became even more certain of his view.

After sending Zhu Tian off, the feeling of exhaustion that Lu Yang experienced nearly vanished.

"Sister Mengmeng, shall we take a stroll?"

"Let’s do it!"

Yun Mengmeng, who had been lying on the bed making a "big" character shape, suddenly sprang up from it.

Considering his current high profile, knowing that he would be recognized if he went out, Lu Yang put on a mask, altering his appearance.

"Speaking of which, this mask was given by the Di Jiang Clan."

The mask provided by the Di Jiang Clan was indeed very useful.

As soon as they stepped outside, they were greeted by a delicious scent, and without needing a map, they could follow the aroma to the main venue of the Spirit Chef competition.

Walking through two streets, they arrived at the market where small carts were everywhere. Spirit Chefs demonstrated their skills, using this opportunity to promote their culinary philosophies.

These Spirit Chefs came from all corners of the world. The one with the lowest Cultivation Level was at the Foundation Establishment Stage, while the highest were at the Divinity Transformation Stage, but no matter how high their status in the outside world, here, they were just street food stall owners.

To call them street food stalls was not entirely accurate, after all, one wouldn’t expect a street food stall to battle with a Demon Beast, adhering to the principle of ’kill-before-you-eat.’

The Divinity Transformation Stage three-eyed cow demon kept rubbing its hooves against the ground and with a bellow, charged at the street food stall owner.

The street food stall owner remained calm in the face of danger, clenching his fists; his muscles swelled, bursting his clothes at the seams, and surprisingly, he overpowered the three-eyed cow demon in the contest of strength!

He shook his arms forcefully, hurling the three-eyed cow demon into the sky, then pulled out a gleaming bone knife from behind his waist, which was different from ordinary bone knives because its handle was golden.

The snack stand owner leapt high into the air, rising to the level of the cow demon:

"Bone Knife Technique!"

Swish, swish, swish—

The snack stand owner’s knife skills were so exquisite that one could only see the afterimages, and in the blink of an eye, the cow demon was stripped down to its skeleton, the snowflake-like slices of beef neatly arranged on a plate.

The food enthusiasts watching around burst into cheers.

"What a skillful knife technique!"

"Beautiful!"

"To have refined the ordinary bone knife technique to such a level of perfection, could he be the famous Golden Knife Master?"

"The golden bone knife, it must be him!"

Lu Yang also saw a Spirit Chef pounding a turtle shell with his fists until it cracked into pieces, another evolving thousands of knife auras to chop vegetables and meat, and another controlling over a hundred cauldrons with an astonishing strength of Spiritual Sense.

Lu Yang felt that the profession of a Spirit Chef being so respectable must be about more than just cooking to increase one’s cultivation level.

Demon Beasts and the Demon Race are different beings. The former are merely wild beasts with cultivation levels, and the Demon Race does not consider them as part of the same race; Demon Beasts are indeed part of the Demon Race’s menu.

If that were not the case, the Spirit Chef Alliance would not dare to hold a Spirit Chef competition in Demon City.

As they were strolling along, they saw a young man stumbling over to Lu Yang and bowing with his fists clenched: "Senior cultivators, I am Song Tianjing, the seventh-generation descendant of the Yangyuan Building. I wonder if I could borrow some Spirit Stones from you two?"

"Yangyuan Building?" Lu Yang repeated, having never heard of this place. Yun Mengmeng also shook her head.

"Why do you seek to borrow Spirit Stones specifically from us?" Lu Yang saw that the young man’s cultivation level was only at the Foundation Establishment Stage; he posed no threat to him.

He was in disguise, so no one would recognize him as Lu Yang. Why did this Song Tianjing not approach others but instead come to him?

"This cultivator is at the Foundation Establishment Stage, right?" Lu Yang asked Fairy Eternity just to be sure.

"Yes."

"Then, that’s good."

"The two senior cultivators carry themselves with exceptional composure, surely you come from a major power."

"Interesting, follow me."

Lu Yang led the young man to a shop and ordered three pots of soup.

"Tell me more about your background and why you need Spirit Stones."

"My name is Song Tianjing. The Yangyuan Building was an inn established by my ancestors. The inn had been passed down to the sixth generation, which is my father’s, gradually falling into decline."

"Although my father aspired to be a Spirit Chef, his talent was limited. He could never pass the examination and was depressed, spending his days in gambling dens."

"Later, the people at the gambling den took a fancy to Yangyuan Building’s reputation and set a trap to swindle my father. Blinded by greed, he gambled more and more until he eventually mortgaged the Yangyuan Building."

"When those people came to take away Yangyuan Building, my father realized he had been fooled and was filled with regret."

"Overcome with anger and frustration, my father passed away suddenly. On his deathbed, holding my hand, he wished for me to redeem the Yangyuan Building."

"To redeem the Yangyuan Building, I need to become a Spirit Chef. That’s why I’ve come to Demon City. However, my home is too far from Demon City, and to get here, I’ve spent all my resources, so I’m hoping to borrow some Spirit Stones from the two seniors to rent a stall."

"With my cooking skills, I should be able to pay it back quickly!"

Seeing Lu Yang still hesitant, Song Tianjing gritted his teeth and pulled out a tattered ancient scroll from his bosom, "My ancestors happened upon this Ancient Fragment and established Yangyuan Building. If the seniors don’t trust me, I will leave this Ancient Fragment as collateral!"

Lu Yang took the ancient book and looked at it, "Culinary Bible."

Chapter 1088: 1087: The Young Sect Master Bestows Opportunities (Seeking Monthly Pass at the Beginning of the Month)

Lu Yang gasped in shock. Song Tianjing’s ancestor was quite capable, opening a restaurant just by following recipes from the "Complete Culinary Compendium."

It was also a mystery where Song Tianjing’s ancestor found this book, badly damaged, without even a cover, just a dozen or so pages left in the middle.

"The book is all in classical script, can you understand it?" Not to brag, but within the current cultivators, only Old Meng and I can fully understand classical script.

"My ancestor poured his heart and soul into deciphering partial recipes; for example, for a dish that requires eighteen ingredients, my ancestor managed to decipher six."

"Based on these six ingredients and his own research, my ancestor then created the current menu of Yangyuan Building."

"The heartfelt wish passed down through generations in my Yangyuan Song Family is to decode the entire menu."

Lu Yang got it. Song Tianjing’s ancestor was lucky, deciphering only the editable parts and adding genuine research, which led to the establishment of Yangyuan Building.

"Yangyuan Song Family, it seems like you’re an influential family then?"

"Our Song Family has had Golden Core Great Abilities for generations." Speaking of heritage, Song Tianjing puffed out his chest a little, brimming with confidence.

"So, your Song Family is also a well-known and respected clan in the area?"

"I won’t lie to my senior, my Yangyuan Song Family on Fuguang Street in the North City area of Tianxing City in Qingzhou, Great Xia, is a famous establishment!"

Lu Yang: "..."

No wonder I thought from the Third Grade Sect to Super-grade Sect and couldn’t find the location of your Yangyuan Building.

"Then after you become a Spirit Chef, are you planning to take revenge on those who wronged your father?"

Song Tianjing shook his head: "I’ve already reported them for running a gambling den, and they’ve been sent away."

"This kid has Immortal Fairy’s recipes, it seems he’s quite fated with them," Fairy Eternity excitedly patted Lu Yang’s shoulder.

"Little Yang, you must give him the complete version of Immortal’s recipes right away!" Lu Yang had never imagined how malicious the Fairy could be, out to overwhelm the young cultivator’s beliefs.

Seeing Lu Yang ignoring her, Immortal Fairy stretched out her tiny foot and poked Lu Yang in the back with her pearl-like toes, persistently urging, "Hurry up, hurry up."

Lu Yang sighed in his heart, realizing that this Song lad wouldn’t be able to avoid it.

"Fairy, your recipes are after all Immortal Objects, wouldn’t it tarnish your reputation if you simply handed them over to him?"

"You make a good point, so what should we do?"

"We could put a restriction on the recipes, allowing him to unlock new parts with each realm he advances to. This way, not only will he diligently study your recipes, but he will also quicken his cultivation. With this approach, he certainly won’t disgrace you."

"That’s a good idea!"

Lu Yang returned the "Complete Culinary Compendium."

"What does this mean, senior?" Song Tianjing thought Lu Yang still didn’t believe him.

"You’re also in luck, meeting me. Take good care of this book and don’t bring it out again."

"This book relates to the Ancestor of Spirit Chef and has been declared a forbidden book by the Spirit Chef Alliance, to be destroyed upon discovery. If the people of the Spirit Chef Alliance learn that you have this book, even a damaged one, I need not spell out what would happen to you."

"A forbidden book?!" Song Tianjing had never imagined that the book passed down from his ancestors had such a significant background, being connected to the legendary Ancestor of Spirit Chef.

This was a major opportunity!

Wait a minute, the senior was telling him about such a big opportunity so easily?

Lu Yang knew exactly what Song Tianjing was thinking and said lightly, "This bit of opportunity..."

"Boss, another bowl of soup!" Yun Mengmeng called out.

"This bit of opportunity..."

"You two drink up quickly, the soup won’t taste good after it cools down." Yun Mengmeng kindly reminded them, wondering why the Second-in-command came to the store but didn’t drink soup, only talking.

"..."

Lu Yang had no choice but to pick up the spoon and slurped the soup while saying, "Heh, this bit of fortune... slurp... I don’t even care for... slurp..."

Song Tianjing, a junior cultivator, dared not defy Yun Mengmeng and also sipped the soup spoonful by spoonful.

"Who exactly are you... slurp..."

"Heavenly Court Sect... slurp... Young Sect Master."

Clatter, as Song Tianjing learned of Lu Yang’s true identity, his pupils shook, and in shock, he let the spoon slip into the bowl.

Though Lu Yang was still sipping the soup, in Song Tianjing’s eyes, he was no longer the approachable senior, but a mysterious and immensely formidable being of legend.

"You’re a smart man, you won’t tell anyone my identity, right?" Lu Yang said with a light smile, the steam from the soup enveloped him, leaving only his voice audible.

Song Tianjing nodded dumbly, and when he regained his senses, he found an additional book and a storage ring on the table, but Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng were nowhere to be found, with only Lu Yang’s voice echoing in his ears.

"I have some connections with the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, and today I’ve taken a liking to you, so I’m giving you the original of this book."

"I’ve placed a restriction on it; as your cultivation level increases, you will see more of its content."

"In the storage ring, there are ten thousand spirit stones, consider it a gift from me, intriguing lad, I’m curious to see how far you can go in the world of Spirit Chefs, hahahaha..."

Song Tianjing, holding the original Complete Culinary Compendium, felt like he was holding a hot potato; a gift from the Young Sect Master of Heavenly Court could be either a blessing or a curse.

Lu Yang had already translated the Complete Culinary Compendium long ago, it was a challenge left to him by Immortal Fairy when he was learning Ancient Language.

The Ancient Language contained many obscure words hard to understand, and the Complete Culinary Compendium essentially covered these words; as a test, it couldn’t be more appropriate.

Song Tianjing bit his teeth, and with difficulty, made up his mind to learn!

He couldn’t wait to open the Complete Culinary Compendium after returning, but he could only understand the first ten pages, featuring just three dishes.

"Complete Culinary Compendium, authored by Yingtian Immortal. It really is the work of the Ancestor of Spirit Chef!"

Song Tianjing became extremely excited upon seeing the name Yingtian Immortal; it was an existence akin to a belief.

"Pearl Fish Soup... a dash of salt?"

Song Tianjing fell silent for a while, then continued to read:

"With the Five Poisons as supplements, mutually generating and inhibiting each other, the cycle never ends..."

"Using Firmament Tree as firewood..."

The more Song Tianjing read, the more shocked he became; so this was the work of the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, truly not something an ordinary mortal could comprehend.

"Let’s try."

Getting Firmament Tree was impossible, so he’d have to make do with ordinary kindling, and he couldn’t afford high-quality Five Poisons, only somewhat inferior ones.

Song Tianjing followed the methods of the Complete Culinary Compendium to the letter and soon produced a delicious pot of fish soup, its aroma was so tempting it assaulted the senses.

He carefully took a sip and incredulously covered his throat, stepping back twice in surprise: "Good..."

Thump, Song Tianjing fell to the ground, lying there unconscious.

He did not know how much time had passed, but when Song Tianjing woke up again the fish soup had cooled. He staggered to his feet, feeling that something was different about himself.

He sensed his body briefly and his eyes widened in surprise.

"Did I break through to the Foundation Establishment Late Stage?"

He had been stuck at the Foundation Establishment Middle Stage for a long time, struggling to advance, and to think he had broken through after just waking up?!

"And this physique, doesn’t it closely resemble the Poison Immune Body mentioned in the book, with Immunity to Hundred Poisons?"

Chapter 1089: 1088: The Unfathomable Depths of the Complete Culinary Compendium

The leftover Five Poisons he had previously used for his soup had yet to be depleted, Song Tianjing bit into a dried scorpion, which would normally have turned his lips purple and made him dizzy to the point of directly passing out if it were severe.

But now, after eating an entire dried scorpion, nothing happened to him.

"The Poison Immune Body, indeed this is the Poison Immune Body!" Song Tianjing was ecstatic, such a legendary physique had actually appeared on him.

Although his Poison Immune Body was far from even beginner level, this was a good start.

Song Tianjing was over the moon, just one dish had not only caused his breakthrough but also given him a physique impervious to all poisons!

Once his excitement subsided, Song Tianjing pondered calmly on why the recipes in the book granted him immunity to poison.

"That’s it, in Ancient Times the Human race didn’t know which foods were edible and which were not, poisonous substances were everywhere, and the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, in order for the Human race to flourish, designated the first dish in the cookbook as a detoxifying dish, so that the Human race could have the ability not to be poisoned to death."

"The Ancestor of Spirit Chef took great pains indeed."

With the first fish soup having such an effect, Song Tianjing couldn’t wait to study the second dish. When he was cooking the soup before, he had already glanced through the cookbook, but now he wanted to study it meticulously.

"The name of the second dish is Eight Treasure Chicken Duck Fish."

"Don’t throw away the fish bones from the first dish; the fish bones absorbed the purification of the first dish. Retrieve them, dry them, grind them into powder, and use it as the starter for Eight Treasure Chicken Duck Fish."

"A starter?" Song Tianjing was full of questions, he had only heard of medicine starters, this was the first time he heard of a dish starter.

Never mind, just do as the book instructs.

"Feed the chicken and duck with the fish bone powder, they will fall into a coma, then take advantage of their state to slaughter them."

"The chicken and duck’s internal organs contain the essence of the Five Elements, so they must not be discarded. Cut open the duck’s belly, stuff it with the chicken, then add eight precious ingredients and steam for two hours..."

The ingredients listed in the cookbook were too preposterous, some were ancient exotic species, Divine Grass and Immortal Flower, others were long extinct and impossible to find, thus Song Tianjing had to find the best substitutes he could.

Two hours later, Song Tianjing looked at the chicken and duck in the plate with their eyes unshut in death, feeling that his substitutes were too excessive?

No matter, let’s eat.

Song Tianjing braced himself and took a bite, and indeed, it was a delicacy of the Mortal World, the taste of duck in the chicken, the taste of chicken in the duck, and upon closer tasting, there was also a taste of fish, the three flavors dancing on the tip of the tongue, with an endless aftertaste.

Song Tianjing wolfed down the dish, patting his belly afterward with a satisfied smile on his face.

He felt something sticky on his body, and looking down, he discovered a black substance secreting from his skin, both fishy and foul-smelling.

"Washing Marrow?" Song Tianjing’s voice trembled; he had taken Bone Marrow Cleansing Pills, but since the elixirs were too low-grade, a few uses and they were useless. Besides, he couldn’t afford higher-end ones.

Could it be that just by eating the second dish, he had undergone a Washing Marrow process?!

After taking a bath, Song Tianjing felt lighter and immediately couldn’t wait to start on the third dish.

The third dish was named Red Dust Memories.

Song Tianjing, as usual, used low-grade substitutes and after eating, once again fell into a coma.

When he woke up, he found that his body had not undergone the same changes as before.

"Strange, how long was I out?"

Song Tianjing emerged from his house in a daze, only to learn upon inquiring that he had been comatose for seven days, and today was the day the Spirit Chef exam was supposed to take place!

He rushed over to the Spirit Chef competition, signed up for the Foundation Establishment Group Spirit Chef exam, and by chance met the Holy Daughter of the Jade Rabbit Clan, which also led to the Saint Heir of the Five Poison Clan holding a grudge against him.

The exam’s theme was "fish," but the finished product couldn’t show any fish.

The Saint Heir of the Five Poison Clan secretly poisoned the fish for Song Tianjing, but Song Tianjing’s Pearl Fish Soup actually required the Five Poisons, which rather improved the quality of the dish.

Then Song Tianjing made a second dish with fish bones, which was widely praised by the judges.

The Holy Daughter of the Jade Rabbit Clan, sitting below the stage, had her beautiful eyes continuously conveying silent admiration and her heart secretly promised.

Song Tianjing won first place once again with the third dish in the Spirit Chef Competition, enjoying unparalleled glory.

However, the people from the Spirit Chef Alliance recognized that the dish made by Song Tianjing was from the Complete Culinary Compendium, a forbidden recipe, and secretly sent an assassin after him.

During the assassination attempt, Song Tianjing continually made breakthroughs, acquiring new recipes and successfully turning the tables on his attacker.

In the end, Song Tianjing obtained the entire Complete Culinary Compendium, and in the Great Xia Palace, he faced off against the Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance in a culinary duel, winning two out of three matches, and overthrowing the Alliance Hierarch’s rank.

He also became the universally acknowledged successor to the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, generation by generation.

Before Emperor Xia, Song Tianjing took the hand of the Holy Daughter of the Jade Rabbit Clan, looking at her with deep affection, "Yu’er, I like you, let’s get married."

The two closed their eyes and kissed, but when Song Tianjing woke up again, he found himself lying on the ground, his head aching as if he had hit it on something.

Struggling to his feet, he touched the bowl of Pearl Fish Soup on the table; the soup was still warm, his eyes filled with confusion.

"What is real, and what is fake..."

...

Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng walked side by side through the bustling square, holding freshly baked meat pies, taking bites that left their mouths oozing with grease.

The air around them was filled with the aroma of various foods, enough to make anyone’s mouth water.

"This place sure has a lot of good food." Although Lu Yang wasn’t particular about food, after a round of eating, he felt that accepting the Spirit Chef Alliance’s invitation was the right choice.

"Right? Right? I told you this place is great," Yun Mengmeng said with a smile that brightened her eyes.

Both of them were cultivators, with virtually bottomless stomachs, able to eat endlessly as long as they desired.

And without worrying about overeating to the point of losing their appetite.

"Where is that seventh-generation successor from the Yangyuan Building, though? Why haven’t we seen him?" Lu Yang wondered. It was already the second day, and the guy had said he wanted to rent a stall to make money; why wasn’t he seen after making a round? The guy seemed honest enough; he couldn’t have taken the money and run, could he?

"He’s probably studying my recipes; after all, my recipes aren’t something one can understand at a glance," Fairy Eternity confidently stated.

"What’s being sold over there, chunks of soil?" Yun Mengmeng noticed a small, quiet snack stall with little business, where two Spirit Chefs were stir-frying soil chunks in an iron pot.

"Is this some sort of local specialty?" Yun Mengmeng asked curiously, pulling Lu Yang over with her.

"Boss, two portions, please."

"Coming right up."

One of the Spirit Chefs served two bowls of soil chunks, and Yun Mengmeng took a bite, making a face of disgust.

"Isn’t this just ordinary soil chunks?"

Lu Yang was also perplexed; while others sold food, these people were selling soil. What was the meaning of this?

Could it be that these soil chunks contain some mystery?

Lu Yang activated his spiritual essence, employing Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth, and took a bite as well.

"These aren’t just ordinary soil chunks, are they mixed with some Soil of Rest?"

Seeing this, the two Spirit Chefs exchanged a knowing look and quietly approached, "Fellow Daoist, you’ve come quite late, you might be the last one here, the meeting is about to begin, please, this way."

Lu Yang: "..."

What are you two talking about?

Chapter 1090: 1089: This Leader Position Should Be Mine!

Lu Yang observed the expressions of the two Spirit Chefs, which seemed like they were making contact on the street corner.

Could it be that a random movement of his just now happened to be their secret contact signal?

Let’s see then.

Thinking this, Lu Yang’s face broke into an enthusiastic smile, "I was delayed by some matters on the way, but fortunately, I still made it on time."

"Sister Mengmeng, I have something to take care of, so I can’t accompany you shopping. Let’s meet at the inn tonight."

"Alright." Yun Mengmeng found this change puzzling, but since the Second-in-command had said so, she didn’t ask more.

"Fellow cultivator, this way please." One of the Spirit Chefs gestured invitingly for Lu Yang to follow him, while the other stayed at the stall.

Lu Yang used his Spiritual Sense to scan and discovered that both Spirit Chefs were at the Divinity Transformation Stage, and both had sheep horns on their heads.

Are they from the Sheep Clan?

"They are from the Taotie Clan." Immortal Fairy said, Lu Yang was unfamiliar with the Taotie Clan and couldn’t identify the roots of these two Spirit Chefs, but she was very familiar with the Taotie Clan.

"The Taotie Clan? Did they see me use Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth and mistakenly think I was one of them?"

Lu Yang quickly figured out what was going on.

If consuming soil was the secret contact signal, then only by employing Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth could you swallow it, whereas someone like Yun Mengmeng who ate it directly would be judged an outsider.

Lu Yang followed one of the Spirit Chefs out of the marketplace, opened a secret door, and entered a crowded underground chamber.

After entering the underground chamber, the Spirit Chef returned to the stall to continue frying soil blocks.

The underground chamber was filled with people who had sheep horns, others with different demon features, and some with no demon features at all.

Noticing the door of the underground chamber opening, the people in the back row all looked toward Lu Yang, realized they did not recognize him, and then turned their heads back.

"There are more than just Taotie Clan here?" Lu Yang puzzled, if there were other demons and humans aside from the Taotie Clan, how did they pass the test of eating soil.

"Probably these people have ingested the blood of the Taotie Clan. Ingesting the blood of the Taotie Clan can also teach you to master Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth, although the effectiveness is definitely not as good as that of the Taotie Clan itself."

"And after ingesting the blood of the Taotie Clan, the Taotie Clan will exert a suppressive power over them, a common tactic used by the Taotie Clan to build alliances."

"The time has come, and everyone should be here now," said the middle-aged cultivator with sheep horns standing at the front.

"Is that person the Taotie Clan Leader Jin Tan?" Lu Yang vaguely remembered seeing him once at the Demon Nation’s founding ceremony, a transcendent Demon Emperor ranked among the top ten in power in the Demon Territory and a senior minister of the Demon Nation.

As soon as Jin Tan spoke, everyone immediately fell silent.

Jin Tan looked at the crowd and slowly said, "You all should more or less know the purpose for which I have gathered you."

The crowd looked up at Jin Tan standing on high, their eyes fiery and fists clenched tight, as if ready to brave boiling waters and step through fire for their beliefs.

Lu Yang had no choice but to pretend the same, convincingly.

Jin Tan was very pleased with the crowd’s reaction, knowing that plotting big endeavors wouldn’t be possible without these people’s support.

"Nowadays, the leader is foolish, dictatorial, and disregards advice from others, even absurdly deciding to hold this Spirit Chef competition in Demon City!"

"And look at the current state of our organization, the internal management is chaotic, the leader does nothing, reluctant to manage, allowing infighting and wasting our strength!"

"It has not been easy for the organization to reach its current size, yet if we let the leader manage like this, it will certainly head toward decline!"

Jin Tan scanned the crowd, his technique of observing people was skillful and slow, as though making eye contact with everyone, giving a sense of being respected.

"You come from the Demon Territory, Great Xia, the East Sea, the Extreme Northern Lands: from all corners of the world, each with Divine Abilities. My Taotie Clan has spent hundreds of years and all our resources to groom you for this moment!"

Lu Yang’s eyes narrowed slightly, was this the start of a rebellion against Zhu Tian? Perhaps they had a Semi-Immortal backing them, which would indeed be very interesting.

Jin Tan’s expression revealed a righteous indignation as he shouted loudly, "Everyone, what should we do!"

His words ignited the atmosphere like a blaze, with the crowd responding by raising their arms and shouting, "Overthrow him, overthrow him!"

"Exactly, Elder Ming Yuan should have stepped down long ago, that leader’s position should rightfully be mine!"

Lu Yang was startled. Elder Ming Yuan? Wasn’t he the Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance?

It then dawned on Lu Yang that Jin Tan had another identity, the Vice Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance.

So, you orchestrated all this just to become the Alliance Hierarch?

"To defeat Elder Ming Yuan, the only way is to beat him in culinary skills so thoroughly that he accepts defeat wholeheartedly!"

"I have devised a dish from the mortal world which will surely surpass Elder Ming Yuan."

"However, gathering the ingredients is immensely challenging, and four of the ingredients are stored in the Spirit Chef Alliance’s ingredient reserve. These four are extremely precious, and acquiring them requires the unanimous agreement of the three vice alliance hierarchs and the Alliance Hierarch."

"But there is one exception, which is to win at the Spirit Chef Competition!"

"The Spirit Chef Competition is divided into five groups: Foundation Establishment Group, Golden Core Group, Nascent Soul Group, Divinity Transformation Group, and Unification Group. The champion of each group may select one ingredient from the ingredient store!"

"What you must do is win the competition by any means necessary! Remember, by any means!"

"Yes!" everyone shouted in unison, making the basement tremble.

Having boosted morale, Jin Tan’s tone was no longer so passionate.

"There’s one more thing, some of you have been diligently practicing your culinary skills, yet have never qualified as Spirit Chefs."

"If you were to take the Spirit Chef qualification exam, there’s a risk that your skills might be exposed."

"So I can use my authority to grant you the title of Spirit Chef without needing to pass the exam, allowing you direct entry to the Spirit Chef Competition."

"Now, those who haven’t obtained their Spirit Chef qualification, stay behind."

"A Spirit Chef Competition, huh? I’ve never tried it, why not seize the opportunity and experience it?" Lu Yang thought, since he was there to report on the Spirit Chef Competition, it was only natural to experience the whole process.

Over half the people gradually left, including Lu Yang, who was among the small group remaining to line up and register their names.

"What’s your name?" Jin Tan asked when it was Lu Yang’s turn.

"Lu Yunyang."

"Good, next."

Lu Yang’s mask was of such high quality that even those at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage could not discern anything amiss.

...

After parting with Lu Yang, Yun Mengmeng hummed a tune and continued to wander the streets and feast.

However, without Lu Yang accompanying her, many cultivators approached Yun Mengmeng, finding her beautiful and adorable, much to her annoyance.

"So annoying, I can’t even eat in peace, I’ll go somewhere else."

Yun Mengmeng muttered to herself and left the market, winding through the alleys.

"This small restaurant looks decent," Yun Mengmeng discovered a restaurant with a new sign deep in the alley, though it appeared to be lacking in business.

She lifted the door curtain and entered the clean and tidy shop, indicating that the owner was someone who valued cleanliness.

"Is anyone there?"

A woman in a sky-blue dress came out of the kitchen, wiping her hands and apologetically said, "I’m sorry, dear customer, we’ve run out of ingredients, the owner has gone out to buy some."

Chapter 1091: 1090: It’s Good That Everything is Fine

"Is there really nothing to eat?" Yun Mengmeng’s eyes revealed a barely detectable hint of dimness.

She had thought she would get to enjoy some good food here, but her luck had turned out to be so bad that all the ingredients had been used up.

"Not even a little bit left?" Yun Mengmeng asked, unwilling to give up.

The woman in the sky-blue dress recalled what was left in the kitchen and seriously said, "There’s still a dish of garlic pickles."

"... then never mind. Have you eaten yet?"

The woman in the sky-blue dress paused, "No, why?"

"Then let’s eat together." Yun Mengmeng excitedly lifted up the paper bags she was carrying, which were packed full, and warmly invited the woman in the sky-blue dress.

Eating while walking was never as comfortable as sitting down to eat.

She had originally planned on ordering a few dishes in the restaurant and then enjoying some Spirit Food from the paper bags, but now it looked like she’d have to slightly modify her plan.

The woman in the sky-blue dress felt that Yun Mengmeng had no ill intentions. Sharing a meal didn’t seem too bad, so she agreed, "That works."

Yun Mengmeng tore open the paper bags, and the woman in the sky-blue dress went to the kitchen to grab some bowls and chopsticks. On a small table, the paper bags were arranged fully.

"Try this meat sesame seed cake; both the Chief Housekeeper and the Second-in-command think it’s delicious." Having company for the meal swept away the previous gloom, and Yun Mengmeng’s mood greatly improved.

The woman in the sky-blue dress delicately took a small bite, poured the soup juice into a spoon, and then carefully ate the meat sesame seed cake. Her movements were elegant.

"It’s really good."

Seeing this, Yun Mengmeng felt her own eating manners were somewhat lacking and elegantly mimicked the woman’s style.

During their conversation, she learned that the woman in the sky-blue dress was called Little Lotus, the head chef of the restaurant, which was only staffed by her and the owner. Due to the shortage of staff, the owner had to personally go out to buy groceries, leaving Little Lotus to mind the store. Their daily lives sounded quite tight, which probably explained why the restaurant was located in such a remote place.

Little Lotus also found out that the guest was named Mengmeng, and from her frequent mentions of the Chief Housekeeper and the Second-in-command, and her regular stays in the mountains, she seemed likely to be part of some mountain bandits. Little Lotus thought the girl didn’t seem very bright, probably tricked into going up the mountain, and listening to her, it appeared that the Second-in-command often deceived the Chief Housekeeper, not exactly an upstanding character.

Clang.

The two clinked their tea cups together and continued their earlier conversation.

"Is your shop really that unlucky? You had the demonic sect come before, which caused a fight and almost flattened your place. Luckily, your boss had some skills to save your small shop. After that incident, did you all feel the original place was too dangerous, so you moved to Demon City?"

Yun Mengmeng, eating her turtle jelly sprinkled with honey, was quite surprised by Little Lotus’s experiences.

"You sound like you’ve had a tough time too, running away from home to your close sister. Then your sister finds you a Chief Housekeeper and Second-in-command, who then leave you to fend for yourself, following the two bosses around."

Little Lotus, eating freshly baked mung bean cakes, which were warm and brought out the full fragrance of the mung beans leaving a persistent aroma, only found them a bit too dry.

"It’s actually not so bad. Both the Chief Housekeeper and the Second-in-command are good people. Originally, the Second-in-command was reluctant to come to Demon City, but he came because I asked."

The more Little Lotus listened, the more she felt that Yun Mengmeng might have been deceived.

Seeing that their food was finished, Little Lotus stood up to see her out, "Oh, Mengmeng, how long will you be staying in Demon City?"

"Will you come back here?" Little Lotus rarely had the chance to chat with someone, and she felt a strange kinship talking with Mengmeng today, wanting to chat more, given that there were still many days until the Spirit Chef competition ended.

"I might." Yun Mengmeng felt that after the Second-in-command was pulled away, it would probably be quite busy afterward, not like these last few days when he accompanied her shopping. Better not to trouble the Second-in-command anymore; coming here to sit seemed nice.

"How about you come over in the evening? Do you have time? The boss usually goes out to buy groceries then, and she... she really doesn’t like me interacting with other people."

"Sure." Yun Mengmeng agreed cheerfully. It’s probably something no boss would want, a cook chatting with customers during work hours.

After Yun Mengmeng left, a while later, the woman in the gray skirt came back with a basket of vegetables. Seeing Little Lotus wiping the tables, she felt curious.

"Is there something good going on? Why do you seem happier than before I left?"

"You’re overthinking it."

The woman in the grey skirt instantly became alert. Had Lu Yang been here?

No, she could always sense Lu Yang’s location, and he hadn’t come towards their shop.

The woman in the grey skirt shook her head. She was overthinking.

Ever since she learned that Lu Yang had connections with Immortal Fairy, the woman in gray had had a premonition that the person she was looking for was related to Lu Yang—finding Lu Yang would lead her to Immortal Fairy.

However, when she sensed that Lu Yang had arrived in Demon City, the woman in gray found herself in a dilemma. But she didn’t know what she was struggling with—was she afraid of meeting Immortal Fairy or was it something else? She didn’t know, and she had never felt this way before.

"Let me help you." The woman in grey grabbed the rag from Little Lotus and began wiping the tables and chairs.

...

"Second-in-command, are you there?" Yun Mengmeng knocked on Lu Yang’s door.

"Yes, come in."

Lu Yang had thought Yun Mengmeng would return earlier than him, but it turned out he was back first.

Yun Mengmeng, carrying a few paper bags, placed them on Lu Yang’s table. These were items she had bought on her way back.

"I found a few types of tasty Spirit Food that I think neither the Chief Housekeeper nor the Second-in-command have tried yet. Want to try?"

"Yes, yes."

Lu Yang said yes twice, one of which was on behalf of Immortal Fairy.

In later times, Spirit Chefs were often creative; many types of Spirit Food were things that even Immortal Fairy had never seen.

Often, Lu Yang took a bite, Immortal Fairy possessed Lu Yang to take a bite, then returned the body to Lu Yang, and back in the Spiritual Space, she would incessantly criticize where the Spirit Food fell short, while Lu Yang just listened to Immortal Fairy’s commentary while eating.

"What did they pull you away for just now, Second-in-command?"

"It was the Taotie Clan Leader, wanting to compete for the position of the Spirit Chef Alliance Hierarch. He’s trained a large group to participate in the Spirit Chef Competition to win ingredients, allowing him to create his ideal dishes."

"Because I eat dirt, they thought I might use the Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth technique. Well, I do know how to use Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth, but anyway, it was a bit of a misunderstanding, and they mistook me for one of their own, so they wanted me to participate in the Spirit Chef Competition too."

"I thought it would be a novel experience, so I just went along with it."

Yun Mengmeng felt that the Second-in-command indeed had a great destiny, to have encountered a power struggle at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage so quickly.

"Sister Mengmeng, did you run into some trouble? You came back so late?"

"Not really no, I just happened to meet a Spirit Chef I could chat with, so we talked a bit more," Yun Mengmeng said sheepishly.

"As long as you’re okay. If anything happens, let me know. We have connections here in Demon City."

Chapter 1092: 1091 Nine Star Spirit Chef

As Yun Mengmeng had guessed, Lu Yang had been very busy during the following days.

As the saying goes, love what you do; since he was to participate in the Spirit Chef competition, merely having the connections to qualify was far from enough. He needed at least a basic understanding of Spirit Chefs.

One of the fundamental skills of a Spirit Chef, for example, is bone and meat removal without damaging the bones.

Spirit Chefs have their own ranking system, divided into nine stars, corresponding to the stages of Qi Cultivation, Foundation Establishment, Golden Core, Nascent Soul, Divinity Transformation, Void Refinement, Body Integration, Transcendance Tribulation, and Immortal.

The Qi Cultivation stage corresponds to one star, while the Immortal corresponds to nine stars.

A six-star Spirit Chef at the Void Refinement stage is basically just a title. When a five-star Spirit Chef from the Divinity Transformation stage advances to the Void Refinement Stage, the Spirit Chef Alliance grants them the title of six-star Spirit Chef.

There is only one nine-star Spirit Chef, and that is the legendary Ancestor of Spirit Chefs, Yingtian Immortal!

Lu Yang had uniquely favorable conditions for studying the Spirit Chef knowledge, as the real legendary nine-star Spirit Chef, Fairy Eternity, personally taught him.

"Little Yang, look, this is the structure of the Kui Ox. This white line is where the bone meets the meat. When removing bones and flesh, start at the seam."

In the Spiritual Space, Fairy Eternity squatted on the ground, drawing simplified sketches with a stick. If she hadn’t said this was a Kui Ox, Lu Yang might have thought it was a skewer of two hawthorns, the first one being the head and the second the body.

"Fairy, maybe we should get a real object to practice on," Lu Yang earnestly suggested. At this rate, he feared he would forget what a real Kui Ox looked like, let alone learn how to debone one.

However, Lu Yang couldn’t directly criticize Fairy Eternity’s drawing skills; changing a Spirit Chef to learn from might hurt her feelings.

"Getting a real object, that might indeed be a good idea."

What Lu Yang needed wasn’t just the body of a Kui Ox, but many bodies of Demon Beasts.

Obtaining a large number of Demon Beast bodies by buying them himself would be troublesome. The simplest method was to have someone buy them for him.

"Is the Gold Clan Leader available?" Instead of troubling Zhu Tian, Lu Yang approached the Qiongqi Clan.

Zhu Tian was, after all, a ruler of a nation, and his regular duties kept him busy. It was hard enough for him to spare time for a meeting; such a small affair wasn’t worth bothering him about.

After Zhu Tian established his nation, aside from the Dragon and Phoenix Clans, all the Demon Clans with a Transcendance Tribulation Demon Emperor settled in Demon City, including the Qiongqi Clan.

"Who are you?"

Lu Yang couldn’t interact with the Qiongqi Clan under his real identity, so he used a disguise.

Lu Yang took out a small wooden sword, which contained one of his Sword Qi: "Give this to the Gold Clan Leader; he will come out to greet me himself."

Hearing this, the Qiongqi Clan guard did not dare to underestimate Lu Yang and took the small wooden sword.

Soon after, the Gold Clan Leader hurried out from his clan and personally welcomed Lu Yang, escorting him to his Abode before Lu Yang dispelled his disguise.

"Lu... my nephew... Daoist friend... Senior?"

The Gold Clan Leader changed his address three times, clearly unsure how to address Lu Yang.

He should be subordinated to Lu Yang, considering the Heavenly Court Young Sect Master’s lineage.

But he was also Daoist Buyu’s younger brother, which technically made him of a higher generation than Lu Yang.

"You flatter me too much, Clan Leader. It’s too much for me to be addressed as a ’Daoist friend’ or ’Senior.’"

"Young Sect Master, please, have a seat," the Gold Clan Leader finally stopped fussing over titles and warmly welcomed Lu Yang.

Since their Qiongqi Clan started handling the business of Recording Orbs, their days had finally improved, compensating for the loss they faced from monopolizing the candle industry. From this standpoint, Lu Yang was practically their clan’s lifesaver.

"I’ve come to purchase some Demon Beast bodies to practice my culinary skills."

"Demon Beast corpse? That’s easy, Young Sect Master, what kind of Demon Beast corpse do you need?" Since Jin Caiwei had made an appearance in the battle of Imperial City, the status of the Qiongqi Clan had dramatically risen too. Among the Demon Race, aside from the Dragon and Phoenix Clans and the Di Jiang Clan, the Qiongqi Clan had a high status and many channels.

"The more types, the better."

"Alright, give me a little time." Gold Clan Leader hastily bid farewell to Lu Yang and went out to instruct his subordinates to collect various types of Demon Beast corpses.

Not long after, the door was pushed open again, but it wasn’t Gold Clan Leader who entered.

"Young Sect Master, how come you didn’t notify me when you arrived in Demon City?" Little Qiongqi Jin Caiwei, upon hearing the report from Gold Clan Leader, put down what she was doing and rushed over directly.

Lu Yang smiled as he stood up and clasped his hands together. "Knowing Senior Jin is busy, I wouldn’t dare to disturb you."

"Where’s the talk? You are the Young Sect Master and our Qiongqi Clan’s benefactor. If you come to see me, there’s no disturbance at all."

"Have you received all the Spirit Stones I sent over?"

"I have, and that’s way too many. I only offered a suggestion, it’s not worth so many Spirit Stones." Given Lu Yang’s current worth, not to mention those in Divinity Transformation Stage, even some in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage don’t have as much wealth as Lu Yang.

For example, Daoist Buyu.

Soon Gold Clan Leader had delivered an endless stream of Demon Beast corpses—those that swam in water, crawled on land, or flew in the sky, with not one being of the same type, too much to fit in the Abode.

Lu Yang simply moved them all into the Green Blade Sword Small World.

Jin Caiwei had not yet visited the Green Blade Sword Small World and was curious about what Lu Yang intended to do, so she followed him in.

"Little Tiger, it’s been a while." Upon entering the Green Blade Sword Small World, Immortal Fairy appeared, smiling as she tousled Jin Caiwei’s head, messing up her hair.

"Immortal Sister!"

"So well-behaved."

"Little Yang, bring out this Kui Ox." Immortal Fairy pointed at a one-legged cow demon among the Demon Beast corpses.

"Alright."

Lu Yang lifted the Kui Ox out, and the piled-up Demon Beast corpses crashed down, but Lu Yang moved quickly, and they didn’t hit him.

"The meat of the Demon Beasts has a different taste depending on the part." The Nine Star Spirit Chef was sharing her experience.

"That sounds a bit troublesome. Oh, Little Tiger, press this ox down with the Outline of Dao Fruit."

Jin Caiwei activated the Plane Dao Fruit Outline, pressing the Kui Ox into a sheet of paper.

"That makes it easier to explain," Immortal Fairy said as she drew circles on it, "This part is called ’tenderloin’, this here is ’beef shank’, this is ’beef shank’, and this is called ’handle’..."

Lu Yang nodded and took note, earnestly learning.

Jin Caiwei was also learning on the side; it was her first time hearing this information, and she realized she had been far too casual about her food before.

"Have you remembered everything?"

"I have."

"Carve out these parts."

Lu Yang held the Inheriting Shadow Sword, facing the Kui Ox carcass as if it were a formidable enemy. The body structure of the Kui Ox was clear under his powerful Spiritual Sense, and as the sword light flashed, the skeleton collapsed and scattered into pieces, and the various parts were neatly arranged on a platter.

"Ha ha, turns out it’s quite simple."

In the vast Green Blade Sword Small World, where Demon Beast corpses piled up like mountains and emanated an ominous aura, the Blood Clothes Swordsman walked among the corpses. Each swing of his blade brought another Demon Beast to the ground, brutally mutilated and unrecognizable from what they once were, leaving only scattered bones. The Blood Clothes Swordsman laughed maniacally...

According to the official "Dou Book·Emperor Chronicles," the Green Blade Sword’s Small World, possessed by Emperor Dou Ling Lu Yang, was littered with carcasses and piled with skeletons.

Chapter 1093: 1092: How Many Can Be Considered Cultivators with the Hundred Skills of Cultivation at Their Side

In the kitchen, amidst a chaos of pots, pans, ladles, and basins scattered across the floor, along with food and medicinal herbs, Song Tianjing’s dark circles under his eyes were pronounced; he had not slept for five days, completely engrossed in the "Complete Culinary Compendium," having completely lost track of time.

Of course, this also had to do with his inability to differentiate between dreams and reality.

Song Tianjing opened the window; the morning sunlight, though not too strong, still seemed harsh to his eyes.

"Damn, I was so absorbed in studying the recipes that I nearly forgot there’s a Spirit Chef exam today!"

One had to become a Spirit Chef first in order to compete in the Spirit Chef competition—the exam was something he absolutely couldn’t miss!

He didn’t bother to tidy up the kitchen and dashed straight to the site of the Spirit Chef exam.

...

"Qian’er, the Clan Leader has already agreed to our marriage. He will soon come to propose to the Jade Rabbit Clan, and it will surely breed a Tribulation Crossing Demon Emperor!"

"Young Master Wu, you should give up on that idea. There’s no possibility between us!" Qian’er, dressed in a white skirt and socks, her white hair shining, even the long ears atop her head were snow-white without a single blemish, extremely rare.

Young Master Wu, agitated upon hearing this, grabbed the wrist of the girl named Qian’er: "Qian’er, do you still not understand my feelings for you? I truly like you!"

"Let go of me!" Qian’er struggled, feeling repulsed by the contact with Young Master Wu.

"Qian’er, actually I..."

"Make way, make way!" Song Tianjing, looking disheveled and dirty, rushed to the registration desk. He had intended to bypass Young Master Wu and Qian’er, but with five days devoid of sleep, his sense of distance and direction were somewhat out of control, and he accidentally bumped into Young Master Wu.

"I’m so sorry, are you alright?"

"Are you blind to bump into me, do you know who I am? I am the Saint Heir of the Five Poison Clan!" Young Master Wu was furious to see Song Tianjing mess up his prospects, but Qian’er secretly breathed a sigh of relief, using the opportunity to escape from Young Master Wu.

Song Tianjing internally cursed his luck, as since coming to Demon City he had made a few acquaintances among the Cultivators of the Demon Race at the Foundation Establishment Stage. They all said the one person not to offend among the Foundation Establishment Stage was this retaliatory Saint Heir of the Five Poison Clan.

The girl standing next to Young Master Wu was someone he had met once before, Yu Qian’er of the Jade Rabbit Clan.

"My apologies, Saint Wu. It’s almost time, I need to go and register. After the exam, I’ll come to your door to formally apologize!"

Song Tianjing finally managed to sign up for the exam in the nick of time, just as the incense stick burned out.

Watching the anxious figure of Song Tianjing as he scrambled to register, a cold smile formed on the corner of Young Master Wu’s mouth: "It seems he wants to become a Spirit Chef, just like you and me."

"I will let him understand the cost of offending me!"

"Wu, I warn you, you better not lay a hand on him!" Qian’er said anxiously. Although it was unintentional, Song Tianjing had indeed helped her out of a tight spot, and she could not just stand by and watch Young Master Wu trip him up.

Seeing Qian’er defending Song Tianjing stirred even more displeasure in Young Master Wu.

"Don’t worry, Qian’er, he’s just a fool; I’m not that trivial," Young Master Wu said with a benign smile, but inwardly he scoffed with contempt.

It was too late to argue; while Song Tianjing was registering, Young Master Wu used a poisoned needle and secretly poisoned him. The poison was mild, but it was timed to take effect right during the Spirit Chef exam—collapsing on the stove would certainly be an interesting scene.

Seeing the darkness around the kid’s eyes, a clear sign of lack of sleep, if he fell asleep during the exam, nobody would think anything of it.

However, when the exam ended and he saw Song Tianjing emerge unscathed from the examination hall, sporting a two-star sleeve badge, he was astounded.

How could this be; the kid wasn’t poisoned?!

"Young Master Wu, so you’re still waiting here for me. I am truly sorry for before!" Song Tianjing, seeing that Young Master Wu was still there, hurried over to offer his sincere apologies.

"I don’t know why, but before the registration I was all groggy, and after it ended, suddenly my mind was clear, and I performed exceptionally well while cooking. The examiner couldn’t stop praising my dish."

Mr. Wu was startled. Absorbing poison as power, the characteristic of the Poison Immune Body?

What a joke, this kid has the very Poison Immune Body that restrains their Five Poison Clan?

Qian’er blinked her eyes; she wasn’t foolish and guessed that since Mr. Wu was the kind to seek revenge for the smallest grievances, he must have poisoned Song Tianjing, yet it was easily neutralized by him.

Song Tianjing was unassuming in appearance, but he had quite a few secrets.

After Song Tianjing and Qian’er had left, Mr. Wu also revealed a two-star sleeve badge.

"The Spirit Chef Competition is tomorrow, and I personally will take action. I really want to see just how capable you are!"

...

"The Spirit Chef Competition is tomorrow, Little Lotus, aren’t you participating?" Yun Mengmeng, as usual, bought a large quantity of Spirit Food to share with Little Lotus. These were all things she had tried and found delicious.

She’d share them again with the Second-in-command once she got home at night.

Yun Mengmeng felt sweet inside; sharing once could bring double the happiness, and sharing twice meant quadruple the joy!

"Unfortunately, I can’t leave this place." Little Lotus sighed. Since the battle at the Western Paradise Temple, she had been restricted by Ashy Bean and couldn’t leave the shop.

"What a pity. Our Second-in-command is joining the competition, and I wanted to introduce you to him."

"Your Second-in-command is also entering the competition?" Little Lotus frowned upon hearing this. Mengmeng had mentioned that the "Second-in-command" had reluctantly come to Demon City just because of her. Now, he even qualified to participate in the Spirit Chef Competition? Could that be called reluctant?

This Second-in-command even deceived such an innocent person as Mengmeng; he surely was not a good person!

...

The Qiongqi Clan has been eating well these days, under the guidance of a Nine Star Spirit Chef, supervision by the ancestor, and a Five Star Spirit Chef handling the knife. They had a variety of Demon Beasts to eat, never repeating a single day.

In the Green Blade Sword Small World, only white bones remained on the ground; all the meat was consumed by the Qiongqi Clan, and Lu Yang’s cooking skills visibly improved before one’s eyes.

"It’s time to join the competition."

Lu Yang put on his sleeve badge, ready for departure. The badge had five black pentagrams, signifying his prestigious status as a Five Star Spirit Chef.

Lu Yang was considered, more or less, a Cultivator who possessed the Hundred Skills of Cultivation, even though this identity was secured through backdoors by the Taotie Clan.

After emerging from seclusion at the Qiongqi Clan, Yun Mengmeng waited at the clan’s entrance, accompanying him to the competition.

Fairy Eternity had shown her skills twice, and Jin Caiwei unfortunately suffered food poisoning and couldn’t accompany Lu Yang there.

"So many Spirit Chefs! There are way more than at the market." Yun Mengmeng had never seen so many Spirit Chefs in her life; she just wished she were a judge so she could taste all those delicious foods.

The competition registration site was crowded; the Spirit Chefs had varied appearances. Some Spirit Chefs shaved their heads clean and went shirtless, with two cleavers hanging around their necks; some carried a large pot on their back and a complete set of kitchenware at their waist; others, although they looked young, had fingers darkened and arms as withered as if petrified.

Compared to these people, Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng looked too normal and seemed somewhat out of place.

"The Spirit Chef Competition is the grand event of the Spirit Chef World, once every ten years. The champion will receive a Red Sleeve Badge, representing that you are the best among your peers."

"The Spirit Chefs we saw at the market a few days ago were only a part of them; many more hadn’t left their homes, sharpening their skills at the last minute."

"I see."

Including myself, Lu Yang added silently.

Chapter 1094: 1093 Gathering Intelligence

In the backstage of the Spirit Chef competition, Vice Alliance Hierarch Liu Xing anxiously waited for the Alliance Hierarch to arrive.

"The competition is about to begin, why hasn’t the Alliance Hierarch come?"

Ever since arriving in Demon City, the Alliance Hierarch had disappeared without a trace, leaving most of the organizing work for the Spirit Chef examination and competition to Liu Xing himself.

"Liu Xing, what’s the hurry? Haven’t I arrived?" A hoary and hearty laugh sounded by Liu Xing’s ear, followed by a cool breeze that transformed into a white-bearded elder—it was the Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance, Daoist Ming Yuan.

Daoist Ming Yuan had an ordinary background from a small island in the East Sea; there wasn’t much remarkable about his origins. However, after several strokes of fortune, he cultivated to the Body Integration Realm in the East Sea. Unlike other human cultivators, he didn’t take over a large island to become an Island Master but chose to travel and exchange culinary arts with master chefs from renowned restaurants, rarely returning to the East Sea.

And it was precisely because of this conduct that he not only excelled in culinary skills but also had excellent relations with others, allowing him to become the Alliance Hierarch of the Spirit Chef Alliance smoothly.

"Alliance Hierarch, you’ve finally come. Where have you been all these days?" Seeing Daoist Ming Yuan was like finding his backbone, allowing Liu Xing to feel as if a massive weight had been lifted from his shoulders.

"I was handling some matters outside the city, a bit troublesome, but it’s almost sort to out now. You’ve worked hard these days."

Liu Xing heaved a long sigh: "I don’t mind the hard work, but I’ve heard that Jin Tan plans to launch a challenge for the position of Alliance Hierarch, to take your place!"

A flicker of surprise crossed Daoist Ming Yuan’s eyes: "It’s been three hundred years since Jin Tan lost to me, hasn’t it? It seems like his culinary skills have improved considerably during these three hundred years."

"I don’t know about his culinary progress, but I do know that he’s concocted an exceptional dish. Its preparation is extremely difficult, necessitating the use of legendary ingredients from our alliance’s food stores, which is why he has yet to challenge you."

"This is the first I’ve heard about it. So he sent someone to this competition, hoping to win the prize and get those ingredients?"

"Yes, apparently, he’s been preparing for today for a full three hundred years."

The more Daoist Ming Yuan heard, the more he became bewildered: "Then why didn’t he just exchange for them directly? We didn’t know he needed the legendary ingredients for his exceptional dish—surely we wouldn’t have stopped him if he had wanted to exchange?"

"Perhaps he assumed we knew?"

The two men exchanged glances.

"By the way, Alliance Hierarch, you know about Lu Yang, right?"

"Of course, I know him; wasn’t it I who invited him?" Daoist Ming Yuan looked at Liu Xing as if he were looking at an idiot, thinking that without him as the Alliance Hierarch, the Spirit Chef Alliance would truly be beyond help.

"Oh right, it was you who invited him. You would never guess who he saw after I sent him to the hostel!" Liu Xing had been holding in the news for several days, and upon meeting the Alliance Hierarch, he couldn’t contain it any longer.

"Who did he see?"

Liu Xing was about to say it when the order from Zhu Tian, in the form of the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline, forbidding him from speaking about the meeting, took effect. He couldn’t reveal it, neither explicitly nor by implication.

"Who exactly he saw, I am not at liberty to say, but I saw many beautiful dancers at his door, all likely to be gifts to him!"

"You mean to say that Lu Yang is a lustful person, and that we should also try to bribe him with women’s favors?"

Liu Xing had intended to hint at "Guess who sent the women to Lu Yang", but it turned into merely suggesting the scenario.

Under the influence of the Survival of the Fittest Dao Fruit Outline, Liu Xing couldn’t speak; he could only nod vigorously.

Seeing Liu Xing nod, Daoist Ming Yuan hissed, "Could it be true that after Lu Yang’s visit to the Laurel Fairy Palace, many fairies are truly pregnant? Not just rumors?"

...

"Look quickly, that’s Golden Blade Master. He really came!" Golden Blade Master performs bone trimming for the cow demon daily in the market, throwing cows into the air and brandishing his blades, making the loudest noise. After several days of buzz, his reputation is the loudest in the market.

Golden Blade Master, bare-chested, has two golden blades hanging in front of his chest. The blade edges emit a fierce aura, likely stained with much demon beast blood. Among the Five Star Spirit Chefs skilled in handling ingredients, this fierce aura is particularly eye-catching; many Five Star Spirit Chefs consciously keep their distance from him.

"And that one, wearing a hood, is Poisonous Granny. Rumors say she practices Dual Cultivation of Elixir and Poison. On one hand, her medicinal food can heal any illness, but if she wants someone dead, it would only take her a word!"

"Look behind her, there are two cultivators. Judging by their attire, they seem to be from the Great Xia government office. It looks like she’s leading them, could it be that she has now defected to the Dynasty?"

"She’s also wearing handcuffs and shackles. Could it be she’s engaged in weighted cultivation, and will take them off when the official competition begins, drastically increasing her strength?"

Lu Yang, listening from the side, couldn’t help but comment, "No, doesn’t it look more like she was captured by Great Xia and is taking part in the competition during her sentence?"

"As you say that, I remember, it seems I’ve heard she was arrested by Great Xia on suspicion of poisoning," a passerby began, then realized something was off.

The previous conversation had been as casual as between friends, the two of them talking as if they knew each other, and it had totally slipped his mind that he didn’t recognize this person.

"Hello, my name is Lu Yunyang."

"Oh hello, hello, my name is Ma Bin... Still not right, who are you?"

"I’m here for the competition. I’m not familiar with these people, just thought I’d get to know some through you," Lu Yang indicated his Five Star sleeve badge, thinking about how he would write his article when he got back, coincidentally meeting someone who seemed to know everything.

Ma Bin rolled up his sleeve, showing his Five Star sleeve badge, and said disdainfully,

"As a fellow competitor, do you think I would reveal information to you?"

Lu Yang thought for a moment. It wasn’t quite right to inquire about information directly without offering any benefit. He prepared to take out something to sweeten the deal.

Ma Bin caught a glimpse of Lu Yang’s subtle move and sneered, "Don’t think you can buy me with Spirit Stones. This is a sacred Spirit Chef competition; don’t sully it with your measly Spirit Stones!"

Lu Yang hadn’t planned on using Spirit Stones at all. He took out his Identity Jade, "Actually, I am Lu Yang from the Seek Dao Sect, here to write for the newspaper. If you help me find out who is competing, I’ll put in a good word for you in the paper."

Ma Bin looked coldly at Lu Yang, straightened his lapel, and introduced himself formally,

"I’m Ma Bin, an Imperial Chef from the Imperial Kitchen of Great Xia, specializing in pastries and steam-cooking, as well as complex seasoning with various condiments. Born into the pastry Ma Family, I’ve been passionate about cooking since childhood, devouring all sorts of culinary texts. My culinary skills were initiated through the family’s traditional recipes."

"As time went by, I not only inherited the family’s pastry craft but also continuously learned new cooking techniques and trends, enhancing my culinary skills. After joining the Imperial Kitchen, I practiced my knife skills diligently until they were so mastered they seemed supernatural, earning the nickname ’The Knife of the Imperial Kitchen.’"

"I took leave to participate in this Spirit Chef contest with the goal of becoming a Golden Star Spirit Chef and to elevate my pastry Ma Family’s fame within the Spirit Chef World. Just a few good words about me would do; preferably, mention our pastry Ma Family."

Lu Yang silently wrote down a line: Ma Bin, from the pastry Ma Family.

Chapter 1095: 1094 [Immortal Chef’s Successor] Lu Yunyang

"Master Lu, please look at the cultivator standing in the corner with a smile that hides his eyes. His name is Xie Qi, and he is the hot favorite to win this culinary competition," Ma Bin, who handled the pastries for the Ma Family and cut vegetables for the Imperial Kitchen, introduced various spirit chefs to Lu Yang with great diligence.

Xie Qi had medicine pots tied around his waist and appeared to smile at everyone as if his eyes were never open. In the Spirit Chef World, Xie Qi’s reputation was widespread. Even among Five Star Spirit Chefs, there were differences in rank. Other Five Star Spirit Chefs would look at him with flickering gazes, a sign of reverence towards a stronger opponent.

But Lu Yang didn’t notice that other spirit chefs were also quietly keeping their distance from Ma Bin.

Ma Bin was also a top contender for the championship!

"Oh, is he that capable?"

"Xie Qi has an ability, whether it’s from his cultivation technique or a natural gift, that allows him to mimic the movements of others."

"Moreover, his tongue is also extraordinary, extremely sensitive to the flavors of food. Even the tiniest flaw is magnified in his mouth."

"Anyone who competes in a culinary duel with him will find that he completely imitates their movements, tastes the finished dish, and then improves upon it."

"In this way, the dishes he makes are always one step ahead of his opponents!"

Lu Yang nodded, acknowledging that this was indeed a formidable opponent.

"Anything else?"

"There’s also the female cultivator wearing an ice-blue dress; her name is An Tong. She has never been defeated either, but she relies on sheer strength. Although she was born without a sense of taste, her vision, touch, smell, and hearing are far superior to those of other cultivators at her level. She can create exquisite dishes using just her other four senses!"

Lu Yang had seen this woman among the people gathered by Jin Tan but didn’t know her name: "So her name is An Tong."

Ma Bin introduced these spirit chefs one by one, not only those with Five Star rankings. The rest of the four groups of spirit chefs were also there, after all, the Cultivation Monthly only wrote about Five Star Spirit Chef competitions.

As the sound of a gong echoed, the Spirit Chef competition officially began. Five groups of spirit chefs started their contests simultaneously. As each renowned spirit chef entered, they elicited exclamations of surprise from the audience.

"Look quick, that’s the ’Golden Blade Master’ Duan Lang!"

"That’s the ’Poisonous Granny’!"

"That’s the ’Blade of the Imperial Kitchen,’ Ma Bin!"

"That’s ’Immortal Tongue’ Xie Qi!"

"That’s the ’Invincible’ An Tong!"

Yun Mengmeng listened for a long time, getting frustrated at not hearing anyone call out the name of the Second-in-command, feeling that the audience truly lacked discernment. The Second-in-command was an Immortal Chef’s Successor, far more formidable than any Golden Blade Master or Poisonous Granny.

"That, that’s actually ’Immortal Chef’s Successor’ Lu Yunyang!" Yun Mengmeng, standing in the stands, suddenly pointed at Lu Yang, her expression one of disbelief.

However, her expression was somewhat exaggerated. Fortunately for her, the audience was captivated by Lu Yang’s title and didn’t pay much heed to Yun Mengmeng’s expression.

"Immortal Chef’s Successor, who dares to claim such a grand title?"

"Where, where?"

"What, you don’t know him?" Yun Mengmeng was very surprised.

"Who is this person?" The audience, comprised of gourmands, racked their brains but still could not think of any spirit chef worthy of the heaven-defying title ’Immortal Chef’s Successor.’

Yun Mengmeng whispered mysteriously, "Rumor has it that Lu Yunyang received cooking lessons from an immortal in his dreams and mastered techniques only known to immortals. His knife skills are unparalleled, his medicinal food beyond compare. He kept a low profile only because the immortal advised him to be patient for ten years, to temper his character."

"The ten years have passed, and his sharp sword can finally be unsheathed!"

"Really or not, why does this sound so fake?"

"This kid received the legacy of the Yingtian Immortal?"

Although Yun Mengmeng’s tale was elaborate, the gourmands weren’t fools; they didn’t just believe everything she said.

In the Spirit Chef World, only taste is everything!

Clearly, Yun Mengmeng wasn’t as naturally talented at lying as Lu Yang was.

The Spirit Chef competition consisted of three stages, testing knife skills, medicinal ingredients, and the dishes themselves. It was an elimination tournament, with only those passing the previous stage qualifying for the next.

The five competitions were held simultaneously, all testing the same content.

Song Tianjing’s forehead broke out in a layer of cold sweat. The first challenge was said to be a test of knife skills, but it wasn’t looking like it at all.

In the Two-Star Spirit Chef competition, all the Two-Star Spirit Chefs were confined to a Beast Fighting Arena. As cage after cage opened, countless Foundation Establishment Demon Beasts crawled out, baring their teeth and staring at these Spirit Chefs, seeing them as food.

"How come, you’re not telling me you can’t even beat a Demon Beast?" Master Wu watched Song Tianjing’s scared appearance, a cold smile curling the corner of his mouth. It seemed that Song Tianjing was nothing special after all, and there would be no need to target him to eliminate him.

Master Wu and Yu Qian’er, both Two-Star Spirit Chefs, had come to participate in this grand event.

Yu Qian’er explained to Song Tianjing, "The Spirit Chef Alliance believes that as a qualified Spirit Chef, the first thing you must be able to do is overcome your ingredients. If you can’t even defeat your ingredients, what qualifications do you have to process them?"

"So, the first challenge often tests how you overcome and handle a Demon Beast."

Yu Qian’er didn’t linger after speaking and charged toward a Spirit Rhinoceros armored in plate mail. The more difficult the Spirit Beast you chose to handle, the higher the score you would receive in the final evaluation after the three challenges. This Spirit Rhinoceros ranked within the top ten among a certain type of Demon Beast and was a worthy opponent to challenge.

Song Tianjing’s eyes twitched. He was very confident in his knife skills, but the problem was he had little combat experience, especially none against Demon Beasts.

What should I do? What should I do?

Song Tianjing inadvertently saw Yun Mengmeng in the audience stands, his heart jolted. Why were people from the Heavenly Court Sect here?

Could it be that the Young Sect Master had also come? Was he here to watch his performance?

If he performed too poorly, would the Young Sect Master have him eradicated?

The more Song Tianjing thought about it, the more likely it seemed. He decided to throw caution to the wind. Better to face a Demon Beast with courage than to be dispatched by the Young Sect Master. After all, he was at the Foundation Establishment Late Stage; he should be able to defeat at least one Demon Beast.

Song Tianjing chose a purple mink. The purple mink stood on its hind legs, its forepaws tucked into its fur, blinking its small eyes innocently.

Noticing Song Tianjing’s choice, the Purple Sky Mink immediately bared its teeth and its eyes turned into a fierce red. Its claws scraped the ground, leaving behind a trail of marks.

The audience stands immediately stirred, their attention focused on the Two-Star Spirit Chef group.

"Who is that person? He actually chose the Purple Sky Mink!?"

"Isn’t the Purple Sky Mink the strongest among these Demon Beasts?"

"Yes, it is the Beast King!"

The other Two-Star Spirit Chefs also noticed Song Tianjing’s choice, and sneers and taunts were endless.

"The Purple Sky Mink carries the bloodline of an Ancient Demon, its ancestors included a Crossing Tribulation Demon Emperor. It moves as fast as lightning, and even Spiritual Sense can’t catch it."

"More than that, its claws are exceptionally sharp, a mere scratch can sever a head. Did he choose it because he has a death wish?"

"Beast King Purple Sky Mink, even I wouldn’t dare choose it as an opponent," laughed Master Wu uncontrollably.

Song Tianjing quietly reached into his chest, caressing the "Culinary Bible" to boost his courage.

However, just as he touched the Complete Culinary Compendium, the Purple Sky Mink, which had been ready to attack, suddenly seemed to sense something, its red eyes dissipated, and it lay prostrate on the ground.

That was the ever-present fear ingrained in their ancient bloodlines, a grand will that Demon Beasts could not contend with!

"What’s wrong with the Purple Sky Mink?"

"Not right, not just the Purple Sky Mink, look at the other Demon Beasts!"

Exclamations of amazement were incessant from the audience stands, with some spectators so shocked that they stood up straight.

Every Demon Beast in the Beast Fighting Arena stretched their claws forward, prostrating themselves on the ground, bowing to Song Tianjing.

Yu Qian’er covered her mouth in shock, her beautiful eyes filled with boundless astonishment.

"Is it because of this ’Culinary Bible’?" Song Tianjing immediately thought of the key. The partial copy of his family’s ancient "Complete Culinary Compendium" had never instilled fear in Demon Beasts. Could it be that only this original "Culinary Bible" had such power?!

This should please Young Master Lu, right?

The commotion around Song Tianjing was too great for Lu Yang to ignore even if he wanted to.

No, how could a book casually given away by a Fairy have such an effect? Why have I never been treated this way?!

Chapter 1096: 1095: Knife Skills

"A bunch of Foundation Establishment Little Demons kneeling before you, what’s the point?" Immortal Fairy disdainfully said, finding Second-in-command truly lacking in ambition.

"Wait, wait for the day we find Qilin Immortal, then I shall let him kowtow to you, as you are the Eldest Senior Brother of Xiao Ling and Lianyi..."

"No no no, Fairy, what I meant was that Song Tianjing is less fortunate, how can he compare with me?"

"Is that what you meant?"

"Exactly!"

Lu Yang, in a bid to save his life, vigorously nodded.

"Then all right, it seems I misunderstood."

Lu Yang took a deep breath twice, discarded distracting thoughts, paid no attention to the activities on Song Tianjing’s side, and stopped thinking about his doom. What he needed to do now was to focus on displaying his knife skills.

Other stages he could just experience lightly, but the first stage required his full effort.

As they say, there’s no difference between knife and sword, often a Sword Cultivator’s use of a knife is not inferior either; he, with his Sword Spirit Root and renowned as the premier Sword Cultivator of his level, could not lose face in this challenge!

The Five Star Spirit Chef Team was also in the Beast Fighting Arena where Divine Transformation Stage Demon Beasts broke free from their cages, wantonly unleashing their oppressive might.

Merely the oppressive force was enough to scare away some Spirit Chefs, preventing them from acting and causing their hands holding the knives to tremble.

Other Five Star Spirit Chefs had their own unique equipment; Lu Yang was different, as a simple kitchen knife was enough for him.

The competition for the Five Star Spirit Chef Team was slightly different from that of the Two Star Spirit Chef Team, as there was an additional requirement in the topic.

"Vice Alliance Hierarch Liu Xing just said that in knife skills, ’benevolence’ must be reflected."

Lu Yang was still pondering what "Knife Skills" meant, while other Spirit Chefs had already formed their ideas and began slicing.

"Look quick, there’s a small sun!" The audience noticed something unusual about the Five Star Spirit Chef Team.

"No, no, that’s not a small sun, it’s Golden Blade Master, it’s his golden knife shaving so rapidly!"

Golden Blade Master took the lead, choosing a Fei Yi, which was a kind of snake from the Snake Clan, with six legs and four wings, ranking fifth among the Divine Transformation Demon Beasts!

Repeating his old trick, Golden Blade Master stomped his feet down, muscles bulging, suddenly throwing the Fei Yi into the sky.

"Bone Stripping Blade Technique!"

Two golden knives flew swiftly, resembling a great sun, leaving clean snake bones and falling white snake meat wherever the sunlight touched.

"The goodness in knife skills lies in not letting the Demon Beast feel pain, yielding matters," Golden Blade Master hung his golden knives in front of his chest and indifferently spoke, he landed first, followed by the white snake meat falling like rain into the plates.

In fact, Golden Blade Master also had the capability to defeat the first four ranked Demon Beasts; however, not being able to kill instantaneously, inducing pain in the Demon Beast, would not count as benevolent knife skills.

"Why is there such a dense mist over there?"
